### [1] [Subadditive bijections on the positive reals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35851)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明了存在次可加双射 f:(0,∞)→(0,∞)，其在 0 处的下极限为 0、上极限为 1，从而回答了 Matkowski 与 Swiatkowski 提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Matkowski 与 Swiatkowski 在 Proc. Amer. Math. Soc. 119 (1993) 中提出问题：是否存在次可加双射 f:(0,∞)→(0,∞)，使 liminf_{x→0}f(x)=0 而 limsup_{x→0}f(x)=1。该问题涉及次可加函数的结构性质与双射（满射兼单射）约束之间的相容性，具有理论意义。本文旨在给出肯定回答。

Method: 构造性方法：显式构造一个满足次可加性（即 f(x+y)≤f(x)+f(y)）的双射 f:(0,∞)→(0,∞)，并验证其在 0 附近的振荡行为，即下极限为 0、上极限为 1。

Result: 所构造的函数 f 同时满足：(1) 是 (0,∞) 到自身的双射；(2) 次可加；(3) liminf_{x→0}f(x)=0 且 limsup_{x→0}f(x)=1。

Conclusion: 该结果为 Matkowski 与 Swiatkowski 1993 年提出的问题提供了肯定答案，表明次可加双射在 0 处可以具有完全不同的上下极限值。

Abstract: We show that there exists a subadditive bijection $f:(0,\infty)\to(0,\infty)$ such that $\liminf_{x\to 0}f(x)=0$ and $\limsup_{x\to 0}f(x)=1$. This answers a question by Matkowski and Swiatkowski in [Proc. Amer. Math. Soc. 119 (1993), 187--197].

</details>


### [2] [A note on Rainwater's Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36306)
*David Preiss*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文将 Rainwater 定理从 w*-紧凸集推广到凸解析集，证明与 M 中每个测度都奇异的测度可集中于 M 全体测度皆零的 Baire 集，并由此推广 Glicksberg–König–Seever 分解定理，同时给出对符号测度集的适用条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rainwater 定理及 Glicksberg–König–Seever 分解定理原要求测度集 M 在 w* 拓扑下紧且凸。作者希望弱化这一紧性假设，考察凸解析性能否支撑相同结论，从而扩大定理适用范围。

Method: 在紧 Hausdorff 空间上考虑 Baire 测度，将概率测度集 M 的假设由 w*-紧凸减弱为凸解析，通过测度论与泛函分析中凸解析集的性质证明奇异性结论，并进一步分析符号测度集情形所需条件。

Result: 主要结果表明：即使 M 仅为凸解析集，关于 M 中所有测度都奇异的测度仍集中于某个对每个 μ∈M 均为 μ-零的 Baire 集；该结论以标准方式推广了 Glicksberg–König–Seever 分解定理，并给出了符号测度集上的成立条件。

Conclusion: 凸解析性是支撑 Rainwater 型奇异性集中结论的充分条件，比 w*-紧凸更弱，因而推广了经典分解定理；符号测度情形的条件进一步刻画了结论的适用边界。

Abstract: We extend Rainwater's theorem that, for Baire measures on a compact Hausdorff space,a measure singular with respect to any measure from a $w^*$-compact convex set $M$ of probability measures sits on a Baire set which is $μ$-null for every $μ\in M$. Our main result shows that this statement holds under the weaker assumption of convex analyticity of $M$. In the standard way, this extends the Glicksberg--König--Seever decomposition theorem. We also give conditions for our results to hold for sets of signed measures.

</details>


### [3] [Positive Cubature Compression and Optimal Hyperinterpolation Stability on a Conic Surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36430)
*Congpei An,Yan Ge*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 利用圆盘到圆锥的保测度 Z2 商映射，将截断圆锥曲面上的正求积与超插值问题精确转化为偶次圆盘多项式问题，实现节点压缩，并给出 L2 采样等距性与 L∞ 稳定性的最优阶估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究截断圆锥曲面（带 Jacobi 型权）上正求积与超插值的计算代价与稳定性，寻求通过降维/对称性压缩采样节点、降低计算成本，同时保持数值稳定性。

Method: 采用经典二次盘到锥映射，以无基形式构造保测度的 Z2 商映射，将圆锥迹空间与偶次圆盘多项式对应；把 2n 次圆锥正求积与中心对称的 4n+1 次圆盘正求积互相转换，从而迁移 Möller 下界及节点超额，并将再生核与超插值算子一并转移。

Result: 每个 2n 次圆锥正规则给出 Π_n(V) 上的精确 L2 采样等距，加权采样矩阵条件数恒为 1，与节点数量及几何无关；γ=0 径向情形下 Lebesgue 常数满足 c n ≤ Λ_n^V ≤ C n 的尖锐规则无关律，非负半整数 γ 时上界为 Λ_{n,γ}^V ≤ C_γ n^{γ+1}，节点压缩保持精确 L2 条件性且 L∞ 稳定性达最优通用阶。

Conclusion: 盘到锥的保测度商映射为圆锥求积与超插值提供了精确的理论桥梁，既能生成压缩的非乘积型圆锥规则、降低采样成本，又能同时保证 L2 精确条件性和 L∞ 最优稳定性；低阶近最小规则与高阶优化圆盘方案验证了成本降低的效果。

Abstract: We study the computational cost and stability of positive cubature and hyperinterpolation on a truncated conic surface with a Jacobi-type weight. A classical quadratic disk-to-cone map is used in a basis-free form as a measure-preserving $\mathbb Z_2$ quotient. It identifies the full degree-$m$ conic trace space with the even disk polynomials of degree at most $2m$ and converts positive degree-$2n$ cone cubature into centrally symmetric positive degree-$(4n+1)$ disk cubature, and conversely. This yields an exact transfer of Möller's lower bound and of the node excess above it, so that near-minimal disk formulas produce compressed non-product cone rules.
  The same quotient transfers reproducing kernels and hyperinterpolation operators. Every positive degree-$2n$ cone rule gives an exact $L^2$ sampling isometry on $Π_n(V)$; in particular, the weighted sampling matrix has condition number one, independently of the number and geometry of the nodes. For the unweighted radial case $γ=0$, if $Λ_n^V$ denotes the $C(V)\to C(V)$ Lebesgue constant of degree-$n$ hyperinterpolation, then every positive degree-$2n$ cone cubature rule satisfies the rule-independent sharp law $$c n \le Λ_n^V \le C n.$$ More generally, the upper bound $Λ_{n,γ}^V \le C_γn^{γ+1}$ holds when $γ$ is a nonnegative half-integer. Thus node compression preserves exact $L^2$ conditioning while the associated $L^\infty$ stability has the optimal universal order. Low-degree near-minimal rules and higher-degree optimized disk schemes illustrate the reduction in sampling cost.

</details>


### [4] [Assouad and Lower Dimensions of Graphs of Weierstrass-Type Functions with Rapidly Growing Frequencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37388)
*Jun Jason Luo*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究Weierstrass型函数图像的Assouad维数与下维数，给出了使图像Assouad维数为2、下维数为1的定量条件，并确定了Assouad谱，构造出下维数、Hausdorff维数、上盒维数与Assouad维数四者互异的函数图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weierstrass型函数的图像是经典的分形曲线，其Hausdorff维数与盒维数已有Barański的公式。然而关于更精细的Assouad维数、下维数以及Assouad谱的研究尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一空白，并探讨这些维数能否取到互不相同的值。

Method: 针对形如f(x)=Σ a_n φ(b_n x+θ_n)的Weierstrass型函数（φ为非平凡周期C^2函数，a_n,b_n>0，Σa_n<∞，b_{n+1}/b_n→∞），利用频率比b_{n+1}/b_n的渐近行为与系数衰减a_n=b_n^{-α}的关系，通过定量分析给出图像在每个非退化紧区间上Assouad维数与下维数的条件，并计算Assouad谱。

Result: 给出了图像Assouad维数为2或下维数为1的定量充分条件；当a_n=b_n^{-α}时，得到使Assouad谱在α≤ϑ<1上等于2的显式频率间隔条件；在对数频率比收敛的情形下，确定了α以下某非空区间上的谱；结合Barański关于Hausdorff与盒维数的公式，得到了下维数、Hausdorff维数、上盒维数与Assouad维数四个互不相同的图像。

Conclusion: Weierstrass型函数图像的Assouad维数与下维数可由频率比与系数衰减定量刻画，Assouad谱在特定频率间隔条件下具有显式表达；这些结果首次给出四个经典维数（下维数、Hausdorff维数、上盒维数、Assouad维数）两两互异的具体函数图像。

Abstract: We study the Assouad and lower dimensions of graphs of Weierstrass-type
  functions of the form
  \[
  f(x)=\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}a_nφ(b_nx+θ_n),
  \qquad x\in\mathbb R,
  \]
  where $φ$ is a nonconstant $C^2$ function of period one,
  $a_n,b_n>0$, $(θ_n)$ is an arbitrary sequence of real numbers,
  $\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}a_n<\infty$, and $b_{n+1}/b_n\to\infty$. We give quantitative conditions under which the graph over every
  nondegenerate compact interval has Assouad dimension two or lower
  dimension one. In particular, when $a_n=b_n^{-α}$, we obtain
  explicit frequency-gap conditions under which the Assouad spectrum
  of the graph is equal to $2$ for $α\leq\vartheta<1$. When the
  logarithmic frequency ratios converge, we also determine the spectrum
  on a nonempty interval below $α$. Together with Barański's formulas for
  the Hausdorff and box dimensions, these results yield graphs whose lower,
  Hausdorff, upper box, and Assouad dimensions are four distinct numbers.

</details>


### [5] [Compactness and Essential Norms of Weighted Bilinear Hardy Operators on General Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37998)
*Saikat Kanjilal*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 该文研究加权双线性Hardy算子 $\mathcal H_2$ 从 $L^{p_1}(v_1)\times L^{p_2}(v_2)$ 到 $L^q(u)$（含 $0<q<1$ 拟Banach情形）的紧性，证明紧性与有界性加端点尾拟范数消失等价，并给出本质距离、有限秩距离、非紧性Hausdorff测度与组合尾极限的精确等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权双线性Hardy算子的有界性已有较多刻画，但其紧性，尤其在非局部凸目标空间 $0<q<1$ 及端点尾条件、逼近数等定量刻画方面尚不完整，需要系统的紧性理论与显式判据。

Method: 在权重显式局部可积假设下，利用目标空间与其有界双线性映射空间的完整幂度量（$\vartheta=\min\{1,q\}$）；通过消失端点尾拟范数与组合尾极限刻画紧性，用分段常值中窗逼近构造双线性逼近数的定量界，并借助已发表的范数等价有界性特征作用于端点截断输出权重。

Result: 紧性等价于有界性加消失端点尾拟范数；有界时，方向组合尾极限的 $\vartheta$ 次幂恰等于目标度量下的本质距离、有限秩距离与非紧性Hausdorff测度；双线性逼近数由中窗逼近给出定量界并收敛到本质（拟）范数；在凸、混合、中度次临界、深度次临界等区域给出显式紧性判据；四个算例包括非对称混合范围阈值和 $L^{1/2}(0,1)$ 非局部凸例子，其中 $a_{m+1}^{(2)}(\mathcal H_2)=O(m^{-1/2})$。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了加权双线性Hardy算子紧性的完整理论框架，统一处理局部凸与非局部凸目标空间，给出精确的紧性等式、逼近数界与多区域显式判据，并通过算例验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Let $I=(a,b)$ be an open interval with finite or infinite endpoints. For $1<p_1,p_2<\infty$ and $0<q<\infty$, we study the weighted bilinear Hardy operator $ \mathcal H_2(f,g)(x)=(\int_a^x f)(\int_a^x g) $ from $L^{p_1}(v_1;I)\times L^{p_2}(v_2;I)$ to $L^q(u;I)$, including the quasi-Banach target range $0<q<1$. With $\vartheta=\min\{1,q\}$, the target and bounded-bilinear-map spaces are treated through their complete powered metrics. Under explicit local integrability assumptions on the weights, compactness is equivalent to boundedness and vanishing endpoint-tail quasi-norms. Whenever the full operator is bounded, the $\vartheta$-power of the directed combined-tail limit equals exactly the powered essential distance, finite-rank distance, and Hausdorff measure of noncompactness in the target metric. Equivalently, the unpowered quantities agree under the stated quasi-norm-radius normalisation. Piecewise-constant middle-window approximants give quantitative bounds for the bilinear approximation numbers. The finite-rank-distance identity then implies that these numbers converge to the essential (quasi-)norm. Applying published norm-equivalent boundedness characteristics to endpoint-truncated output weights yields explicit compactness criteria in the convex, mixed, moderately subcritical, and deeply subcritical regimes. Algebraic companion exponents are used to make the negative powers arising for $q<1$ explicit. Four worked examples include an asymmetric mixed-range threshold and a nonlocally convex $L^{1/2}(0,1)$ example for which $a_{m+1}^{(2)}(\mathcal H_2)=O(m^{-1/2})$.

</details>


### [6] [Flag multiresolutions: T1, paraproducts and commutators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38092)
*Ben Foster,Kangwei Li,Henri Martikainen*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文在旗型奇异积分背景下发展了一套二元概率多尺度分解理论，证明了双参数旗型奇异积分的完整T1定理乃至表示定理，并推广到三参数情形及加权估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数旗型奇异积分长期缺乏完整的T1定理，已有关于Zygmund伸缩的进展仅限于可消去T1=0或卷积情形，其纠缠结构导致难度显著，需要新的多尺度方法突破。

Method: 发展旗型奇异积分框架下的二元概率多尺度（dyadic-probabilistic multiresolution）方法，并据此构造旗型副乘积分解与表示定理；必要性的论证依赖于T1上的旗型乘积BMO假设。

Result: 证明了双参数旗型奇异积分的完整T1定理与表示定理，给出T1条件的非平凡必要性论证，并将结果推广至可消去三参数旗型情形；表示定理推出对任意旗Ap权的加权估计，并获得旗型副乘积分解与交换子估计的一般方法。

Conclusion: 该多尺度方法能良好保持原奇异积分的旗不变性，可适配一般多参数旗型情形，为加权估计、副乘积分解与交换子估计提供统一且高效的框架。

Abstract: We develop a rich theory of dyadic-probabilistic multiresolution methods in the context of flag type singular integrals. We apply these methods to prove a full $T1$ theorem, even a representation theorem, for bi-parameter flag singular integrals. This is the first time a complete $T1$ theorem appears in a multi-parameter setting with an entangled nature; even the related recent advances with Zygmund dilations have been in the cancellative $T1 = 0$ or convolution situations. This theorem comes with a non-trivial necessity argument related to the flag type product $\mathrm{BMO}$ assumption on $T1$. We also present a version of our theorem in the cancellative tri-parameter flag setting showing that our methods are perfectly adaptable to general multi-parameter flag settings. Importantly, our $T1$ representation theorem implies weighted estimates for every flag $A_p$ weight, showing that our dyadic multiresolutions carefully retain the flag invariance of the original singular integrals. Our multiresolution methods also yield flag style paraproduct decompositions of products of functions and lead to a general and efficient method for proving commutator estimates in various flag settings.

</details>

### [7] [A Note on the Classification of Centroaffine Hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36095)
*Zuzanna Szancer*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文改进了Cao-Wang关于局部强凸中心仿射超曲面的分类定理，去掉了退化情形下的额外假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cao和Wang此前对满足特定Tchebychev向量场条件的局部强凸中心仿射超曲面进行了分类，但在退化情形β=2α时，其高维论证额外假设差张量K的T⊥分量为零。本文旨在表明该额外假设并非必要，从而强化原定理。

Method: 针对退化情形β=2α，去除对差张量K的T⊥分量恒为零的额外假设，重新进行高维论证。

Result: 证明了在退化情形β=2α下，Cao-Wang所加的额外假设是不必要的。

Conclusion: 去掉不必要的额外假设后，Cao-Wang的定理得到加强，分类结果适用范围更广。

Abstract: Cao and Wang recently classified a class of locally strongly convex centroaffine hypersurfaces whose non-vanishing Tchebychev vector field $T$ satisfies $K(T,X)=αX$ and $X(α)=0$ for every $X\perp T$, together with $K(T,T)=βT$. In the degenerate case $β=2α$, their higher-dimensional argument assumes in addition that the $T^\perp$-component of the difference tensor $K$ vanishes identically. We show that this extra assumption is unnecessary and the theorem can be strengthened.

</details>


### [8] [Tachibana's Theorem Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36226)
*Xiaolong Li,Peter Petersen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文在曲率算子特征值的弱化部分和条件下，证明了若干Tachibana型刚性定理，给出了Einstein、Kähler-Einstein及四元数-Kähler流形局部对称性的阈值条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Tachibana刚性定理通常要求曲率算子完全非负（即所有特征值之和非负）。本文旨在弱化这一条件，仅对特征值的部分和施加约束，从而在更一般的设定下刻画流形的局部对称性。

Method: 对闭流形上的曲率算子施加部分和（partial-sum）非负性条件，利用Tachibana型刚性论证，分别针对Einstein流形、Kähler-Einstein流形的本原曲率算子以及四元数-Kähler流形的曲率算子进行分析。

Result: 对于n≥4的Einstein流形，若其曲率算子为(2(n-1))/3-非负，则局部对称；对于复维n≥2的Kähler-Einstein流形，其本原曲率算子的对应阈值为(2(n+1))/3；对于实维4n≥8的四元数-Kähler流形，其曲率算子的阈值为(2(n+2))/3。

Conclusion: 在弱化的部分和曲率算子非负条件下，Einstein、Kähler-Einstein及四元数-Kähler流形仍保持局部对称性，相应阈值分别为(2(n-1))/3、(2(n+1))/3和(2(n+2))/3，推广了经典Tachibana刚性结果。

Abstract: We prove Tachibana-type rigidity theorems for closed manifolds under weakened partial-sum conditions on the eigenvalues of curvature operators. Specifically, an Einstein manifold of dimension $n\geq4$ is locally symmetric if its curvature operator is $\frac{2(n-1)}{3}$-nonnegative. The corresponding thresholds are $\frac{2(n+1)}{3}$ for the primitive curvature operator of a Kähler-Einstein manifold of complex dimension $n\geq2$, and $\frac{2(n+2)}{3}$ for the curvature operator of a quaternionic-Kähler manifold of real dimension $4n\geq8$.

</details>


### [9] [Kähler--Ricci Shrinkers with Constant Scalar Curvature Are Rigid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36418)
*Wenqi Li,Jianyu Ou,Guoqiang Wu,Detang Zhou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明常数数量曲率的完备梯度Kähler–Ricci收缩孤子具有刚性，必为Kähler–Einstein因子与欧氏复空间的黎曼积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究梯度Kähler–Ricci收缩孤子在常数数量曲率条件下的刚性分类，刻画其几何结构。

Method: 设 $(M^{2m},g,f,J)$ 满足方程 $\operatorname{Ric}+\nabla^2 f=\frac12 g$。作为证明的一部分，建立黎曼刚性判据：对具常数数量曲率的完备非稳态梯度Ricci孤子，条件 $\mathcal{L}_{\nabla f}\operatorname{Ric}=0$ 蕴含径向平坦性，从而刚性。

Result: 若数量曲率为常数，则孤子刚性：存在整数 $k\in\{0,\ldots,m\}$ 使 $R\equiv k$，且 $(M^{2m},g,J)\cong (N^{2k}\times\mathbb C^{m-k}, g_N+g_{\mathrm{Euc}}, J_N\oplus J_0)$，其中 $(N^{2k},g_N,J_N)$ 为Kähler–Einstein且 $\operatorname{Ric}_{g_N}=\frac12 g_N$。

Conclusion: 常数数量曲率的完备梯度Kähler–Ricci收缩孤子可完全分类为Kähler–Einstein流形与平坦复空间的乘积，且标量曲率和等于整数。

Abstract: Let $(M^{2m},g,f,J)$ be a complete gradient Kähler--Ricci shrinker satisfying \[ \operatorname{Ric}+\nabla^2 f=\frac12 g. \] We prove that if the scalar curvature is constant, then the soliton is rigid. More precisely, there exists an integer $k\in\{0,\ldots,m\}$ such that \[ R\equiv k \] and \[ (M^{2m},g,J)\cong \bigl(N^{2k}\times\mathbb C^{m-k},

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张拉膜在均匀张力下呈极小曲面，其柔性不可伸长的边界沿常测地曲率的渐近线分布。斯图加特轻质结构研究所自1960年代起对此进行实验研究，近年网格壳也沿渐近线与测地线网络建造。受此建筑背景驱动，作者希望构造由有限多条常曲率渐近弧围成的嵌入极小曲面。

Abstract: Minimal surfaces bounded by cables or threads arise naturally in tensile architecture: a membrane under uniform tension takes the shape of a minimal surface, and its flexible, inextensible boundary lies along an asymptotic line of constant geodesic curvature. Motivated by this architectural context, we construct two new families of embedded minimal surfaces bounded by finitely many asymptotic arcs of constant curvature, using the Plateau-conjugate method applied to the solution of a partially-free boundary problem for minimal disks that meet the unit sphere orthogonally along the free boundary component.

</details>

Result: 1）在Berger空间SO(5)/SO(3)上构造出首个非齐次近并行G₂结构，其锥具有完整的Spin(7)和乐群；2）建立了S³×S³正弦锥有限商的局部去奇点：当奇异轨道坍缩时，去奇点族的远端收敛到正弦锥商，自然blow-up收敛到C₇的AC无挠G₂度量。

Abstract: Nearly $\mathrm{G}_2$ manifolds are positive Einstein seven-dimensional manifolds whose associated cone metric has special holonomy equal to, or contained in, $\mathrm{Spin}(7)$. We study nearly parallel $\mathrm{G}_2$-structures invariant under a cohomogeneity-one action of $\mathrm{SO}(4)$, and construct the first inhomogeneous nearly parallel $\mathrm{G}_2$-structure on the Berger space $\mathrm{SO}(5)/\mathrm{SO}(3)$. We show that as the singular orbit of a family of nearly parallel $\mathrm{G}_2$ manifolds collapses, away from the singular orbit the desingularising family converges to the sine cone quotient, while the natural blow-up converges to the AC torsion-free $\mathrm{G}_2$-metric of $C_7$.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Kobayashi 关于（强伪凸）Finsler 度量与曲率正性关系的问题，并对相关经典猜想（Fulton 问题、Hartshorne–Schneider 凸性猜想）给出反例。

Abstract: We construct slope-stable ample rank-two vector bundles on every Hirzebruch surface $\mathbb{F}_e$ that admit no smooth strongly pseudoconvex Finsler metric with semipositive Kobayashi curvature. The same bundles give counterexamples to the smooth strongly pseudoconvex Finsler formulation of Fulton's problem and to the Hartshorne--Schneider conjecture on the convexity of complements. ### [17] [On filtration formula and nodal sets in min-max theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37769)
*Talant Talipov*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文否定了Marques-Neves关于Laplace-Beltrami特征函数节点集扫出的渐近最优性猜想，并提出一种刚性较弱的节点扫出概念，证明了节点(p,N)-宽度在N→∞时收敛到Gromov体积谱的过滤公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2014年Marques-Neves猜想：由φ_0,…,φ_p的节点集生成的扫出在p→∞时对Gromov体积谱ω_p(M,g)是渐近最优的。本文旨在检验该猜想的正确性，并在其不成立时寻找能恢复体积谱的正确节点型变分框架。

Method: 在闭光滑Riemannian流形上，利用谱几何与min-max理论。先构造反例证明原猜想在任意维数、甚至正曲率实解析度量下均失败。随后引入较弱的节点扫出：在射影化特征空间P(E_N(g))的上同调类a_N的p次幂非零的有限复形映射族上定义节点(p,N)-宽度ν_p^N(M,g)，取节点集n维Hausdorff测度的上确界下确界。

Result: 证明Marques-Neves猜想在每一维数下都错误，包括正曲率实解析度量。同时证明过滤公式ω_p(M,g)=lim_{N→∞}ν_p^N(M,g)对所有p≥1成立。

Conclusion: 更宽松的节点扫出概念能够精确恢复Gromov体积谱；原猜想的刚性版本不成立。文中还提出若干关于节点几何与min-max理论的问题。

Abstract: Let $(M^{n+1},g)$ be a closed smooth Riemannian manifold, and let \[ 0=λ_0\leqλ_1\leq\ldots \] be the spectrum of the Laplace-Beltrami operator, with corresponding real eigenfunctions $\{φ_j\}_{j\geq0}$. Let $\{ω_p(M,g)\}_{p\geq1}$ denote Gromov's volume spectrum. In 2014, Marques-Neves conjectured that the sweepout generated by the nodal sets of $φ_0,\ldots,φ_p$ is asymptotically optimal for $ω_p(M,g)$ as $p\to\infty$. We show that this conjecture is false in every dimension, even for positively curved real-analytic metrics.
  Nevertheless, we show that a less rigid notion of nodal sweepouts recovers the volume spectrum. Let \[ E_N(M,g):=\operatorname{span}\{φ_0,\ldots,φ_N\}, \qquad a_N\in H^1\bigl(\mathbb P(E_N(g));\mathbb Z_2\bigr) \] be the generator, and for $p\geq1$ define \[ \mathcal N_p^N(g) := \left\{ Ψ\mid X\text{ is a finite complex},\ Ψ\in C\bigl(X,\mathbb P(E_N(g))\bigr),\ Ψ^*(a_N^p)\neq0 \right\}. \] We define the nodal $(p,N)$-width by \[ ν_p^N(M,g) := \inf_{Ψ\in\mathcal N_p^N(g)} \sup_{x\in\operatorname{dmn}(Ψ)} \mathcal H_g^{n}\bigl(\{Ψ_x=0\}\bigr). \] We prove the following filtration formula \[ ω_p(M,g) = \lim_{N\to\infty}ν_p^N(M,g) \] for every $p\geq1$. We state several questions on nodal geometry and min-max theory.

</details>


### [18] [Smoothing polyhedral spaces via Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37549)
*Richard H. Bamler,Esther Cabezas-Rivas*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明Petrunin光滑化猜想：无边、非负Alexandrov曲率的紧致欧氏多面体空间是几何非负曲率光滑黎曼轨道流形的Gromov-Hausdorff极限，且近似度量是单一轨道Ricci流的正时间切片。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Petrunin提出的光滑化猜想，即非负Alexandrov曲率的欧氏多面体空间能否用光滑非负曲率度量逼近，并将这一逼近纳入Ricci流的框架中。

We also show that the representation-dependent part of the He--Liu--Yau construction requires only a uniform bound on the infinitesimal action fields.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Let $χ$ be a Dirichlet character mod $D$ with $L(s,χ)$ its associated $L$-function, and let $ψ(x,q,a)$ be, as usual, Chebyshev's prime-counting function for the primes of the arithmetic progression $a$ (mod $q$) with $(a,q)=1$. Let $χ$ be a primitive character modulo $D$, and let $ν>0$ be small. We prove that if $L(s,χ)$ has a Siegel zero at $s=β=1-\frac{1}{η\log D}$ with $η>η_0(ν)$ for some large $η_0(ν)$, there exists a range of $x$ for which the asymptotic $$ψ(x,q,a)=\frac{ψ(x)}{φ(q)}\left[1+O(\varepsilon_{η_0})-χ\left(\frac{aD}{(q,D)}\right)\right]$$ holds for $q<x^{\frac{30}{59}-ν}$. We also show slightly better bounds for $q$ if we take an average over a range of $q$, finding an Elliott-Halberstam-type result for $q\sim Q$ on the range $Q<x^{\frac{16}{31}-ν}$. This improves on a 2003 result of Friedlander and Iwaniec that requires $q<x^{\frac{233}{462}}$ and builds on recent work of Sachpazis.

Abstract: We study a $p$-adic rigidity phenomenon suggested by rank two attractors. Near an ordinary special fiber of a Calabi--Yau family, the excellent Frobenius of Beukers and Vlasenko acts on the parameter disk. Achinger--Zdanowicz construct a canonical $W_2$-lift of the Frobenius twist, while Brantner--Taelman construct a canonical formal lift. We conjecture that the Dwork and Achinger--Zdanowicz first-order obstruction maps agree and that the fixed points of the excellent Frobenius are precisely the parameters of the Brantner--Taelman canonical formal lifts.

The law returns 7.1032053341370017 at (2,2,3), agreeing with Zeilberger-Zudilin to 17 digits, and 7.1018628323563507 at Bai's point (1857,1857,2785), agreeing with his published 7.101862832357; it reproduces Salikhov's 7.606308 from his own two-branch window.

This rests on three finite computations and is labelled verified, not proved; the resulting floor 7.101862832356 is stated as a conjecture.

</details>

We disprove it. For every a in F_p^*, put P_a = T^p - T - a, irreducible of degree p, and let i = p^n - 1 with 0 <= n <= p-1, a q-magic index. We prove an exact congruence showing that the L-polynomial reduces to (1-X)^n modulo P_a, whence deg_X g = n-1. For p >= 5 and 3 <= n <= p-1 this gives deg_X g >= 2, contradicting Goss's conjecture; moreover deg_X g = n-1 is unbounded, attaining p-2.

*Shisheng Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős提出两个问题：(1)可容许集能否对任意ε>0都具有密度1−ε；(2){1,…,N}是否总含大小为(1−o(1))N的可容许子集。允许重复的变体已被Erdős–Ruzsa–Sárközy及Granville–Soundararajan否定回答；对于互异元素的乘积情形，第一问最近才以密度界7/8否定回答，第二问仍悬而未决。本文旨在解决这两个遗留问题。

Method: 证明是初等的。第二个结果依赖于一个耦合（coupling）方法，用该方法替换由小素数组成的整数的最大除数，从而避免了沿乘法表计数商时固有的除数函数损失。

Result: 证明每个可容许集A⊆{1,…,N}满足∑_{a∈A}1/a≤½log N+(log log N+2)²；并证明存在绝对常数η>0，使得每个可容许集A⊆{1,…,N}对所有充分大的N都有|A|<(1−η)N。两个否定答案均在Lean 4中针对Formal Conjectures项目的陈述进行了形式化验证。

Conclusion: Erdős关于可容许集密度与大小的两个问题均得到否定回答，且证明为初等，其中第二个结果通过耦合方法克服了除数函数损失问题。

Abstract: Call a set $A$ of positive integers admissible if, whenever $a_1\cdots a_r=b_1\cdots b_s$ with $a_1,\dots,a_r$ distinct elements of $A$ and $b_1,\dots,b_s$ distinct elements of $A$, necessarily $r=s$. Erdős asked whether admissible sets can have density $1-\varepsilon$ for every $\varepsilon>0$, and whether $\{1,\dots,N\}$ always contains an admissible subset of size $(1-o(1))N$. For the variant in which repetitions are allowed both questions were answered negatively by Erdős, Ruzsa and Sárközy and by Granville and Soundararajan; for products of distinct elements, the first question was answered only recently (with density bound $7/8$), and the second has remained open. We show that every admissible $A\subseteq\{1,\dots,N\}$ satisfies $\sum_{a\in A}1/a\le\tfrac12\log N+(\log\log N+2)^2$, and that there is an absolute constant $η>0$ such that every admissible $A\subseteq\{1,\dots,N\}$ has $|A|<(1-η)N$ for all large $N$. Both questions therefore have negative answers. The proofs are elementary; the second rests on a coupling that replaces the largest divisor of an integer composed of small primes, which avoids the divisor-function losses inherent in counting quotients along a multiplication table. Both negative answers are formally verified in Lean 4 against the statements of the Formal Conjectures project.

</details>

Abstract: We study the distribution of integers with a large smooth part. Abstract: We prove an analog of the Ax-Schanuel theorem for the Drinfeld $j$-function in odd characteristic. *Senping Luo,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在四维协体积为单位一的格中，根格 $\mathcal D_4$ 在正参数下极小化 theta 函数，从而极小化 Epstein zeta 函数（$s>2$），解决了四维 Rankin-Sobolev 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sarnak-Strömbergsson（2006）猜想四维根格 $\mathcal D_4$ 极小化 theta 与 Epstein zeta 函数，并证明了其局部极小性；四维 Rankin-Sobolev 问题可追溯至 Éndibaev（1978）与 Shushbaev（1978），此前的全局最优性结果仅限于更低维情形。

Method: 在协体积归一化为 1 的全秩格 $L\subset\mathbb R^4$ 上，研究 theta 函数 $\Theta(\alpha,L)=\sum_{v\in L}e^{-\pi\alpha|v|^2}$（$\alpha>0$）与 Epstein zeta 函数 $E(L,s)=\sum_{v\in L\setminus\{0\}}|v|^{-2s}$（$s>2$）；将 $\mathcal D_4=2^{-1/4}D_4$ 归一化到协体积一，并证明在正交变换意义下 $\Theta$ 的极小元唯一为 $\mathcal D_4$。

Result: 对任意 $\alpha>0$，$\arg\min_{\mathrm{covol}(L)=1}\Theta(\alpha,L)=\mathcal D_4$（模正交变换）；由 theta 与 Epstein zeta 的 Mellin 变换关系，推出对任意 $s>2$，$\arg\min_{\mathrm{covol}(L)=1}E(L,s)=\mathcal D_4$。这给出 $\mathcal D_4$ 在四维所有格中的全局极小性。

Conclusion: 该结果完整证明了 Sarnak-Strömbergsson 猜想在四维的情形，将局部极小性提升为全局极小性，并解决了悬置约三十年的四维 Rankin-Sobolev 问题。

Abstract: Let the theta and Epstein zeta functions be $Θ(α,L)=\sum_{v\in L}e^{-πα|v|^2}$ for $α>0$ and $E(L,s)=\sum_{v\in L\setminus\{0\}}{|v|^{-2s}}$ for $s>2$, respectively. We consider full-rank lattices $L\subset\mathbb R^4$. Let the covolume of $L$ be one, and let $\mathcal D_4=2^{-1/4}D_4$ be the root lattice $D_4$ rescaled to covolume one. We prove that, up to orthogonal transformations, \begin{equation}\nonumber arg\,min_{covol(L)=1}Θ(α,L)=\mathcal D_4 \qquad\text{if}\quad α>0, \end{equation} and this implies that \begin{equation}\nonumber arg\,min_{covol(L)=1}E(L,s)=\mathcal D_4 \qquad\text{if}\quad s>2. \end{equation} This proves that the root lattice minimizes the theta and Epstein zeta functions among all lattices in dimension four, as conjectured by Sarnak-Strömbergsson (\cite{SS}, 2006), who established the local minimality of $\mathcal D_4$. Thereby, this resolves the Rankin-Sobolev problem in dimension four dating back to Éndibaev (\cite{End1978}, 1978) and Shushbaev (\cite{Shu1978}, 1978).

</details>

Abstract: The 290-Theorem of Bhargava-Hanke completely classifies all universal quadratic forms over the rational integers in terms of a finite criterion set: a positive definite quadratic form is universal if and only if it represents every integer in this finite set.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二项式系数子族最大公约数在满足 $p\equiv -1\pmod m$ 的素数处的 $p$-进赋值，补全此前只覆盖同余情形或单奇偶子情形的分类结果，获得完整的减一分类定理。

Method: 将 $N=\sum_i d_i p^i$ 的各位数字按奇偶位求和得 $A,B$；用 Kummer 定理把赋值问题转化为逐位借位计数，再进行极小带符号零和分类；同时在 Lean 4 / Mathlib 中对定理、加一同伴、缩放约化及 $m=3,4,6$ 特例作形式化验证。

Result: 在 $m\geq 3$、$m\mid N$、$m<N$ 假设下：例外混合情形 $(A,B)=(1,1)$ 且 $p=m-1$ 时 $v_p(G)=2$；混合情形 $(A,B)=(1,1)$ 且 $m<p$ 时 $v_p(G)=1$；单奇偶情形 $(A,B)=(m,0)$ 或 $(0,m)$ 时 $v_p(G)=1$；其余情形 $v_p(G)=0$。截至 2026-09-25 的文献核查未发现等价的完整减一分类定理。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了减一素数列的完整赋值分类，填补了 McTague 修正同余扩展未覆盖的混合奇偶分支与 $p=m-1$ 区域，并已通过交互式定理证明器形式化确认。

Abstract: For integers $m\geq 2$ and $m\mid N$, let $G(N;m)=\gcd\{\binom{N}{k}:0<k<N,\ m\mid k\}$. We prove a complete $p$-adic valuation formula for $G(N;m)$ at primes $p\equiv -1\pmod m$, under the hypotheses $m\geq 3$, $m\mid N$, and $m<N$. Writing $N=\sum_i d_i p^i$, put $A=\sum_{i\text{ even}}d_i$ and $B=\sum_{i\text{ odd}}d_i$. Then $v_p(G(N;m))$ is $2$ in the exceptional mixed case $(A,B)=(1,1)$ with $p=m-1$, is $1$ in the mixed case $(A,B)=(1,1)$ with $m<p$, is $1$ in the one-parity cases $(A,B)=(m,0)$ and $(A,B)=(0,m)$, and is $0$ otherwise. The proof uses Kummer's theorem to translate the problem into digitwise borrow counts and a minimal signed zero-sum classification. A source-level literature audit through 25 September 2026 located no equivalent prior theorem for the full minus-one classification: McTague's corrected same-residue extension covers the one-parity $p>m$ subcases, but not the mixed-parity branch or the $p=m-1$ regime. The theorem, its plus-one companion, a scaling reduction, and the $m=3,4,6$ specializations have been formalized in Lean 4 / Mathlib.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a conjecture of Smyth on the Mahler measure of non-reciprocal trinomials of height $1$, determining exactly when their Mahler measures are greater than or less than $M(x+y+1)=ρ=1.381356\ldots$, conjectured to be the smallest limit point of $M(P)$ for non-reciprocal polynomials. Conjecturally, our result gives the full spectrum of $M(P)<ρ$ for all non-reciprocal polynomials, not just for trinomials.

TL;DR: 本文研究无重复偶数部分限制下的EO分区函数$\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$，证明其模2的幂次同余、无穷同余族及递推关系，方法综合运用初等、算法与模形式技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在Andrews于2018年定义并研究分区函数$\mathcal{EO}(n)$（偶数部分均小于奇数部分的分区）之后，Uncu考察了其中无重复偶数部分的子集，记为$\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$。该子类的同余性质尚缺乏系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Conclusion: $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$具有丰富的模2幂次同余结构，包含无穷同余族并满足递推关系，多种技术结合是研究此类受限分区函数的有力途径。

Abstract: In 2018, Andrews defined and studied the partition function $\mathcal{EO}(n)$, which counts the number of partitions of $n$ where each even part is less than each odd part. Thereafter, Uncu considered a different subset of such partitions, namely those partitions counted by $\mathcal{EO}(n)$ where there are no repeated even parts (we denote the number of such partitions by $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$). In this paper, we prove several congruences modulo powers of $2$ for $\mathcal{EO}_u(n)$. We also prove some infinite families of congruences as well as a recurrence for the number of this class of partitions. Our methods involve elementary, algorithmic, and modular form techniques.

</details>


### [57] [Computing residual representations of abelian threefolds with imaginary multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37975)
*Shiva Chidambaram,Pip Goodman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究虚二次域上具有虚乘的极化阿贝尔三维簇的剩余Galois表示，给出判定其像"尽可能大"的判据与高效算法，并在数百万条曲线及Picard曲线数据上大规模验证，找到一个含13次同源的Picard曲线及若干有趣的Picard曲线有理族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿贝尔簇的剩余Galois表示像是数论与算术几何的核心问题，但其在虚乘情形下的显式刻画尚不充分，缺乏可支撑大规模计算的高效判据与算法，且实现在具体曲线族上验证并发现新现象的需要。

Result: 将算法应用于若干族的数百万条曲线（其Jacobian为具虚乘的阿贝尔三维簇）以及Sutherland的7-smooth Picard曲线数据集，得到一个在Q(ζ_3)上似有13次同源的Picard曲线显式例子；在计算范围内，其余所有自同态代数M的曲线其Jacobian的mod-ℓ像对任意素数ℓ>7均尽可能大，由此将酉半相似群ΓU_3(ℓ)实现为Q上的Galois群（对所有ℓ≢1,25,121 mod 168）。

Conclusion: 算法还发现若干有趣的Picard曲线有理族，其一般成员的代数维数为6且非CM域，对应Picard模曲面上的曲线，似为Shimura曲线；其中一些似乎参数化具有四元数乘的非主极化阿贝尔曲面。

Abstract: Let $M$ be an imaginary quadratic field. Let $A$ be a polarised abelian threefold defined over $M$ with geometric endomorphism algebra isomorphic to $M$. We study residual Galois representations attached to $A$. In particular, we describe the endomorphism field and the natural restrictions placed on the residual representations by their endomorphisms. We then devise criteria for the image of residual Galois representations to be large, and provide efficient algorithms suitable for large scale calculations.
  Subsequently, we apply this algorithm to millions of curves in several families, whose Jacobians are abelian threefolds with imaginary multiplication, and to Sutherland's dataset of $7$-smooth Picard curves. We produce an explicit example of a Picard curve which appears to have an isogeny of degree $13$ defined over $\mathbb{Q}(ζ_3)$. The Jacobians of all other curves, in the range of our computation with endomorphism algebra $M$, have mod-$\ell$ image as large as possible for any prime $\ell>7$. This allows us to realise the group $Γ\mathrm{U}_3(\ell)$ of unitary semisimilitudes as a Galois group over $\mathbb{Q}$ for all $\ell \not\equiv 1, 25, 121 \pmod{168}$.
  Our algorithms also led us to the discovery of several interesting rational families of Picard curves whose generic members appear to have endomorphism algebra of dimension $6$ which is not a CM field. These represent curves on the Picard modular surface and appear to be Shimura curves. Some of them appear to parametrise non-principally polarised abelian surfaces with quaternionic multiplication.

</details>

*Pranabesh Das,Kyle Pratt*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Given integers $k,\ell \geq 2$, define the Erdős--Selfridge curve \begin{align*} \mathcal{C}_{k,\ell} : y^\ell = x(x+1)\cdots (x+k-1). \end{align*} Bennett and Siksek proved that if $(x,y)$ is a rational point on $\mathcal{C}_{k,\ell}$ with $\ell$ a prime and $y \neq 0$, then $\ell$ is bounded (doubly-exponentially) in terms of $k$. We improve this bound on $\ell$ when $k$ is sufficiently large. Our method uses combinatorial and sieve-theoretic arguments, as well as results on solutions to generalized Fermat equations.

</details>

### [60] [Asymptotic Rigidity and Boundary Structure of Yang's Numerical Invariants for the Bidisk Submodules $[z^k-w^\ell]$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36516)
*Yin Liu,Yufeng Lu,Yixin Yang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究了希尔伯特模空间上子模 M_{k,ℓ} 的 Yang 数值不变量 Σ_j 的大指标渐近性及其生成函数的边界结构，证明了一阶 O(1/j) 渐近、二阶周期修正（周期为 lcm(k,ℓ)），并证明生成函数收敛半径为 1 且在 t=1 处具有对数奇点，从而否定了 Yang 的多项式性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画 Yang 数值不变量的渐近行为并回答 Yang 关于生成函数是否为多项式的多项式性问题，同时揭示准齐次族中边界结构的刚性。

Method: 基于前期工作给出的精确阶梯公式（staircase formula），对 Σ_j(M_{k,ℓ}) 作大 j 渐近展开；计算一阶积分常数 C_{k,ℓ}，分析二阶周期修正 Ψ_{k,ℓ}(j) 及其最小周期 lcm(k,ℓ)；并研究生成函数 P_{k,ℓ}(t) 的收敛半径与 t=1 处奇点，利用单位根多重对数边界模式与重正化边界极限恢复 Fourier 系数。

Result: 得到 Σ_j(M_{k,ℓ}) = C_{k,ℓ}/j + O(j^{-2})，其中 C_{k,ℓ} = ∫_0^∞ x²/((x+1/k)²(x+1/ℓ)²) dx；首次严格下降与 C_{k,ℓ} 共同恢复无序对 {k,ℓ}，构成渐近刚性；二阶项给出最小周期为 lcm(k,ℓ) 的周期修正，并确定严格下降的主振幅；生成函数收敛半径为 1、在 t=1 处有对数奇点，故对 k≠ℓ 永不为多项式；对数主系数与二阶边界支集可确定 {k,ℓ}，重正化边界极限恢复各有限阶周期渐近项的 Fourier 系数。

Conclusion: 该准齐次族中 Yang 的多项式性问题被否定；不变量的大指标渐近具有由 {k,ℓ} 决定的刚性结构，生成函数的单位根多重对数边界模式可系统恢复高阶渐近信息。

Abstract: Let \[ M_{k,\ell}=[z^k-w^\ell]\subset H^2(\mathbb D^2), \qquad k,\ell\in\mathbb N,\quad k\ne\ell . \] We study the large-index asymptotics of Yang's numerical invariants and the boundary structure of their generating function. Starting from the exact staircase formula obtained in our preceding work, we prove that \[ Σ_j(M_{k,\ell}) = \frac{C_{k,\ell}}{j} + O(j^{-2}), \] where \[ C_{k,\ell} = \int_0^\infty \frac{x^2} {(x+1/k)^2(x+1/\ell)^2}\,dx . \] The first strict descent together with $C_{k,\ell}$ recovers the unordered pair $\{k,\ell\}$, yielding an asymptotic rigidity principle. At the next order we obtain a periodic correction \[ Σ_j(M_{k,\ell}) = \frac{C_{k,\ell}}{j} + \frac{Ψ_{k,\ell}(j)}{j^2} + O(j^{-3}), \] whose least period is \(\operatorname{lcm}(k,\ell)\), and we determine the leading amplitudes of the strict drops.
  For Yang's generating function \[ \mathcal P_{k,\ell}(t) = \sum_{j=0}^{\infty}Σ_j(M_{k,\ell})t^j, \] we prove that it has radius of convergence one and a logarithmic singularity at $t=1$; hence it is never a polynomial for $k\ne\ell$, giving a negative answer to Yang's polynomiality question within this quasi-homogeneous family. The periodic higher-order corrections generate root-of-unity polylogarithmic boundary modes. The leading logarithmic coefficient together with the second-order boundary support determines the unordered pair $\{k,\ell\}$, while successive renormalized boundary limits recover the Fourier coefficients of every finite-order periodic asymptotic term.

</details>

Abstract: For every $α>1$ we construct a finite set $A\subset\mathbb{D}$ such that the zero-based invariant subspace $I_A$ fails the wandering subspace property. Consequently, the Beurling-type theorem holds on the weighted Bergman space $A^2_α$ if and only if $-1<α\le1$, confirming a conjecture of Shimorin. As a further application, we extend the prescribed-curvature construction of Hedenmalm and Perdomo to all $α>1$, thereby replacing the constant $α_0\approx1.04$ in their result by $1$.

Method: 在系数C*-代数配备有限忠实迹的条件下构造Banach Gelfand三元组；针对有限生成投射Hilbert C*-模，研究其外张量积与投射张量积的相容性，进而证明抽象核定理；最后将结论应用于Heisenberg模。

Result: 建立了Hilbert C*-模的Banach Gelfand三元组框架；证明了有限生成投射模的抽象核定理；对Heisenberg模得到时频分析中的新核定理。

Conclusion: Banach Gelfand三元组为Hilbert C*-模提供了自然的函数空间解释，并可作为导出核定理的统一工具，成功推广到时频分析中的Heisenberg模。

Abstract: Hilbert C*-modules whose coefficient C*-algebra is equipped with a finite faithful trace generate Banach Gelfand triples. This provides a natural setting for a function space interpretation of Hilbert C*-modules. In particular, when the modules are finitely generated and projective, their external tensor products interact well with projective tensor products, allowing us to derive abstract kernel theorems for Hilbert C*-modules. As an application, we apply our results to Heisenberg modules and obtain new kernel theorems for time-frequency analysis.

</details>


### [63] [The Karcher Mean on $2\times 2$ Triangular Matrices with Positive Diagonal Entries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37018)
*Huajun Huang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究了$\operatorname{UT}_n$（正对角上三角矩阵群）上的Karcher均值，给出$\operatorname{UT}_2$的闭式解析表达、投影均值迭代的尖锐收敛阈值与充分条件、log-Euclidean均值显式公式及Lie--Trotter型公式；并对$\operatorname{UT}_n$求出Karcher方程解的对角及第一上对角元，且从$\operatorname{UT}_2$迁移非收敛例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Karcher均值在正定矩阵等非正曲率空间上有良好理论，但在非交换、非半单的可解Lie群（如上三角矩阵群）上缺乏显式表达与迭代收敛性分析，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以$\operatorname{UT}_n$这一可解Lie群为对象，通过求解Karcher方程并结合Lie群上的指数映射与测地结构，推导$\operatorname{UT}_2$的Karcher均值闭式解；分析固定初始对角情形下投影均值迭代的收敛阈值，并给出任意初始点的充分收敛条件；同时推导log-Euclidean均值显式公式与Lie--Trotter型公式；对一般$\operatorname{UT}_n$提取Karcher方程解的对角与第一上对角分量，并从$\operatorname{UT}_2$构造非收敛示例。

Result: 得到$\operatorname{UT}_2$上Karcher均值的闭式解析表达式；建立固定初始对角下投影均值迭代的尖锐收敛阈值，并给出任意初始点的充分收敛条件；给出log-Euclidean均值的显式公式及Karcher均值的Lie--Trotter型公式；对$\operatorname{UT}_n$给出Karcher方程解的对角元与第一上对角元的表达，并给出非收敛例子。

Conclusion: 该工作为可解Lie群上均值理论提供了显式、可计算的基准结果，揭示了$\operatorname{UT}_2$上Karcher均值的结构及迭代法的精确收敛边界，并为一般$\operatorname{UT}_n$的分析提供了部分显式信息与非收敛性证据。

Abstract: Let $\operatorname{UT}_n$ denote the group of $n\times n$ real upper triangular matrices with positive diagonal entries. We derive a closed-form analytic expression for the Karcher mean on $\operatorname{UT}_2$ and obtain a sharp convergence threshold for the projection mean iteration with fixed initial diagonal, together with a sufficient convergence condition for arbitrary initial points. Abstract: For any Tychonoff space $X$, let $D(X)$ denote either the space $C(X)$ of all continuous real-valued functions on $X$ or the space $C^*(X)$ of all bounded continuous real-valued functions on $X$. We write $D_p(X)$ when $D(X)$ is endowed with the topology of pointwise convergence.

  A linear continuous surjection $T: E_{p}(X)\to E_{p}(Y)$ is said to be densely defined if $E(X)$ and $E(Y)$ are dense linear subspaces of $D_{p}(X)$ and $D_{p}(Y)$, respectively, and $E(X)$ is correct
  (see Definition 1.5(a)).

  Additionally, for arbitrary Tychonoff spaces $X$ and $Y$, assuming that $T: E_p(X)\to E_p(Y)$ is a densely defined linear continuous operator,
  we show that $X\in\mathcal P$ implies $Y\in\mathcal P$, where $\mathcal P$ is the property $(κ)$, the strong $σ$-scatteredness, or the property of being a $Δ_1$-space.

Conclusion: 在两个受限情形下证实了Chollet猜想的严格形式（常数<1）。但无限制猜想（含一般非紧的9阶秩4矩阵）不在结果范围内。

Abstract: Chollet's permanent conjecture asks whether per(A o B) <= per(A) per(B), where o denotes the entrywise (Hadamard) product, for complex Hermitian positive semidefinite matrices. We present computer-assisted proofs of two restricted forms with explicit constants strictly smaller than one. For every integer n >= 10 and every such matrix A of rank at most four, the self-conjugate ratio per(A o conjugate(A)) / per(A)^2 is bounded by 999991742359 / 10^12 when the denominator is nonzero. For order nine, we obtain the bound 999815240367 / 10^12 for correlation matrices whose four nonzero eigenvalues all equal 9/4. The first argument combines complex-sphere integrals, spectral subspace tilts, projection bounds, exact finite covers, and an analytic infinite tail. The second uses a quadratic relation among nine Gram vectors, a positive operator on the ten-dimensional space of quadratic forms, and rigorously bounded entropy. All decisive finite calculations use rational arithmetic and full closed-domain certificates. The unrestricted conjecture, including general non-tight order-nine rank-four matrices, is outside these results.

</details>

Abstract: We introduce two James-type constants associated with approximate isosceles and approximate Birkhoff--James orthogonality in real normed spaces. The isosceles family coincides with the classical James constant of Gao and Lau [J. Austral. Math. Soc. Ser. A 48 (1990), 101--112], while the Birkhoff family extends the orthogonal James constant of Baronti and Papini [Constr. Math. Anal. 5 (2022), no.~1, 37--45]. We show that approximate orthogonality constraints can, in certain settings, recover classical geometric constants exactly. In particular, the approximate isosceles family collapses to the classical James constant, while the approximate Birkhoff--James family forms a genuine parameter-dependent interpolation between the orthogonal and classical James constants.We also compare the Birkhoff profile with several classical geometric constants and study how it changes with the parameter.Several examples are also given to illustrate the relation between geometric constants and approximate orthogonality.

Abstract: We investigate the von Neumann--Jordan constant of Banach tensor products equipped with the injective and projective tensor norms. We first establish lower estimates in terms of the geometric properties of the factor spaces and show that, whenever both factors are infinite-dimensional, the von Neumann--Jordan constants of both canonical tensor products attain the maximal value \(2\). For finite-dimensional factors, we obtain explicit results for classical sequence spaces and characterize the extremal case through operator-space and orthogonal-contact conditions. We further derive dimension-sensitive estimates based on Euclidean sections. Finally, we study the constant under equivalent renormings and determine its exact renorming envelope for injective and projective tensor products. As consequences, we characterize when these tensor products are superreflexive and when they are weak Hilbert spaces. Abstract: We study Schatten embeddings through circular means of trace powers. The other parts of that problem are not claimed to be solved.Our results extend previous studies on Schatten embeddings, particularly those in \cite{CHPPR,

Abstract: For a bounded linear operator on an infinite-dimensional Hilbert space, we consider the class $β(H)$ of operators whose restrictions to all nonzero reducing subspaces attain their norms. Motivation: 研究正交系与Fourier矩阵在 $L_p$ 或行 $\ell_1$ 意义下的逼近（宽度、低秩逼近）极限，刻画最优误差随维数/秩的衰减规律，并给出最优指数。

Result: 得到宽度估计 $d_n \gtrsim \min\{1,n^{-1/p}N^{1/2}\}$，在 $n\asymp N$ 时为 $N^{-\alpha_p}$，$\alpha_p=1/p-1/2$ 为最优；好逼近需 $n\gtrsim N^{p/2}$。Fourier矩阵存在秩 $N^{1-a}$ 逼近使行 $\ell_1$ 误差至多 $N^{1/2+\eta}$，指数 $1/2$ 最优。

Conclusion: 结果推广至循环矩阵、三角函数的逼近（维数 $|\Lambda|^{1-a}$、最优误差 $|\Lambda|^{-\alpha_p+\eta}$），并给出加权Wiener类宽度的界。

Abstract: We prove that if $f_1,\ldots,f_N$ is an uniformly bounded orthonormal system, then for all $p\in[1,2)$ there is an estimate for its Kolmogorov widths: $d_n(\{f_1,\dots,f_N\},L_p) \gtrsim \min\{1,n^{-1/p}N^{1/2}\}$, $n\le N/2$. In the regime $n\asymp N$ this provides the lower bound $N^{-α_p}$ with a sharp exponent $α_p:=1/p-1/2$. Besides that, it follows that a good approximation of such ONS requires the dimension $n\gtrsim N^{p/2}$.
  Our second result is an approximation theorem for Fourier matrices of finite abelian groups (this includes the usual DFT matrices). For every $η>0$ there is $a=a(η)>0$ such that any Fourier matrix $F$ of sufficiently large order has an approximation of rank $N^{1-a}$ with the row-wise $\ell_1$-error at most $N^{1/2+η}$; the exponent $1/2$ is sharp. Consequences include the approximation of the same kind for circulant matrices; the approximation of the trigonometric functions $\exp(2πi\langle λ,x\rangle)$, $λ\in Λ=K\cap\mathbb{Z}^d$, $K$ is symmetric convex, with dimension $|Λ|^{1-a}$ and an optimal error $|Λ|^{-α_p+η}$; bounds for widths of weighted Wiener classes.

</details>

Abstract: For an $r$-uniform hypergraph $H$, let $ν(H)$ be the maximum number of edges no two of which share $r-1$ vertices, and $τ(H)$ the minimum number of $(r-1)$-sets such that every edge contains one of them. Aharoni and Zerbib conjectured that $τ(H)\le\lceil\frac{r+1}{2}\rceil\,ν(H)$, which for $r=3$ generalizes Tuza's conjecture on triangles. We prove that the fractional relaxation $τ^*(H)$ of $τ(H)$ satisfies $τ^*(H)\le\frac{r+1}{2}\,ν(H)$. This constant is best possible for every $r$, and for $r\ge4$ it improves on the previous bound of roughly $3r/4$.

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明每个 N≥9 个顶点的二部图表示数至多 ⌈N/4⌉，结合冠图的已知表示数，解决了"冠图在同阶二部图中最大化表示数"的猜想，并在 Lean 4 中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示数刻画图中顶点用字排列、使相邻顶点对应字母恰好交替的最少重复次数。已知冠图表示数较大，猜想其同阶二部图中最大化表示数，但缺乏一般二部图的统一上界证明。

Method: 发展 Mozhui 与 Krishna 的构造，将表示字的选取归结为邻域排序问题；刻画该排序的障碍，用概率估计对足够大的平衡二部划分排除障碍；剩余奇数部分用排序论证的改进直接处理；两个有限断言通过布尔不可满足性证书验证。

Result: 证明每个 N≥9 顶点的二部图表示数至多 ⌈N/4⌉；由此得到冠图极值性推论，即冠图在同阶二部图中最大化表示数。主要定理与推论（含有限证书论证）已在 Lean 4 中形式化并验证。

Conclusion: 建立了二部图表示数的紧上界，证实冠图极值猜想，并提供机器可验证的形式化证明。

Abstract: The representation number of a graph is the least positive integer $k$ for which its vertices can be arranged in a word, each occurring $k$ times, so that two distinct letters alternate precisely when the corresponding vertices are adjacent. We prove that every bipartite graph on $N\ge9$ vertices has representation number at most $\lceil N/4\rceil$. Together with the known representation number of crown graphs, this settles the conjecture that crowns maximise the representation number among bipartite graphs of the same order. The proof develops a construction of Mozhui and Krishna by reducing the choice of a representing word to an ordering problem for neighbourhoods. We characterise the obstructions to this ordering and use probability estimates to exclude them for all sufficiently large balanced bipartitions. Two finite assertions complete the argument, each established by a checked Boolean unsatisfiability certificate. A refinement of the ordering argument treats the remaining odd part sizes directly. The main theorem and the crown-extremality corollary, including the finite certificate arguments, have also been formalised and verified in Lean 4.

</details>

*Caroline Huang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过递归构造刻画了矩形网格图 $P_m \square P_n$ 中基数至少为 4 的所有极小分辨集，并给出基数 $4 \le k \le 2\min(m,n)-2$（偶数）的 $k$-极小集的闭式计数公式，结合基数 2、3 的已知结果，完成了对所有可能基数的极小分辨集的完整刻画与枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andersen 等人刻画了 3-极小集、确立了最大基数 $2\min(m,n)-2$ 并将网格极小分辨集的完整刻画与枚举列为开放问题；Adar 和 Epstein 证明了 3 是唯一可能的奇基数，且基数至少为 4 的极小分辨集可排序为 zigzag 序列。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，给出统一的分辨集生成与计数方法。

Method: 针对矩形网格图 $P_m \square P_n$（$m,n \ge 3$）及以出租车距离为度量的分辨集，构造了一个递归生成方案，精确生成所有基数至少为 4 的极小分辨集；并基于该构造推导闭式计数公式。

Result: 递归构造恰好生成所有基数至少为 4 的极小分辨集；由该构造得到闭式公式，可枚举所有偶数 $4 \le k \le 2\min(m,n)-2$ 的 $k$-极小集。

Conclusion: 结合基数 2、3 的已知刻画以及对 3-极小集的直接枚举，本文实现了矩形网格图在所有可能基数下极小分辨集的完整刻画与枚举，解决了此前提出的开放问题。

Abstract: Let $m,n \geq 3$. A set of vertices $S$ of the rectangular grid graph $P_m \square P_n$ is resolving if the taxicab distance vectors of the vertices of $P_m \square P_n$ with respect to $S$ are pairwise distinct. A resolving set $S$ is minimal if no proper subset of $S$ is resolving, and a minimal resolving set of cardinality $k$ is called a $k$-minimal. We extend the work of Andersen et al., who characterized $3$-minimals, established the maximum cardinality $2\min(m,n)-2$, and posed the complete characterization and enumeration of minimal resolving sets for grids as an open problem, and Adar and Epstein, who showed that $3$ is the only possible odd cardinality and that every minimal resolving set of cardinality at least $4$ can be ordered to form a sequence corresponding to a zigzag sequence. We provide a recursive construction that generates exactly the minimal resolving sets of cardinality at least $4$ for grids. We derive from the construction closed-form formulas to enumerate the $k$-minimals for all even $4 \leq k \leq 2\min(m,n) -2$. Together with the known characterizations for cardinalities $2$ and $3$ and a direct enumeration of the $3$-minimals, this yields a complete characterization and enumeration of the minimal resolving sets of rectangular grid graphs of every possible cardinality.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 隐子集猜想成立：对每个对偶相交族 F ⊆ 2^[n]，总存在 i ∈ [n] 满足 |F^L(i)| ≥ |F(i)|。

Conclusion: 本文解决了 Fishburn 的隐子集猜想，验证了其正确性，并表明加权星不等式是处理此类对偶相交族结构问题的有效工具。

Abstract: Fishburn's latent-subset conjecture, proposed in 1987 and revisited in 1988, asserts that for every dual intersecting family $\mathcal F\subseteq 2^{[n]}$, there exists $i\in[n]$ such that $|\mathcal F^L(i)|\geq|\mathcal F(i)|$. Here $\mathcal F^L$ is the family of the subsets of members of $\mathcal F$ that do not belong to $\mathcal F$. In this paper, we prove the conjecture using the recent weighted star inequality of Chang, Liu, and Liu.

</details>


### [77] [Sharp Thresholds for Distance Patterns in Random Subsets of $\mathbb Z^d$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36022)
*Christina Giannitsi,Eyvindur Ari Palsson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究二项随机子集$\Omega_N$的稠密子集$B$的平方距离集，证明在最优指数失效概率下，$B$在每个尺度上实现特定形式的平方距离，并得到$D^2(B)$的最大阶与仿射结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机点集的稠密子集在多大尺度范围内能实现哪些距离（尤其是平方距离），是加性组合与几何组合的交叉问题。经典结果多针对确定性格点，而随机稀疏背景下的最优尺度与最优概率界尚不清楚。本文旨在确定随机子集$Q_N$中稠密子集的平方距离实现尺度及其最优性。

Method: 令$d \geq 5$，$0 < \gamma < d-2$，$\Omega_N$为$Q_N$上保留概率$p_N=N^{-\gamma}$的二项随机子集。核心工具为：对$Q_N$的稠密子集建立有限多尺度膨胀（multidilate）超饱和定理；获得相关球面距离图在直至$p_N^{-2/(d-2)}$这一尖锐尺度上的有界性估计；进而应用Schacht的转移定理（transference theorem）将稠密结果转移到随机稀疏情形。

Result: 以最优指数阶的失效概率，每个固定正相对密度的子集$B \subseteq \Omega_N$在尺度$p_N^{-2/(d-2)} \lesssim \lambda \lesssim N^2$上均实现形如$q^2 \lambda$的平方距离，其中$q$属于仅依赖维数与密度的固定有限集。下界尺度$p_N^{-2/(d-2)}$是尖锐的。作为推论，$D^2(B)$达到最大阶$N^2$，且包含$\mathbb Z$的每个固定有限子集的仿射副本。

Conclusion: 该工作确定了随机格点中稠密子集平方距离可实现尺度的尖锐阈值，并给出$D^2(B)$的最大阶与仿射结构。主要贡献是新建立的有限多尺度膨胀超饱和定理，结合球面距离图的有界性估计与Schacht转移定理。

Abstract: Let $d \geq 5$, $0 < γ< d - 2$, and $Ω_N$ be the binomial random subset of $Q_N = [-N,N]^d \cap \mathbb Z^d$ with retention probability $p_N = N^{-γ}$. We prove that, with failure probability of optimal exponential order, every subset $B \subseteq Ω_N$ of fixed positive relative density realizes, at each scale $p_N^{-2/(d - 2)} \lesssim λ\lesssim N^2$, a squared distance of the form $q^2 λ$, where $q$ belongs to a fixed finite set depending only on the dimension and the density. The lower scale $p_N^{-2/(d - 2)}$ is sharp. As a consequence,the squared-distance set $D^2(B)$ of $B$ has maximal order $N^2$ and contains affine copies of every fixed finite subset of $\mathbb Z$.
  The main new input is a finite multidilate supersaturation theorem for dense subsets of $Q_N$, which, together with boundedness estimates for the associated spherical distance graphs down to the sharp scale, allows us to apply Schacht's transference theorem.

</details>


### [78] [Resultants, Recursive Formulas and Binet Formulas for $\textrm{SU}(N)$ Verlinde Sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36088)
*Jay Jorgenson,Anders Karlsson,Lejla Smajlović*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究关联于 SU(N) 的 Verlinde 和 V_n(N,m)（三角函数的有限和），证明了其生成多项式 D_N(w) 等于 Q(u)=u^m-1 与一个显式初等多项式 W_N 的迭代结式（相差一可计算因子），并给出 V_n 的线性递推关系（阶不超过二项式系数且实际阶等于 D_N 的不同根数）以及 Binet 型闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Verlinde 和 V_n(N,m) 是有限三角函数和，其结构性质（生成多项式、递推关系、闭式表达）此前缺乏系统刻画，本文旨在建立这些代数结构性质。

Method: 引入生成多项式 D_N(w)=∏(w-r_x)，通过迭代结式方法将 D_N 与 Q(u)=u^m-1 及一个不依赖 n、m 的显式初等多项式 W_N 关联；利用代数数（D_N 不同根的缩放倒数）刻画 V_n 的 n 次幂和的线性递推与闭式。

Result: (i) D_N(w) 等于 Q(u)=u^m-1 与 W_N 的迭代结式乘以显式可计算因子；(ii) 固定 N、m 时 V_n(N,m) 满足阶至多 C(m-1,N-1) 的线性递推，且实际递推长度为 D_N 的不同根数；(iii) 得到将 V_n(N,m) 表示为代数数 n 次幂有限和的 Binet 型闭式。对 N=2,3,4,5,6,8 给出显式例子。

Conclusion: Verlinde 和的生成多项式、线性递推与闭式表达均由其底层代数结构（D_N 的根）决定，方法为处理此类 SU(N) 三角函数和提供了结式与 Binet 型框架。

Abstract: We study Verlinde sums $V_{n}(N,m)$, which for our purposes are finite sums of trigonometric functions $\{r_{x}\}$, associated to $\textrm{ SU}(N)$. In this article we prove the following results: (i) the polynomial $D_N(w):=\prod_x(w-r_x)$ is equal to, up to an explicit and computable factor, the iterated resultant of $Q(u)=u^m-1$ with an explicit elementary polynomial $W_N$ which is independent of $n$ and $m$; (ii) for fixed $N$ and $m$, $V_n(N,m)$ satisfies a linear recursion of order at most $\binom{m-1}{N-1}$, together with arguments showing that one actually has a recursion of length equal to the number of distinct roots of $D_{N}$; (iii) for fixed $N$ and $m$, a Binet-type closed form expressing $V_n(N,m)$ as an explicit finite sum of $n$-th powers of algebraic numbers which are rescaled reciprocals of the distinct roots of $D_N(w)$ is derived. Several explicit examples are provided for $N=2,3,4,5,6$ and $8$.

</details>


### [79] [The $h$-expansion of the Plethysm $h_n[p_r]$ and Polysymmetric Expansions in $h^\otimes$ and $e^\otimes$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36181)
*Aditya Khanna*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了多重对称函数代数 PSym 中四类非纯张量基（H、E、E⁺、P，统称 plethystic 基）在纯张量基 {h⊗_τ} 与 {e⊗_τ} 下的展开，并用称为"polywrapping block tabloids"的组合对象给出解释；通过字上的统计量与 abacus 方法计算并证明了 plethysm h_n[p_r] 的 h-展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PSym 定义为若干份对称函数代数 Sym 的张量积（第 i 份变量被 i 缩放）。由 Sym 的基 {f_λ} 出发取所有纯张量可构造 PSym 的基，但 Asvin G 与 Andrew O'Desky 定义的四类基 H、E、E⁺、P 是非纯张量基，其在纯张量基下的展开尚不清楚，本文旨在刻画这些展开关系。

Method: 将 plethystic 基展开到纯张量基 {h⊗_τ} 和 {e⊗_τ}；对 H、E、E⁺，借助 plethysm h_n[p_r] 的 h-展开来实现；该展开通过字（words）上的统计量进行计算，并用 abacus（算盘）方法加以证明。引入 polywrapping block tabloids 作为组合解释工具。

Result: 给出了 H、E、E⁺ 在 h⊗ 与 e⊗ 下的展开，其系数来自 h_n[p_r] 的 h-展开；并用 polywrapping block tabloids 对这些展开系数给出组合解释；同时给出了 h_n[p_r] 的 h-展开的一个基于字统计量的公式，并借助 abacus 方法完成了证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立了 PSym 中 plethystic 基与非纯/纯张量基之间的显式展开关系，提供了组合描述与证明工具，深化了对 PSym 基结构及 plethysm 展开的理解。

Abstract: The algebra of polysymmetric functions (PSym) is defined as the tensor product of copies of the algebra of symmetric functions (Sym) where the $i$th copy has variables scaled by $i$. One way to construct a basis of PSym is to start with a basis $\{f_λ\}$ of Sym and consider all pure tensors arising from this basis. Asvin G and Andrew O'Desky described four families of non-pure tensor bases, namely $H, E, E^+$ and $P$, which we call plethystic bases. In this paper, we study the expansions of the plethystic bases into the pure-tensor bases $\{h^\otimes_τ\}$ and $\{e^\otimes_τ\}$, and interpret the results via combinatorial objects called polywrapping block tabloids. The expansions of $H, E$ and $E^+$ in $h^\otimes$ and $e^\otimes$ are found via the $h$-expansion of the plethysm $h_n[p_r]$. We compute this $h$-expansion using a statistic on words and prove it through abacus methods.

</details>


### [80] [Additive Quasi-isometry via rooted graph partitions and layering partition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36186)
*Dibyayan Chakraborty,Yann Vaxès*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 论文证明了在强等距路径复杂度有界且存在诚实、良根的R-有界H-划分的条件下，图G可(1, 33Rk²)-拟等距于树宽不超过tw(H)的图F，并由此推出K_{2,t}-渐近minor-free图可拟等距（加性失真）于K_{2,t}-minor-free图，回答了Albrechtsen等人的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图之间的拟等距关系，特别是刻画渐近minor-free图类能否通过加性失真的拟等距嵌入到相应的minor-free图类中，从而回答Albrechtsen、Distel与Georgakopoulos提出的公开问题。

Method: 结合基于图划分的方法与基于分层划分（layering partition）的方法（Chepoi等人2012），在源图G及目标图H的所有细分都具有有界强等距路径复杂度的条件下，利用诚实且"良根"的R-有界H-划分构造加性拟等距。

Conclusion: 将拟等距的乘性失真强化为加性失真，统一了图划分方法与分层划分方法，并解决了关于渐近minor-free图与minor-free图之间拟等距关系的公开问题。

Abstract: For a graph $H$, $\langle H \rangle$ denotes the class of all subdivisions of $H$ and $tw(H)$ denotes the treewidth of $H$. In this paper, we prove the following. For $k\geq 1, R\geq 1$, let $G,H$ be two graphs such that strong isometric path complexities (Chakraborty et al. [\textsc{Disc. Math., 2026}]) of both $G$ and $\langle H \rangle$ are at most $k$, and $G$ admits an honest, ``nicely rooted'' $R$-bounded $H$-partition. Then, there is a graph $F$ with $tw(F)\leq tw(H)$ such that $G$ admits a $(1,33\cdot R\cdot k^2)$-quasi-isometry to $F$.
  Using results of Albrechtsen, Distel, and Georgakopoulos (2025), we also obtain that $K_{2,t}$-asymptotic minor-free graphs admit quasi-isometries with additive distortion to $K_{2,t}$-minor-free graphs. This answers an open question raised by the above authors.
  As part of our proof, we combine the graph-partition based method and the layering partition based method (Chepoi et al. [\textsc{Discrete Comput. Geom.} 2012]) to obtain additive quasi-isometry when both the source and all subdivisions of the target graph have bounded strong isometric path complexity.

</details>


### [81] [Elementary Askey-Wilson Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37390)
*J. F. van Diejen,A. N. Kirillov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了非常良平衡 ${}_8\Phi_7$ 基本超几何 Askey-Wilson 函数的一个行列式求值公式，用初等函数表示，适用于四参数取离散值（$q^k$，$k$ 为正整数或半整数，符号与幂次四种类型各出现一次）的情形；证明依赖于一个 Cauchy 型行列式的乘积公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为非常良平衡 ${}_8\Phi_7$ Askey-Wilson 函数寻找用初等函数表达的显式求值公式，特别是在参数取特定离散值时，从而揭示该函数的闭式结构与行列式表示之间的联系。

Method: 建立了一个相关联的 Cauchy 型行列式的乘积公式，并借助 Krattenthaler 的“因子识别法”以初等方式予以证明，再由此推导 ${}_8\Phi_7$ 的行列式求值公式。

Result: 得到一个针对 ${}_8\Phi_7$ basic hypergeometric Askey-Wilson 函数的行列式求值公式，用初等函数表示；该公式对四个置换对称的 Askey-Wilson 参数的离散取值（由 $q^k$ 给出，$k$ 为正整数或半整数，正负号与整/半整数幂四种类型各出现一次）成立。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了 ${}_8\Phi_7$ Askey-Wilson 函数在特定离散参数下的显式初等表达式，并提供了一个具有独立价值的 Cauchy 型行列式乘积公式，其证明方法为初等的因子识别法。

Abstract: We present a determinantal evaluation formula for the very-well-poised ${}_8Φ_7$ basic hypergeometric Askey-Wilson function in terms of elementary functions. The formula in question is valid for discrete values of the four permutation-symmetric Askey-Wilson parameters; specifically, these consist of all quadruples of parameter values that are given, up to a sign, by powers $q^k$ with $k$ a positive integer or half-integer, in such a way that each of the four types (positive/negative sign, integer/half-integer power) occurs exactly once. The proof hinges on a product formula for an associated Cauchy-type determinant, which is of independent interest and is established here by elementary means using Krattenthaler's `identification of factors' method for determinant evaluations.

</details>


### [82] [Hadwiger--Nelson Problem for Typical Norms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36220)
*Noga Alon,Matija Bucić,James Davies*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究典型范数下单位距离图的染色数，将Alon-Bucić-Sauermann的指数上界改进为线性上界2d，并证明其紧性。关键工具是Lonely runner猜想的高维矩阵推广，同时彻底解决了1978年Schoenberg的视图障碍猜想及Henze-Malikiosis的覆盖半径猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Hadwiger-Nelson问题询问欧几里得平面单位距离图的染色数。该问题已被推广到各种赋范空间，其中高维欧几里得空间R^d最为自然且研究充分。Alon、Bucić和Sauermann证明了典型范数下R^d单位距离图的染色数至多为2^d。本文旨在改进这一指数上界。

Method: 利用Lonely runner猜想的高维矩阵推广作为关键工具，证明典型范数下R^d单位距离图的染色数上界。

Result: 证明典型范数下R^d单位距离图的染色数至多为2d，并证明该界是紧的，与欧几里得情形存在指数下界形成鲜明对比。同时彻底解决Schoenberg 1978年的视图障碍猜想和Henze-Malikiosis近期的覆盖半径猜想。

Conclusion: 典型范数下单位距离图的染色数上界从指数级改进为线性级2d且紧，揭示了与欧几里得情形的本质差异；高维矩阵推广的Lonely runner猜想成为解决多个长期猜想的有力工具。

Abstract: The classical Hadwiger-Nelson problem asks for the chromatic number of the unit distance graph of the Euclidean plane. Over the years, this problem has been considered for a variety of other normed spaces, with higher-dimensional Euclidean space $\mathbb{R}^d$ being perhaps the most natural and well-studied. Alon, Bucić, and Sauermann proved that, for a typical norm on $\mathbb{R}^d$, the chromatic number of the unit distance graph is at most $2^d$. We improve this exponential bound to a linear one by showing that the chromatic number of a typical norm on $\mathbb{R}^d$ is at most $2d$ and that this is tight. This shows a stark difference in the behavior compared to the Euclidean case, where there is an exponential lower bound.
  One of the key ingredients is a certain high-dimensional, matrix generalization of the Lonely runner conjecture, which also allows us to completely settle the so-called view-obstruction conjecture of Schoenberg from 1978 and a more recent covering-radius conjecture of Henze and Malikiosis.

</details>


### [83] [Dimensional colorful Helly theorems and topological variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36242)
*Wei Rao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了彩色Helly定理的维数加强形式，并给出Kim–Lew定理的维数加强、针对d-Leray复形的拟阵式扩展及拓扑Kim–Lew定理在两类参数范围内的加强，同时构造了反例说明无限制拓扑扩展不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典彩色Helly定理只给出交集非空的存在性结论，缺乏对交集维数的定量刻画；作者希望在维数层面加强彩色Helly定理与Kim–Lew定理，并将其推广到拓扑（单纯复形）框架，同时厘清拓扑扩展的适用边界。

Method: 对R^d中d+1个有限非空凸集族，证明各色类交集的维数之和非负；对颜色类并集的交集给出Kim–Lew型维数加强；对单纯复形引入link-Leray维数（面取link的Leray数，非面取-1），据此证明d-Leray复形上的拟阵式维数彩色Helly定理，并在两类参数范围内建立拓扑Kim–Lew定理的加强，特例可还原Kalai–Meshulam的拓扑彩色Helly定理；最后构造反例。

Result: 若对任意选择C_i∈C_i都有C_1∩…∩C_{d+1}≠∅，则Σ_{i=1}^{d+1} dim(∩C_i)≥0（约定dim∅=-1）；得到Kim–Lew定理的维数加强版本；借助link-Leray维数在d-Leray复形上证明拟阵式扩展；在两类参数范围内给出拓扑Kim–Lew定理的加强，并特例还原Kalai–Meshulam定理；构造出即使在更强局部条件下也不成立的无限制拓扑扩展反例。

Conclusion: 该工作将彩色Helly与Kim–Lew定理从存在性提升到维数定量层面，并借助link-Leray维数把结论推广至d-Leray复形，揭示了拓扑扩展成立的范围与极限。

Abstract: We prove a dimensional strengthening of the colorful Helly theorem. Let $\mathcal{C}_1,\dots,\mathcal{C}_{d+1}$ be finite nonempty families of convex sets in $\mathbb{R}^d$. If $C_1\cap\cdots\cap C_{d+1}\neq\varnothing$ for every choice of $C_i\in\mathcal{C}_i$, then $\sum_{i=1}^{d+1}\dim(\bigcap\mathcal{C}_i)\geq0$, where $\dim\varnothing=-1$. We also obtain a dimensional strengthening of a theorem of Kim and Lew, in which the intersections are taken over unions of color classes. For simplicial complexes, we introduce the link-Leray dimension, defined as the Leray number of the link for a face and as $-1$ for a nonface. Using this invariant, we prove a matroidal extension of the dimensional colorful Helly theorem for $d$-Leray complexes. We further establish a corresponding strengthening of the topological Kim--Lew theorem in two ranges of parameters. As a special case, we recover the topological colorful Helly theorem of Kalai and Meshulam. Finally, we construct counterexamples to the unrestricted topological extension, even under a stronger local condition.

</details>


### [84] [Binomial Complexity of Multidimensional Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36389)
*Mehdi Golafshan,Michel Rigo*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究二维数组（矩阵）的列二项式系数，引入(k,ℓ)-二项式等价与(k,ℓ)-二项式复杂度，给出Thue–Morse二维数组二项式复杂度的精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二项式系数在数组中用于计数特定配置的出现次数。为获得数组的结构性和组合性信息，需要将一维词的二项式等价与二项式复杂度概念推广到二维数组，并对经典无限数组（如Thue–Morse）的复杂度进行精确刻画。

Method: 1) 以二维有限数组的列二项式系数为对象，证明其可通过类比Magnus变换的途径计算；2) 定义有限数组的(k,ℓ)-二项式等价（大小不超过k×ℓ的子数组计数相同），并由此定义无限数组的(k,ℓ)-二项式复杂度函数；3) 对编码无限词直积的无限数组，建立(k,ℓ)-二项式复杂度的一般结果；4) 通过考察逐位补运算在Thue–Morse词因子的k-二项式等价类上的作用，推导二维Thue–Morse数组的复杂度公式。

Result: 证明了列二项式系数可由Magnus型变换计算；给出编码无限词直积的无限数组(k,ℓ)-二项式复杂度的一般结果；获得二维Thue–Morse数组(k,ℓ)-二项式复杂度的精确公式。

Conclusion: 该工作把二项式等价与二项式复杂度从词推广到二维数组，提供了新的计算工具（Magnus变换）与结构性结果，并以二维Thue–Morse数组的精确复杂度公式作为核心应用，揭示了逐位补运算在等价类层面上的作用规律。

Abstract: Binomial coefficients for multidimensional arrays count occurrences of particular configurations. For the sake of presentation, the emphasis is put on column-binomial coefficients for two-dimensional finite arrays. In this article, we first show that these coefficients can be computed through some Magnus transform.
  To get structural and combinatorial information on arrays, we then define \((k,\ell)\)-binomial equivalence for finite arrays and, from it, the \((k,\ell)\)-binomial complexity function of an infinite array. Roughly, two finite arrays are \((k,\ell)\)-binomially equivalent when they share the same number of subarrays of size at most $k\times \ell$.
  We obtain general results on the \((k,\ell)\)-binomial complexity of infinite arrays coding direct products of infinite words. Our main theorem gives an exact formula for the \((k,\ell)\)-binomial complexity of the two-dimensional Thue--Morse array. To that end, we closely examine the action of the bit-wise complement on the \(k\)-binomial equivalence classes of the factors of the Thue--Morse word.

</details>


### [85] [Sets of cardinality seven are not sum-dominant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36415)
*Thai Duong Do,Van Thien Nguyen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 给出七元实数集满足 |A+A|≤|A−A| 的自包含初等证明，无计算机枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答 Chu 关于七元情形的公开问题，并为已知结论提供初等、自包含的证明。

Method: 借鉴 Hegarty 的方法，使用表示计数与最大正差值，结合一个精确计数恒等式、两次 Cauchy–Schwarz 不等式，以及对"六元对称集加一点"结构的分析。

Result: 证明了任意基数七的集合 A⊂R 满足 |A+A|≤|A−A|。

Conclusion: 肯定了 Chu 在七元情形下的问题，并表明该结论可经纯初等组合论证获得，无需计算机枚举。

Abstract: We give a self-contained elementary proof of the known result that every set $A\subset\R$ of cardinality seven satisfies $|A+A|\le |A-A|$. The argument adapts Hegarty's method, using representation counts and the largest positive differences. An exact counting identity, two applications of the Cauchy--Schwarz inequality, and an analysis of a symmetric six-element set with one point added complete the proof, with no computer enumeration. This answers in the affirmative a question of Chu for the seven-element case.

</details>


### [86] [Most A$_α$-eigenvalues of a tree are small](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36427)
*Elvia P. Barturén,Elismar R. Oliveira*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了对于n个顶点的树，当0≤α≤1/2时，$A_α$-特征值小于等于平均度$d_α=α(2-2/n)$的个数至少为⌈n/2⌉，并对1/2<α≤1给出反例，同时将结果推广到Deformed Laplacian和矩阵$B_β$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展已有关于树特征值分布的研究（Jacobs2021、SIN2020），探究$A_α$-特征值低于平均度的数量下界，并考察α参数范围的影响。

Method: 通过谱图论方法分析树的$A_α$矩阵特征值分布，结合平均度$d_α=α(2-2/n)$给出计数下界，并构造反例验证参数区间，同时类比推广到Deformed Laplacian与矩阵$B_β$。

Result: 对0≤α≤1/2，树中不大于平均度的$A_α$-特征值至少有⌈n/2⌉个；对1/2<α≤1给出反例；并在Deformed Laplacian和矩阵$B_β$上得到相同结论。

Conclusion: 在0≤α≤1/2范围内建立了树特征值分布的一般下界，明确了α取值对结论成立性的分界，并将结果推广至相关矩阵族。

Abstract: We study the distribution of $A_α$-eigenvalues of a tree. We extend the works \cite{Jacobs2021} and \cite{SIN2020} by proving that the number of $A_α$-eigenvalues, for $0\leq α\leq \frac{1}{2}$, less than or equal to the average degree $d_α= α(2- 2/n)$ is, at least, $\lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil$ for a tree with $n$ vertices. Several counterexamples for $\frac{1}{2} < α\leq 1$ are exhibited. We also derive the same result for other well-known families such as the Deformed Laplacian and the matrix $B_β$.

</details>


### [87] [Degree conditions for $k$-strong orientations of digraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36551)
*Jørgen Bang-Jensen,Shuo Wei*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了有向图中存在生成k-强定向子有向图的尖锐度条件，包括半度阈值与Woodall型条件，并给出总度阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jackson与Thomassen猜想每个2k-强有向图包含生成k-强定向子有向图。本文旨在给出保证此类子有向图存在的尖锐度条件。

Method: 对有向图D，研究最小半度δ⁰(D)与缺失弧上的度条件d_D⁺(x)+d_D⁻(y)，证明在阈值下D存在k-强定向。

Result: 对固定正整数k与充分大的n，若δ⁰(D)≥⌊(n+k-1)/2⌋，则D存在k-强定向，且阈值最优；若每个缺失弧xy满足d_D⁺(x)+d_D⁻(y)≥n+2k-2，则D存在k-强定向，界最优；并确定尖锐最小总度阈值；半度结果对k≤αn（0<α<0.094882…）仍成立。

Conclusion: 结果给出了k-强定向存在的尖锐度阈值，推广并解决了Jackson-Thomassen型问题，同时给出Woodall型类比与总度阈值。

Abstract: Jackson and Thomassen conjectured that every $2k$-strong digraph contains a spanning $k$-strong oriented subdigraph. We prove sharp degree conditions for the existence of such a subdigraph. For every fixed positive integer $k$ and all sufficiently large $n$, every $n$-vertex digraph $D$ with $δ^0(D)\ge\lfloor(n+k-1)/2\rfloor$ admits a $k$-strong orientation. This threshold is best possible even for the weaker conclusion that $D$ itself is $k$-strong. We also prove a sharp Woodall-type analogue for every fixed positive integer $k$ and all sufficiently large $n$: if $d_D^+(x)+d_D^-(y)\ge n+2k-2$ for every missing arc $xy$, then $D$ admits a $k$-strong orientation, and this bound is again best possible. As a further consequence, we determine the sharp minimum total degree threshold. Finally, the semi-degree result also remains valid when $k\leαn$ for every fixed $0<α<0.094882\ldots$.

</details>


### [88] [Block-Transitive $5$-$(v,k,2)$ Designs with $k$ divides $v$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36613)
*Huijiao Hu,Zheng Huang,Shouqiang Shen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究满足Camina-Gagen条件（k整除v）的区传递简单5-(v,k,2)设计，利用有限2-齐次置换群分类分仿射型与几乎单型讨论，证明无仿射型区传递自同构群，几乎单型情形仅有PGL(2,11)作用于5-(12,6,2)与PGL(2,23)作用于5-(24,8,2)两个同构类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区传递5-设计的分类仍是公开难题。附加参数条件k整除v即Camina-Gagen条件，在该条件下对5-(v,k,2)设计进行刻画，有助于推进区传递5-设计的分类工作。

Method: 采用有限2-齐次置换群的分类定理，将区传递自同构群分为仿射型与几乎单型两类分别处理；结合设计的参数约束与群作用分析（如基柱、点稳定子结构）逐一排除或确定可行情形。

Result: 证明了不存在仿射型区传递自同构群的此类设计；几乎单型情形下，同构意义下恰有两种：具PGL(2,11)区传递自同构群的5-(12,6,2)设计，以及具PGL(2,23)区传递自同构群的5-(24,8,2)设计。

Conclusion: 在Camina-Gagen条件下，区传递简单5-(v,k,2)设计的可能情形被完全确定，仅剩两个与射影一般线性群相关的设计，为区传递5-设计的分类提供了新的完整结果。

Abstract: The classification of block-transitive 5-designs remains an open problem. The additional parameter condition that $k$ divides $v$ is called the Camina-Gagen condition. In this paper, we investigate block-transitive simple $5$-$(v,k,2)$ designs satisfying the Camina-Gagen condition. Using the classification of finite $2$-homogeneous permutation groups, we consider the affine and almost simple cases separately. We prove that no such design admits a block-transitive automorphism group of affine type. For the almost simple case, up to isomorphism, there are exactly two possibilities: a $5$-$(12,6,2)$ design admitting PGL(2,11) as a block-transitive automorphism group and a $5$-$(24,8,2)$ design admitting PGL(2,23) as a block-transitive automorphism group.

</details>


### [89] [On the nonnegativity of monomial immanants for hook partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36665)
*Xiangshuai Dong,Tingzeng Wu,Xing Gao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文解决了Stembridge提出的关于单项式immanant非负性的公开问题在hook分拆情形下的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stembridge于1992年提出公开问题：若矩阵A的所有阶数不超过r的子式非负，且分拆λ的长度不超过r，单调immanant φ^λ[A]是否非负？该问题在一般分拆下未解决，本文旨在推进特殊分拆类别的解答。

Method: 引入与Young置换特征对偶的类函数φ^λ，定义对应的单项式immanant φ^λ[A]=Σ_{σ∈S_n} φ^λ(σ)∏ a_{iσ(i)}，针对hook分拆（hook partitions）这一特殊分拆类型进行证明。

Result: 证明了Stembridge公开问题对所有hook分拆均成立，即在此类分拆下φ^λ[A]≥0。

Conclusion: 该结果部分解答了Stembridge的非负性公开问题，覆盖了所有hook分拆，为进一步研究一般分拆情形提供了基础。

Abstract: Let $A=(a_{ij})$ be an $n\times n$ real matrix and let $λ$ be a partition of $n$. Let $φ^λ$ be the class function dual to the Young permutation character, and let $$
  φ^λ[A]
  = \sum_{σ\in\mathfrak{S}_n}φ^λ(σ)
  \prod_{i=1}^{n}a_{iσ(i)} $$ be the corresponding monomial immanant. Stembridge [Canad. J. Math. 44 (1992), pp. 1079-1099] posed the following open problem: If all minors of $A$ of order at most $r$ are nonnegative, and the partition $λ$ has length at most $r$, is it true that $φ^λ[A]\ge 0$? This paper solves the problem for all hook partitions.

</details>


### [90] [Arithmetic Nonexistence Results for Tight Spherical $5$-Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36725)
*Zili Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了两条关于维度为 $(2m+1)^2-2$（$m$ 为偶数）的紧球形 $5$-设计的算术不存在性判据，重新覆盖并推广了 Bannai-Munemasa-Venkov 与 Nebe-Venkov 的适用判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧球形 $5$-设计的存在性问题长期受到关注，已有 Bannai-Munemasa-Venkov 与 Nebe-Venkov 给出部分参数的算术不存在性判据，但二者的适用范围存在未被完全覆盖的参数区间，需要新的判据来填补空白。

Method: 结合格矩恒等式（lattice moment identities）、有限 Gauss 和，以及模 $3$ 的行列式计算。

Result: 得到的两个判据可恢复 Bannai-Munemasa-Venkov 和 Nebe-Venkov 的可用判据，同时覆盖先前结果均未排除的参数；例如第一条判据覆盖 $m=16,100$，第二条判据覆盖 $m=96,168$。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了紧球形 $5$-设计不存在性的算术准则，统一并强化了既有结果，为更多参数的排除提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove two arithmetic nonexistence criteria for tight spherical $5$-designs in dimension $(2m+1)^2-2$ with $m$ even. Together, they recover the applicable criteria of Bannai-Munemasa-Venkov and Nebe-Venkov while covering parameters excluded by neither earlier result. Examples include $m=16,100$ under the first criterion and $m=96,168$ under the second. The proofs combine lattice moment identities, finite Gauss sums, and a determinant calculation modulo $3$.

</details>


### [91] [Two families of Hermite normal form simplices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36818)
*Feihu Liu,Jinlong Tang,Sihao Tao,Zihao Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究两类 Hermite 标准形单纯形的 Ehrhart 系数与整数分解性质：对 $T_{d,N}$ 证明 Ehrhart 系数全维单峰性并分类对数凹与实根情形；对 $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}$ 利用负连分数的同余条件刻画整数分解性质与正则幺模三角剖分，解决了 Bruckamp、Caicedo 与 Juhnke 提出的三个公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hermite 标准形单纯形是格多面体研究中的重要对象，其 Ehrhart 系数行为（单峰性、对数凹性、实根性）与整数分解性质、正则幺模三角剖分的存在性密切相关。Bruckamp、Caicedo 与 Juhnke 此前研究了两类此类单纯形并提出若干未决问题，本文旨在系统刻画这些性质并给出完整分类。

Method: 针对第一族 $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}=\mathrm{conv}(0,e_1,\ldots,e_{d-1},\boldsymbol{a})$ 与第二族 $T_{d,N}=\mathrm{conv}(0,e_1,\ldots,e_{d-2},(d-2,\ldots,d-2,d-1,0),(1,\ldots,1,N))$，结合 Ehrhart 理论、生成函数与组合手段分析其 Ehrhart 系数；对 $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}$ 引入负连分数，通过其同余条件刻画整数分解性质与正则幺模三角剖分的存在性，并将结论推广至任意向量 $\boldsymbol{a}$。

Result: 对 $T_{d,N}$ 证明了 Ehrhart 系数在任意维数下的单峰性，并对对数凹与实根情形给出完整分类；对形如 $\boldsymbol{a}=(N-q,\ldots,N-q,N)$ 的子族 $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}$，通过负连分数的同余条件同时刻画了整数分解性质与正则幺模三角剖分的存在性；进一步将整数分解性质与幺模三角剖分的分析推广到任意向量 $\boldsymbol{a}$ 的 $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}$。

Conclusion: 本文给出 Hermite 标准形单纯形 Ehrhart 系数与整数分解性质的系统结果，并据此解决了 Bruckamp、Caicedo 与 Juhnke 提出的三个公开问题，推进了对该类格多面体结构性质的理解。

Abstract: We investigate the Ehrhart coefficients and the integer decomposition property for two families of Hermite normal form simplices studied by Bruckamp, Caicedo, and Juhnke. The first family consists of simplices of the form $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}=\mathrm{conv}(0,e_1,\ldots,e_{d-1},\boldsymbol{a})$, where $\boldsymbol{a}=(a_1,\ldots,a_{d-1},N)$, while the second comprises the simplices $T_{d,N}=\mathrm{conv}\bigl(0,e_1,\ldots,e_{d-2},(d-2,\ldots,d-2,d-1,0),(1,\ldots,1,N)\bigr)$. For the family $T_{d,N}$, we establish the unimodality of the Ehrhart coefficients in arbitrary dimensions and completely classify the log-concave and real-rooted cases. For the sub-family $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}$ with $\boldsymbol{a}=(N-q,\ldots,N-q,N)$, we characterize both the integer decomposition property and the existence of a regular unimodular triangulation via a congruence condition on a negative continued fraction. More generally, we extend our analysis of the integer decomposition property and unimodular triangulations to $S_{\boldsymbol{a}}$ for arbitrary vectors $\boldsymbol{a}$. As a consequence, our results resolve three open problems posed by Bruckamp, Caicedo, and Juhnke.

</details>


### [92] [Weighted coloop splittings in rank six](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37778)
*James Dylan Douthitt,Lee Kennard,Josef Komissar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究正则拟阵的余回路分裂性质，用Seymour结构定理重新证明Nienhaus的秩四结论，并推广到秩五、六，进而将某Riemannian几何结果中的环面全纯假设从秩十放松到秩九。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 出于Riemannian几何的应用需求，需要研究正则拟阵的余回路（coloop）分裂性质，特别是去掉某余回路后产生两个余回路的结构条件，并放宽相关几何结果中的对称性秩假设。

Method: 使用Seymour关于正则拟阵的结构定理，替代Nienhaus原有的秩四分类证明，进而将结论推广到秩五和秩六的正则拟阵。

Result: 重新证明了Nienhaus的秩四结论，并将其推广到秩五和秩六；作为应用，将Mouillé、Nienhaus及第二作者近期结果中的环面全纯假设从秩十放松到秩九。

Conclusion: 正则拟阵的余回路分裂性质可在秩五、六成立，并据此改进Riemannian几何中相关定理的秩假设。

Abstract: With a view toward applications in Riemannian geometry, we explore coloop splitting properties of regular matroids. Nienhaus showed by classification in rank four that a regular matroid has a cocircuit whose deletion yields two coloops unless the matroid takes a particular form. In the latter case, one can split off any element of the ground set as a coloop. We reprove this using Seymour's structure theorem for regular matroids and prove an extension to matroids of ranks five and six. As an application to Riemannian geometry, we prove that the torus symmetry assumption in a recent result of Mouillé, Nienhaus, and the second author can be relaxed from rank ten to rank nine.

</details>


### [93] [A Characterization of Walk-Matrix Equivalence at Corank Two via Reciprocal WQH Switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36895)
*Chaochao Zhu,Qin Yue*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文完整刻画了行走矩阵秩为 n-2 时具有相同行走矩阵的图对，证明其等价于互反 WQH 切换，并确定最小阶数为 10，否认了 Liu–Siemons 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行走矩阵能否唯一决定图是谱图论中的基本问题。秩为 n 时矩阵可逆、图由行走矩阵唯一确定；秩为 n-1 时也难以出现非平凡重合。秩 n-2 是首个不同图可能共享同一行走矩阵的情形，因此需要刻画该退化层次的结构，并探究最小出现阶数及 Liu–Siemons 猜想。

Method: 设 G、H 为同一标号顶点集上的不同图，且 rank W_G = n-2。利用 W_G^T 的核空间与切换理论，分析 A_G - A_H 的分解形式，证明 W_G = W_H 当且仅当 H 由 G 经互反 Wang–Qiu–Hu (WQH) 切换得到，其中 A_G - A_H = uv^T + vu^T，u,v ∈ {0,±1}^n 支撑不交且构成 ker W_G^T 的基。随后通过穷举/构造确定最小阶数，并用单点并、单点联及图冠 (coronal) 构造推广到所有 n ≥ 10。

Result: 若 rank W_G = n-2 且 G ≠ H，则 W_G = W_H 当且仅当 H 由 G 经互反 WQH 切换得到，此时 A_G - A_H = uv^T + vu^T，u,v 支撑不交且为 ker W_G^T 的基。最小阶数方面：n ≤ 9 时不存在非同行走矩阵的图对；n = 10 时存在连通非同行对；且对每个 n ≥ 10，均可通过单点并、单点联与图冠构造连通非同行且行走矩阵相等的图对。

Conclusion: 该文给出了共秩二行走矩阵相等图对的完整结构定理，将其归结为互反 WQH 切换，并确定了最小阶数 10 及对所有 n ≥ 10 的构造，从而否认了 Liu 和 Siemons 的猜想。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a graph of order $n$ with adjacency matrix $A_G$, let $\mathbf e$ denote the all-one vector, and let $W_G=[\mathbf e,A_G\mathbf e,\ldots,A_G^{n-1}\mathbf e]$ be its walk matrix. We consider the case $\operatorname{rank}W_G=n-2$, the first corank for which distinct graphs can have the same walk matrix. We give a complete structural description of such pairs. More precisely, if $G$ and $H$ are distinct graphs on the same labelled vertex set and $\operatorname{rank}W_G=n-2$, then $W_G=W_H$ if and only if $H$ is obtained from $G$ by a reciprocal Wang--Qiu--Hu (WQH) switching. In this case, $A_G-A_H=uv^T+vu^T$, where $u,v\in\{0,\pm1\}^n$ have disjoint supports and form a basis of $\ker W_G^T$. We also determine the minimum order at which a non-isomorphic pair with equal corank-two walk matrices can occur. No such pair exists for $n\leq 9$, while a connected pair exists on $10$ vertices. Starting from this example, we use singleton union and join operations, together with the graph coronal, to construct connected non-isomorphic pairs with equal walk matrices of corank two for every $n\geq 10$. This, in particular, disproves a conjecture of Liu and Siemons.

</details>


### [94] [Part Size Count in Cylindric Partitions and Bijections for Small Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36910)
*Kağan Kurşungöz,Halime Ömrüuzun Seyrek*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究柱面分拆的部分大小统计量，给出粗略而一般公式的若干特例；建立普通分拆与小轮廓柱面分拆（带附加限制）之间的双射；并将带不同部分的柱面分拆生成函数公式及无限制柱面分拆生成函数泛函方程推广至涉及部分大小统计量的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柱面分拆是整数分拆理论中的重要对象，其生成函数与泛函方程已有较多结果，但对"部分大小"（part size）这一统计量的系统研究尚不充分；同时普通分拆与柱面分拆之间的组合双射在小轮廓情形下仍有待建立。

Method: 1) 研究柱面分拆中的部分大小统计量，给出一个粗略但一般性公式并列举其若干特殊化；2) 构造普通分拆与小轮廓柱面分拆（附加限制条件）之间的双射；3) 推广已有的带不同部分柱面分拆生成函数公式，以及无限制柱面分拆生成函数的泛函方程，使其包含部分大小统计量。

Result: 得到部分大小统计量一般公式的多个特殊化；建立了普通分拆与小轮廓受限柱面分拆的双射；成功将柱面分拆（含不同部分情形）的生成函数公式及无限制情形的泛函方程推广到包含部分大小统计量的形式。

Conclusion: 部分大小统计量可自然地融入柱面分拆的生成函数与泛函方程框架，且普通分拆与小轮廓柱面分拆存在结构性双射，表明两类分拆对象之间存在更深的组合联系。

Abstract: We first study the part size statistic in cylindric partitions, listing some specializations of a crude but general formula. Then, we establish bijections between ordinary partitions and cylindric partitions of small profiles with additional restrictions. Lastly, we extend earlier formulas for generating functions of cylindric partitions into distinct parts and functional equations of unrestricted cylindric partition generating functions, involving the part size statistic.

</details>


### [95] [An equivalent form of Hindman's Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37159)
*Lorenzo Carlucci,Andrea D'Amico*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Hindman有限和定理与Ramsey定理的一个自然推广之间的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ramsey定理与Hindman有限和定理均为组合数学中的重要结果，作者希望揭示两者之间的深层逻辑联系，说明Hindman定理可视为Ramsey定理的一种自然扩展形式。

Method: 采用反推/sharpening方法，通过逻辑推导证明二者的等价性（通常借助可数无限染色下的有限和结构，构造Ramsey型命题并互相推导）。

Result: 建立了Hindman有限和定理与扩展版Ramsey定理之间的等价性。

Conclusion: Hindman有限和定理与Ramsey定理的自然推广在逻辑上等价，说明二者属于同一层次的组合原理。

Abstract: We prove the equivalence of Hindman's finite sums theorem with a natural extension of Ramsey's theorem.

</details>


### [96] [A counterexample to the Fang--Lin conjecture: Edge and spectral extremality diverge near a Turán graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37208)
*Qi Wu,Yong Lu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否定地回答了 Fang 和 Lin 提出的问题：存在边色临界图 F，使得极大化边数与极大化谱半径的非 r-部 F-free 图集合不相交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fang 和 Lin 猜想：当 F 为边色临界且 χ(F)=r+1 时，任何非 r-部、F-free 且邻接谱半径最大的图也应当极大化边数。本文旨在检验该猜想是否成立。
