Method: 取 F=K_1∨μ(K_3)（μ(K_3) 为三角形的 Mycielskian），该图是边色临界的且 χ(F)=5。通过构造与分析，比较极大谱半径图集合 SPEX_5(n,F) 与极大边数图集合 EX_5(n,F)，证明二者在充分大 n 时不相交。

Result: 证明了对于充分大的 n，SPEX_5(n,F)∩EX_5(n,F)=∅，即不存在同时极大化边数和谱半径的图，从而给出该猜想的反例。

Conclusion: Fang-Lin 猜想不成立：边色临界性与谱 Turán 型极大图之间的边数极大化性质并无必然联系。

Abstract: Fang and Lin [J. Algebraic Combin. 63 (2026), Art.~58] asked whether, whenever $F$ is edge-color-critical with $χ(F)=r+1$, every non-$r$-partite, $F$-free graph of maximum adjacency spectral radius must also maximize the number of edges. We give a negative answer. Let $F=K_1\veeμ(K_3)$, where $μ(K_3)$ is the Mycielskian of a triangle. This graph is edge-color-critical with $χ(F)=5$. We prove that $\SPEX_{5}(n,F)\cap\EX_{5}(n,F)=\varnothing$ for all sufficiently large $n$. Thus no graph can simultaneously maximize both the edge count and the spectral radius.

</details>


### [97] [The small Davenport constant of $E_2\times C_3^r$ for $0\le r\le3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37262)
*Andreas Volkmann*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了对阶为3^5、指数为3的extraspecial群E₂，当0≤r≤3时d(E₂×C₃^r)=2r+10；通过带符号零块恒等式与四维辛空间有限命题给出上界，并附完整覆盖论证、证书文件与独立校验程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定extraspecial群与初等阿贝尔3群的直积的最小生成元数d，是群论与相关表示/上同调问题中的基本量；r≥4的一般公式尚未解决，因此需要先对小r建立精确结果并发展可验证的计算方法。

Method: 利用带符号零块恒等式(signed zero-block identities)构造上界；将问题化归为F₃上四维辛空间中的两个有限命题：(1)为任意索引列表中至多16个非零向量的所有可完备平衡三角形提供边权；(2)为临界列表中可能出现的方向族（无中心项）提供权。给出完整覆盖论证、精确证书文件与分别实现的检查程序；并用压缩论证避免在16项潜在定理中通过更短列表长度进行归纳。

Result: 证明d(E₂×C₃^r)=2r+10对0≤r≤3成立；上界由上述带符号零块恒等式及两个有限命题给出，且所有有限命题均通过证书与独立程序验证。

Conclusion: 建立了r≤3时的精确公式，并提供可机器验证的有限覆盖与压缩技术；r≥4的情形仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Let $E_2$ be the extraspecial group of order $3^5$ and exponent three. We prove that $d(E_2\times C_3^r)=2r+10$ for $0\le r\le3$. The upper bounds follow from signed zero-block identities and two finite statements in the four-dimensional symplectic space over $\mathbb F_3$. The first supplies edge weights for all completable balanced triangles on any indexed list of at most sixteen nonzero vectors. The second supplies weights for the direction families that can occur in a critical list with no central terms. We give complete coverage arguments, exact certificate files, and separately implemented checking programs. A compression argument removes any need for an induction through smaller list lengths in the sixteen-term potential theorem. The formula for $r\ge4$ remains open.

</details>


### [98] [Perfect Matching in $k$-Partite $k$-Uniform Hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37290)
*Jie Han,Hongliang Lu,Bin Wang,Feihong Yuan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文确定了所有固定k≥5的平衡k部k图中完美匹配的最小顶点度阈值（类规模充分大），完成Lo-Markström（k=3）与Lu-Wang-Yuan（k=4）之后的一般情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡k部k图中完美匹配的最小顶点度阈值此前仅对k=3（Lo-Markström, 2014）与k=4（Lu-Wang-Yuan）已知，缺少对所有固定k≥5的一般性精确结果。

Method: 证明分两种情形。闭（close）情形直接引用Lu-Wang-Yuan的一般定理；非闭情形将他们的稳定性结果从3部情形推广到任意部均匀度，并用Cao-Liu-Zhang的概率尾部刚性定理替代此前的加权引理。

Result: 对每个固定k≥5及所有充分大的类规模，给出了平衡k部k图中完美匹配顶点度阈值的精确值。

Conclusion: 该结果补齐了k≥5的空白，统一了平衡k部k图完美匹配的顶点度阈值理论。

Abstract: A balanced $k$-partite $k$-graph is a $k$-uniform hypergraph whose vertex set is partitioned into $k$ classes of the same size and whose edges meet every class in exactly one vertex. Lo and Markström (2014) determined the minimum vertex-degree threshold for perfect matchings when $k=3$, and Lu, Wang and Yuan recently determined it when $k=4$. We prove the corresponding exact result for every fixed $k\ge5$ and all sufficiently large class sizes. The close case follows from the general theorem of Lu, Wang and Yuan. For the non-closed case, we extend their stability result from the 3-partite setting to arbitrary partite uniformity, using the probability-tail rigidity theorem of Cao, Liu and Zhang, thereby replacing the earlier weighted lemma.

</details>


### [99] [Generalized Turán problems for shorter even cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37376)
*Zhen Liu,Chuanshu Wu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文解决了Gerbner等人遗留的开放问题：确定了ex(n,C_{2ℓ},C_{2k})在k>ℓ≥2时的精确渐近主项，给出公式ex(n,C_{2ℓ},C_{2k})=((k-1)_ℓ/(2ℓ)+o(1))n^ℓ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gerbner、Győri、Methuku和Vizer已证明ex(n,C_{2ℓ},C_{2k})=Θ(n^ℓ)（k>ℓ≥2），并确定了ℓ=2时的主项，但对k>ℓ≥3，其一般下界与上界的主常数不同，留下主项未定的开放问题。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 通过构造性下界与匹配的上界证明，确定精确渐近主项常数，其中(k-1)_ℓ=(k-1)(k-2)···(k-ℓ)为下降阶乘。

Result: 证明对任意k>ℓ≥2，ex(n,C_{2ℓ},C_{2k})=((k-1)_ℓ/(2ℓ)+o(1))n^ℓ。

Conclusion: 该结果完整确定了所有k>ℓ≥2情形下C_{2ℓ}在C_{2k}-free图中的极值计数主项，解决了前述开放问题。

Abstract: For graphs $H$ and $F$, let $\text{ex}(n,H,F)$ denote the maximum number of copies of $H$ in an $n$-vertex $F$-free graph. Gerbner, Győri, Methuku, and Vizer proved that $\text{ex}(n,C_{2\ell},C_{2k})=Θ(n^\ell)$ for $k>\ell\ge2$. They determined the leading term for $\ell=2$, but for $k>\ell\ge3$ their general lower and upper bounds had different leading constants, leaving open the problem of closing this gap. We solve this problem by showing that, for every $k>\ell\ge2$, \[\text{ex}(n,C_{2\ell},C_{2k})=\left(\frac{(k-1)_\ell}{2\ell}+o(1)\right)n^\ell, \] where $(k-1)_\ell=(k-1)(k-2)\cdots(k-\ell)$.

</details>


### [100] [A counterexample to Nagamochi's scoring lemma and a new rectangle packing bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37410)
*Hakan Karakuş*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文指出 Nagamochi (2005) 关于矩形内单位正方形填充的引理 1 评分断言有误，构造了局部的反例族，并给出较弱矩形界的独立证明，恢复了 s(k²−1)=k，并改进了 s(N) 的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nagamochi (2005) 的矩形填充界被用于推导 s(k²−1)=k 与 s(k²−2)=k 两类精确值，但其证明依赖 Lemma 1 的评分断言。作者怀疑该断言不成立，希望检验该证明的可靠性并尝试独立复现部分结论。

Method: 针对 Nagamochi Lemma 1 的评分断言，构造位于容器角落的局部反例族，说明已发表证明不完整；随后引入 strip measure（条带测度）作为独立工具，证明一个较弱的矩形界。

Result: 反例族显示 Nagamochi 矩形界的已发表证明不完整，但并未否定该界本身。借助条带测度的独立证明恢复了 s(k²−1)=k（k≥2），并给出对每个非平方整数 N≥8 严格优于面积界的 s(N) 显式下界。

Conclusion: Nagamochi 的原始证明存在漏洞，其完整矩形界与 s(k²−2)=k 仍未被本文证实或否证；本文仅独立确立了较弱的矩形界及 s(k²−1)=k。

Abstract: Let $s(N)$ denote the smallest side length of a square containing $N$ unit squares with arbitrary orientations and pairwise disjoint interiors. Nagamochi's Packing Unit Squares in a Rectangle (2005) states a rectangle packing bound from which he deduces two infinite families of exact values: $s(k^2-1) = k$ and $s(k^2-2) = k$ for every integer $k \geq 2$. We construct a family of counterexamples, local to a corner of the container, to the scoring assertion in Nagamochi's Lemma 1. These counterexamples show that the published proof of the rectangle bound is incomplete, but do not disprove the bound itself. We then give an independent proof of a weaker rectangle bound using a strip measure. This recovers $s(k^2-1) = k$ for every integer $k \geq 2$ and yields an explicit lower bound for $s(N)$ that improves strictly on the area bound for every nonsquare integer $N \geq 8$. Our argument does not establish Nagamochi's full rectangle bound or the identity $s(k^2-2) = k$.

</details>


### [101] [Real-rootedness and ultra log-concavity of rank-two matroid Ehrhart $h^*$-polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37439)
*Houshan Fu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 秩二拟阵的Ehrhart $h^*$-多项式的实根性被部分证明，并给出反例推翻Ferroni猜想；秩二或余秩二拟阵的$h^*$-系数是超对数凹的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究拟阵Ehrhart $h^*$-多项式的实根性（real-rootedness），检验Ferroni关于实根性的猜想，并探讨更弱的单峰性（unimodality）与对数凹性在低秩情形下是否成立。

Method: 针对恰有三个平行类（parallel classes）的秩二（rank-two）拟阵，证明当最小平行类大小不超过3时，其$h^*$-多项式实根；通过构造平行类大小为$(4,561,600)$及$(4,a,a+29)$（$a$充分大）的秩二拟阵作为反例；利用其对偶为theta图的圈拟阵（cycle matroids）这一性质，并研究$h^*$-系数的超对数凹性（ultra log-concavity）。

Result: 最小平行类≤3的三个平行类秩二拟阵的$h^*$-多项式实根，且该界是紧的：上述$(4,561,600)$与$(4,a,a+29)$实例的$h^*$-多项式非实根，从而否定Ferroni的实根性猜想。其对偶（theta图的圈拟阵）具有相同的$h^*$-多项式。此外，任意秩二或余秩二拟阵的$h^*$-系数序列为正且是阶数等于多项式次数的超对数凹序列。

Conclusion: 实根性在秩二拟阵中并非普遍成立，Ferroni猜想被反例推翻；但在秩二与余秩二情形下，较弱的单峰性猜想成立，且$h^*$-系数具有强对数凹性（超对数凹）。

Abstract: We prove that the Ehrhart $h^*$-polynomial of a rank-two matroid with exactly three parallel classes is real-rooted whenever its smallest parallel class has size at most three. This bound is sharp: the rank-two matroids with parallel-class sizes $(4,561,600)$ and $(4,a,a+29)$, for all sufficiently large integers $a$, have $h^*$-polynomials that are not real-rooted. These counterexamples disprove Ferroni's real-rootedness conjecture. Their duals are cycle matroids of theta graphs and have the same $h^*$-polynomials. Nevertheless, every matroid of rank two or corank two has a positive $h^*$-coefficient sequence that is ultra log-concave of order equal to the polynomial's degree. In particular, the unimodality conjecture holds in both cases.

</details>


### [102] [Lorentzian polynomials and log-concavity of the independence polynomials of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37553)
*Lily Li Liu,Hongyue Tang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造图 $\mathcal{F}(l,m,t,s)$、$\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)$ 及 $\mathcal{F}_n(l,m,t,s)$ 与边替换算子 $E_{\mathcal{G}_4}$，利用 Lorentzian 多项式理论证明相关图的独立多项式对数凹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图独立多项式的对数凹性，推进 Alavi、Malde、Schwenk 与 Erdős 的猜想，并推广 Bendjeddou 与 Hardiman 的结果。

Method: 构造两类新图 $\mathcal{F}(l,m,t,s)$、$\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)$，定义由 n 个 $\mathcal{F}$ 识别顶点 c 得到的图 $\mathcal{F}_n$，以及将任意简单有限无向图 G 的每条边替换为 $\mathcal{G}_4$ 的算子 $E_{\mathcal{G}_4}$；借助 Lorentzian 多项式理论证明独立多项式的对数凹性。

Result: 证明了 $\mathcal{F}_n(l,m,t,s)$ 及 $E_{\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)}$ 像图的独立多项式均是对数凹的。

Conclusion: 所得结果推进了 Alavi–Malde–Schwenk–Erdős 猜想，并推广了 Bendjeddou 与 Hardiman 的相关结论。

Abstract: In this paper, we first construct two graphs $\mathcal{F}(l,m,t,s)$ and $\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)$. Then we introduce the graph $\mathcal{F}_n(l,m,t,s)$ and the operator $E_{\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)}$, where $\mathcal{F}_n(l,m,t,s)$ is defined by identifying the vertex $c$ of $n$ copies of $\mathcal{F}(l,m,t,s)$, and $E_{\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)}$ is defined by replacing each edge of $G$ with $\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)$, for any simple finite undirected graph $G$. By using the theory of Lorentzian polynomials, we prove that the independence polynomials of the graphs $\mathcal{F}_n(l,m,t,s)$ and the image graphs of $E_{\mathcal{G}_4(l,m,t,s)}$ are log-concave, respectively. As applications, our results not only make progress on the conjecture of Alavi, Malde, Schwenk and Erdős, but also generalize the results of Bendjeddou and Hardiman.

</details>


### [103] [Extremal hypergraphs without generalized 4-cycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37744)
*Hao Huang,Jie Ma,Tianchi Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Füredi关于不相交边对并集互异的r-一致超图最大边数的猜想，并确定所有极值构型，同时解决了Mubayi的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1977年Erdős提出确定n个顶点r-一致超图中，所有不相交边对并集互异的最大边数f_r(n)的问题；Füredi随后猜想对固定r≥4且n充分大时f_r(n)=C(n-1,r-1)+⌊(n-1)/r⌋。

Method: 结合此类稠密超图的稳定性定理，并对关联的二部3-图应用精细的删点论证。

Result: 证明了Füredi猜想，确定了所有极值构型；稳定性定理还解决了Mubayi的一个猜想。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了Erdős-Füredi问题的极值结构，并推广解决了相关猜想。

Abstract: In 1977, Erdős posed the problem of determining the maximum number $f_r(n)$ of edges in an $n$-vertex $r$-uniform hypergraph in which all disjoint pairs of edges have distinct unions. Füredi later conjectured that, for every fixed $r\ge 4$ and all sufficiently large $n$, $f_r(n)=\binom{n-1}{r-1}+\lfloor \frac{n-1}{r}\rfloor$. In this paper, we prove this conjecture and determine all extremal configurations. Our proof combines a stability theorem for such dense hypergraphs with a delicate deletion argument applied to an associated bipartite $3$-graph. The stability theorem also resolves a conjecture of Mubayi.

</details>


### [104] [Forbidden subposet problems in the linear lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37748)
*Balázs Patkós,Casey Tompkins*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究线性格 $L_n(q)$ 中弱与强禁用子偏序集问题，证明 Bukh–Griggs–Lu 猜想的 $q$-类比对所有素数幂 $q$ 均不成立，并在多个情形下确定了 $La_q$ 与 $La_q^*$ 的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典布尔格中的禁用子偏序集（弱/强）问题推广到线性格 $L_n(q)$，检验 Bukh–Griggs–Lu 猜想及 diamond 猜想的 $q$-类比是否成立。

Method: 基于线性格 $L_n(q)$ 的层结构与 Sperner 型极值方法，引入 $q$-类比参数 $La_q(n,P)$、$La_q^*(n,P)$ 与 $\Sigma_q(n,d)$，分别处理 $d$ 与 $n$ 奇偶性相反、$D_s$（$2\le s\le q$、$s=q+1$）等族，并对 $n$ 为偶数时构造取自中间三层的最大强 $D_{q+1}$-free 族。

Result: 对每个素数幂 $q$，Bukh–Griggs–Lu 猜想的 $q$-类比均失败；若 $q\ge 3$ 且 $n,d$ 奇偶性相反，则 $La_q(n,L_d(q))=La_q^*(n,L_d(q))=\Sigma_q(n,d)$；对 $2\le s\le q$ 及所有 $n$（以及 $n$ 为奇数时 $s=q+1$），有 $La_q(n,D_s)=La_q^*(n,D_s)=\Sigma_q(n,2)$；$n$ 为偶数时最大强 $D_{q+1}$-free 族可取自中间三层；$s=2$ 的情形肯定地解决了 diamond 猜想的 $q$-类比。

Conclusion: 该工作否定了 Bukh–Griggs–Lu 猜想的 $q$-类比，同时给出若干禁用子偏序集问题的精确极值，并解决了 diamond 猜想的 $q$-类比。

Abstract: We study weak and strong forbidden subposet problems in the linear lattice $L_n(q)$. We show that the $q$-analogues of the Bukh--Griggs--Lu conjecture fail for every prime power $q$. If $q\ge 3$ and $n$ and $d$ have opposite parity, then $La_q(n,L_d(q))=La_q^*(n,L_d(q))=Σ_q(n,d)$. We also show that $La_q(n,D_s)=La_q^*(n,D_s)=Σ_q(n,2)$ for $2\le s\le q$ and all $n$, and for $s=q+1$ when $n$ is odd. For $n$ even, a maximum strong $D_{q+1}$-free family can be contained from the three middle layers. The case $s=2$ settles the $q$-analog of the diamond conjecture in the affirmative.

</details>


### [105] [Connected graphs with minimum adjacency spectral gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37774)
*Lele Liu,Michael Tait,Yi Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明了Stanić (2013) 关于最小邻接谱隙连通图的猜想：当顶点数 $n$ 足够大时，具有最小邻接谱隙的连通 $n$ 顶点图是双风筝图 $DK(r,s)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻接谱隙 $\lambda_1(G)-\lambda_2(G)$ 是图谱理论中的核心参数，刻画图的连通与混合性质。Stanić 于 2013 年猜想在所有连通 $n$ 顶点图中，谱隙最小者由双风筝图 $DK(r,s)$ 实现。该猜想长期未被完全解决，本文旨在对足够大的 $n$ 予以确认。

Method: 针对连通 $n$ 顶点图，围绕使谱隙 $\lambda_1(G)-\lambda_2(G)$ 最小化的极值问题展开分析；通过构造双风筝图 $DK(r,s)$ 并结合谱隙的扰动与界估计技术，对大 $n$ 情形进行极值论证以排除其他竞争图类，从而验证猜想。

Result: 对足够大的 $n$，证明了具有最小邻接谱隙的连通 $n$ 顶点图必为双风筝图 $DK(r,s)$，即 Stanić 猜想在渐近意义下成立。

Conclusion: 该工作确认了 Stanić (2013) 的谱隙极小化猜想（大 $n$ 情形），揭示了最小谱隙连通图的极值结构为双风筝图，为后续图谱极值问题与谱隙理论提供了新的依据。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a connected graph, and let $λ_1(G) > λ_2(G)$ denote its two largest adjacency eigenvalues. The spectral gap of $G$ is defined as the difference $λ_1(G) - λ_2(G)$. For integers $r\geq 2$ and $s\geq 0$, the double kite $DK(r,s)$ is formed by taking two vertex-disjoint copies of the complete graph $K_r$ and joining one specified vertex of each clique to a path with $s$ internal vertices. Stanić (2013) conjectured that every connected $n$-vertex graph with minimum adjacency spectral gap is a double kite. In this paper, we confirm this conjecture for sufficiently large $n$.

</details>


### [106] [Structural and computational aspects of majority coloring games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37796)
*Yash Chawda,Saraswati Girish Nanoti,Brahadeesh Sankarnarayanan,Eshwar Srinivasan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文研究（强）多数着色的Maker-Breaker型博弈。对多数着色博弈，证明2-毛毛虫图、所有叶在深度≤4的根树及任意图的细分满足μ_g(G)≤3，后者解决了2019年Bosek-Grytczuk-Jakóbczak提出的问题；对强多数着色博弈，精确计算了圈的Maj_g(C_n)，并证明强多数博弈色数判定问题是PSPACE完全的，强多数2-着色在欧拉图上NP完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数着色由Kreutzer等提出，要求每点同色邻居数不超过度数一半；Bosek-Grytczuk-Jakóbczak于2019年将其博弈化并提出两个公开问题：细分图是否满足μ_g≤3，以及是否所有树都满足μ_g≤3。此外强多数着色博弈尚未被系统研究，其圈的确切值及计算复杂度均为空白。本文旨在回答这些问题并刻画博弈多数色数的上界与计算难度。

Method: 定义（强）多数着色博弈：Alice与Bob交替着色，须始终维持（强）多数条件，Alice有必胜策略的最少颜色数记为μ_g(G)（强版Maj_g(G)）。对多数着色博弈，针对2-毛毛虫图、叶深≤4的根树、图的细分构造Alice的显式获胜策略；对树的普遍情形分析自然策略的障碍，并与marking博弈、relaxed着色博弈及局部有限无环图（δ>1）上的多数着色博弈作对比。对强多数博弈，通过组合分析精确确定圈的Maj_g(C_n)，并用归约证明强多数博弈色数判定问题PSPACE完全、强多数2-着色在欧拉图上NP完全。

Result: 多数着色博弈：证明μ_g(G)≤3当G为2-毛毛虫图、叶深k≤4的根树或某图的细分，从而肯定回答了细分图的公开问题；对树的一般情形展示自然策略失败的具体困难，并给出与marking博弈、relaxed着色博弈及δ>1局部有限无环图上多数着色博弈的比较。强多数着色博弈：对每个n≥3精确算出Maj_g(C_n)；证明强多数博弈色数判定问题PSPACE完全；证明强多数2-着色问题在欧拉图上NP完全。

Conclusion: 本文在博弈多数着色方面给出多类图3色的充分条件并解决细分图的公开问题，同时指出树的一般情形仍具本质困难；在强多数着色方面给出圈的确切博弈色数并首次确立其计算复杂度为PSPACE完全、欧拉图上2色判定NP完全，为后续研究提供了上界、反例与复杂度基准。

Abstract: A majority coloring of a graph $G = (V,E)$ is a coloring of $V(G)$ such that, for each vertex $v$, the number of neighbors of $v$ with the same color as $v$ is at most $deg(v)/2$. A strong majority coloring is a coloring of $V(G)$ such that, for each vertex $v$, every monochromatic subset of $N(v)$ has size at most $deg(v)/2$. The (strong) majority coloring game is a two-player Maker-Breaker-type game, in which two players Alice and Bob color the vertices of a graph $G$ alternately, maintaining the (strong) majority condition. The least number of colors such that Alice has a winning strategy in such a game is called the (strong) majority game chromatic number of the graph $G$, denoted $μ_g(G)$ (or $\mathrm{Maj}_g(G)$ for the strong version).
  For the majority coloring game, we prove that $μ_g(G) \le 3$ under the following cases: $G$ is a $2$-caterpillar, $G$ is a rooted tree with all leaves at depth $k \le 4$, and $G$ is a subdivision of some graph. The latter resolves a problem posed by Bosek--Grytczuk--Jakóbczak in 2019, who also asked whether $μ_g(T) \le 3$ for every tree $T$. For the latter question, we discuss various difficulties that arise when natural strategies are attempted by Alice to win the majority coloring game on trees. We include a comparison with the marking game and relaxed coloring game on trees, and with the majority coloring game on locally finite acyclic graphs $G$ with $δ(G) > 1$.
  For the strong majority coloring game, we compute $\mathrm{Maj}_g(C_n)$ exactly for each cycle $C_n$, $n \ge 3$. We also initiate the study of the computational complexity of the strong majority coloring game; specifically, we prove that the decision version of the Strong Majority Game Chromatic Number problem is PSPACE-complete. We also show that the Strong Majority 2-Coloring problem is NP-complete on Eulerian graphs.

</details>


### [107] [Near-uniform $q$-matroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37830)
*Giovanni Longobardi,Rocco Trombetti,Lei Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究非退化 F_{q^m}-线性 near-MRD 码所关联的 q-拟阵（称为 near-uniform q-拟阵），显式确定其秩函数并刻画其循环平坦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 秩度量码与 q-拟阵之间存在对应关系，而非退化 F_{q^m}-线性 near-MRD 码所对应的 q-拟阵结构尚缺乏系统的秩函数与循环平坦刻画。

Method: 利用秩度量码与其关联 q-系统之间的对应关系，显式确定 near-uniform q-拟阵的秩函数，刻画其循环平坦；并利用 near-MRD 码的秩分布进行计数分析。

Result: 证明 near-uniform q-拟阵是一类可表示 paving q-拟阵；给出其循环平坦数目的上界，并证明在某些参数下该界是紧的；当 n>m 时，利用 near-MRD 码的秩分布得到关联 q-拟阵非平凡循环平坦的精确计数。

Conclusion: near-uniform q-拟阵构成可表示 paving q-拟阵的重要类别，其循环平坦数目具有可刻画的上界（部分参数下紧），且在一定参数范围内可获得非平凡循环平坦的精确数量。

Abstract: We study the $q$-matroids associated with nondegenerate $\F_{q^m}$-linear near-MRD codes, which we call near-uniform $q$-matroids. Using the correspondence between rank-metric codes and their associated $q$-systems, we determine explicitly their rank functions and characterize their cyclic flats. We show that near-uniform $q$-matroids form a class of representable paving $q$-matroids. and prove an upper bound on the number of their cyclic flats, which is shown to be sharp for certain parameters. Finally, when $n>m$, exploiting the rank distribution of near-MRD codes, we obtain an exact count of the nontrivial cyclic flats of the associated $q$-matroids.

</details>


### [108] [The topological Bárány-Larman conjecture for prime numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37876)
*Pablo Soberón*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 Bárány-Larman 猜想（颜色类版本的相交 $(d+1)$-元组划分）的拓扑版本在 $r$ 为素数时成立，并证明 Blagojević–Matschke–Ziegler 的最优多彩 Tverberg 定理不能推广到素数幂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bárány-Larman 猜想断言 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中 $d+1$ 个颜色类（每类 $r$ 个点）的并集可划分为 $r$ 个凸包相交的彩虹 $(d+1)$-元组。已知的拓扑方法（尤其涉及等变拓扑与配置空间）能处理素数情形，作者希望把拓扑版本推广并检验素数假设的边界，同时厘清该结果与最优多彩 Tverberg 定理之间的关系。

Method: 使用等变代数拓扑工具：利用配置空间的 $\mathbb{Z}_r$（或相关对称群）等变上同调、欧拉类与 Fadell–Husseini 指标等不变量；在 $r$ 为素数时通过素数阶群作用的等变障碍分析构造映射，证明拓扑版本的相交条件成立。对素数幂情形，则利用相应的等变障碍非零性给出反例，说明最优多彩 Tverberg 定理无法推广。

Result: 证明了 Bárány-Larman 猜想的拓扑版本在 $r$ 为素数时成立；同时证明了 Blagojević–Matschke–Ziegler 的最优多彩 Tverberg 定理不能推广到素数幂情形。

Conclusion: 素数假设在相关相交划分问题中具有本质作用；拓扑版本的 Bárány-Larman 猜想在素数情形可解，而最优多彩 Tverberg 定理的适用范围不能扩展到素数幂。

Abstract: The Bárány-Larman conjecture states that for any $d+1$ sets of $r$ points each in $\mathbb{R}^d$, considered as color classes, we can partition their union into $r$ rainbow $(d+1)$-tuples whose convex hulls intersect. We prove that the topological version of this conjecture holds when $r$ is a prime number. We also show that the optimal colorful Tverberg theorem of Blagojević, Matschke, and Ziegler cannot be extended to prime powers.

</details>


### [109] [Improved bounds on completion of partial Latin squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37877)
*Jack Allsop,Candida Bowtell,Thomas Lesgourgues,Kalina Petrova*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了每个行、列及符号出现次数均不超过 0.231n 的部分拉丁方均可完成，显著改进了此前 0.08n 的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Daykin 与 Häggkvist 于 1983 年猜想：若部分拉丁方每行、每列至多含 n/4 个符号且每个符号至多使用 n/4 次，则可完成。此问题可视为 Nash-Williams 三角形分解猜想的分部类比，后者近期被 Delcourt 与 Postle 突破性证明。此前 Fu 与 Weng 仅得到 0.08n 的最佳已知界，亟需改进。

Method: 采用"放电"（discharging）策略，借鉴 Delcourt 与 Postle 的方法，并引入一种新方法来获得分部情形所需的"平衡性"（balancedness）性质。

Result: 证明每个部分拉丁方，若每行、每列至多含 0.231n 个符号且每个符号至多使用 0.231n 次，则可完成。将此前最佳已知界从 0.08n 提升至 0.231n。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了 Daykin-Häggkvist 猜想的研究，将完成阈值大幅提高，并为分部类比问题提供了结合放电法与平衡性构造的新思路。

Abstract: A Latin square of order $n$ is an $n \times n$ array filled with $n$ symbols so that each symbol appears exactly once in every row and column. A partial Latin square of order $n$ is an $n \times n$ array whose cells are either empty or filled in such a way that each symbol appears at most once in every row and column, and at most $n$ distinct symbols are used. In 1983, Daykin and Häggkvist conjectured that every partial Latin square in which each row and column contains at most $n/4$ symbols, and each symbol is used at most $n/4$ times, can be completed to a Latin square. We prove that every partial Latin square in which each row and column contains at most $0.231n$ symbols, and each symbol is used at most $0.231n$ times, can be completed to a Latin square, significantly improving the previous best-known bound of $0.08n$, obtained by Fu and Weng. This problem can be seen as a partite analogue of the Nash-Williams conjecture concerning triangle decompositions of dense graphs, recently proved in the breakthrough work of Delcourt and Postle. Our proof uses a `discharging' strategy, adapting the approach of Delcourt and Postle, combined with a novel method to achieve a `balancedness' property, required for the partite setting.

</details>


### [110] [Nonexistence of strongly regular graphs via multipoint spherical semidefinite bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37901)
*Zhen-Qi Liao,Wei-Hsuan Yu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用多点半定规划证明若干强正则图不存在，通过归一化特征空间投影构造球面二距离集，并用精确有理算术验证对偶可行解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明某些参数下强正则图不存在是代数图论中的经典难题；已有方法（如de Laat等、Kao-Yu的框架）需要改进以处理更大、更难的参数，本文旨在扩展多点半定规划方法以排斥更多候选图。

Method: 采用多点半定规划。对原始强正则图的归一化特征空间投影得到每个顶点对应一点的球面二距离集，若点数上界小于顶点数则排除该图。沿用de Laat等与Kao-Yu的表述，对每种参考类型使用以所选点可行内积标签为索引的系数矩阵；在零度时，将参考点索引的矩阵坐标合并为单一坐标。所得程序使用至多六个点的配置，矩阵阶数至多$2^m+1$（$m$为参考点数）。对偶可行解以精确有理算术验证。

Result: 以精确有理算术验证的对偶可行解排除了参数为$(351,140,73,44)$、$(550,162,75,36)$、$(703,182,81,35)$和$(1344,221,88,26)$的强正则图。

Conclusion: 多点半定规划结合坐标合并与精确有理验证，可有效排除若干此前未决的强正则图参数，扩展了该方法的适用范围。

Abstract: We use multipoint semidefinite programming to prove the nonexistence of strongly regular graphs. A normalized eigenspace projection of a primitive strongly regular graph gives a spherical two-distance set with one point for each vertex. A bound smaller than the required number of points therefore rules out the graph. Following the formulations of de Laat et al. and Kao and Yu, we use, for each reference type, coefficient matrices indexed by the feasible inner-product labels of the selected points. In degree zero, we combine the matrix coordinates indexed by the reference points into a single coordinate. The resulting programs use configurations of up to six points, with matrix orders at most $2^m+1$ for a reference set of $m$ points. Feasible dual solutions, verified in exact rational arithmetic, exclude strongly regular graphs with parameters $(351,140,73,44)$, $(550,162,75,36)$, $(703,182,81,35)$ and $(1344,221,88,26)$.

</details>


### [111] [Low discrepancy and spectral gap for directed hypergraphs via spectral regularity lemma for tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37912)
*Hiep Han*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了有向k-一致超图中低差异性与谱间隙等价；无向情形推广了Lenz–Mubayi定理并给出更短证明；核心工具是基于Friedman–Wigderson变分特征值的Frieze–Kannan型超图谱正则引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低差异性与谱间隙的等价关系在图（特别是无向图）中已有重要结果，Lenz–Mubayi证明了无向超图中的对应定理。本文旨在将此等价推广到有向k-一致超图，并给出更简洁的证明，同时提供一般性的张量分解工具。

Method: 核心是建立Frieze–Kannan型超图谱正则引理：基于Friedman–Wigderson的变分特征值定义，将任意张量分解为有界数量的秩一张量之和加上一个具有小最大特征值的准随机张量。该引理对复值、未必对称的张量均成立。作为推论，结合该分解论证有向k-一致超图的低差异性等价于谱间隙；此外还讨论了Szemerédi型变体以及有限阿贝尔群上Cayley型超图的正则化。

Result: 证明了有向k-一致超图中低差异性与谱间隙的等价性；无向情形复现Lenz–Mubayi定理且证明显著更短；所建立的张量正则引理对复值及非对称张量成立，可分解为有界秩一张量与尾部控制良好的准随机张量；并给出Szemerédi型变体及有限阿贝尔群上Cayley型超图正则化的讨论。

Conclusion: 该工作统一并推广了超图低差异性与谱间隙的等价理论，核心贡献是Frieze–Kannan型超图谱正则引理，其对复值非对称张量的成立使其具有独立价值，并为超图正则化及Cayley型结构的相关研究提供新工具。

Abstract: We show that low discrepancy is equivalent to spectral gap for directed $k$-uniform hypergraphs. For undirected hypergraphs this recovers a theorem of Lenz and Mubayi, with a considerably shorter proof. At the heart of our argument is a Frieze--Kannan type spectral regularity lemma for hypergraphs based on the variational notion of hypergraph eigenvalues by Friedman--Wigderson, which decomposes any tensor into a bounded number of rank-one tensors plus a quasi-random tensor with small top eigenvalue. This lemma may be of independent interest, and we prove it for complex-valued, not necessarily symmetric tensors.
  We also briefly discuss a Szemerédi-type variant and the regularization of Cayley-type hypergraphs over finite abelian groups.

</details>


### [112] [Tight bounds for positive discrepancy via eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37961)
*Oliver Janzer,István Tomon,Fredy Yip*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 论文建立一个关于对称矩阵上的二次型优化与主子矩阵最大特征值的逆命题引理，并用它改进MaxCut、bisection width和discrepancy的若干结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\max_{v\in[-1,1]^n}v^TMv\leq\lambda_1 n$ 是平凡上界，作者希望建立从主子矩阵最大特征值到整体二次型优化值的反向下界，并借此统一改进图论中的MaxCut、bisection width与discrepancy结果。

Method: 证明核心引理：若 $M$ 的每个 $n'\times n'$ 主子矩阵最大特征值至少为 $\lambda$，则 $\max_{v\in[-1,1]^n}v^TMv\geq\lambda(n-n'+1)$。将该引理应用于图的邻接矩阵，推导MaxCut、bisection width与discrepancy的改进界。

Result: 证明每个远离团不交并的 $n$ 顶点 $m$ 边图存在大小至少为 $m/2+n^{5/4-o(1)}$ 的割，且在 $o(1)$ 项意义下紧。证明每个 $d$-正则 $n$ 顶点图在 $d\leq(1-\varepsilon)n/2$ 时bisection width至多 $dn/4-\Omega_\varepsilon(d^{1/3}n)$，在 $d=\Omega(n)$ 时最优，从而证实Räty–Sudakov–Tomon的猜想。

Conclusion: 该逆命题型引理是处理MaxCut、bisection width与discrepancy的有力工具，所得界在相应参数范围内达到最优或紧确，并解决了已有猜想。

Abstract: Given an $n\times n$ symmetric matrix $M$ with largest eigenvalue $λ_1\geq 0$, it is easy to show that the solution of the optimisation problem $\max_{v\in [-1,1]^n}v^TMv$ is at most $λ_1 n$. We prove the following converse: if every $n'\times n'$ principal submatrix of $M$ has maximal eigenvalue at least $λ$, then $\max_{v\in [-1,1]^n}v^TMv\geq λ(n-n'+1)$. We use this lemma to improve a number of recent results on the MaxCut, bisection width, and discrepancy of graphs. Among others, we prove that every $n$-vertex $m$-edge graph that is far from a disjoint union of cliques has a cut of size at least $m/2+n^{5/4-o(1)}$, which is sharp up to the $o(1)$-term. Moreover, we prove that every $d$-regular $n$-vertex graph has bisection width at most $dn/4-Ω_{\varepsilon}(d^{1/3}n)$ for $d\leq (1-\varepsilon)n/2$, which is optimal for $d=Ω(n)$. This confirms a conjecture of Räty, Sudakov and Tomon.

</details>


### [113] [A Combinatorial Proof of an Identity of Malik and Sarma on Overpartitions with Repeated Smallest Non-Overlined Part](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37966)
*Suparno Ghoshal,Arijit Jana*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了Malik和Sarma提出的关于重复最小部分的分拆恒等式中尚未被组合证明的那一条恒等式的组合证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andrews与El Bachraoui研究了具有重复最小部分的分拆，Malik和Sarma将其推广到超分拆并利用生成函数与q级数技巧建立了四个恒等式，其中三个已由Baruah、Li和Mohanta给出组合证明，剩余一个恒等式尚缺组合证明。

Method: 采用组合证明方法（combinatorial proof）。

Result: 成功给出了剩余那条恒等式的组合证明。

Conclusion: 补全了Malik-Sarma四个恒等式的组合证明体系，为超分拆中重复最小部分恒等式提供了完整的组合解释。

Abstract: Motivated by the work of Andrews and El Bachraoui on partitions with repeated smallest parts, Malik and Sarma recently extended this concept to overpartitions. Using generating functions and $q$-series techniques, they established several results, including four identities, and posed the problem of finding combinatorial proofs for these identities. Recently, Baruah, Li, and Mohanta provided combinatorial proofs for three of these identities. In this paper, we present a combinatorial proof of the remaining identity.

</details>


### [114] [On-line majority edge-colourings of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37973)
*Paweł Pękała*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究多数边着色的在线变体：Presenter 逐边揭示图，Algorithm 须立即且不可撤销地为每条边着色。证明了贪心策略（至多使用5种颜色）在 δ=O(log n/log log n) 时为在线算法中的最优策略，并将结果推广到 1/k-多数边着色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bock 等人于2023年提出多数边着色概念，并证明最小度至少为2的图存在多数4-边着色。本文旨在研究该概念的在线变体，即在图逐边揭示且着色不可撤销的限制下多数边着色的可行性与最优性。

Method: 采用在线算法框架：Presenter 逐边揭示图，Algorithm 必须立即且不可撤销地着色每条边。分析贪心策略的颜色数上界（至多5种颜色），并在 δ=O(log n/log log n) 条件下证明该策略在在线算法中的最优性；进一步将结果扩展至 1/k-多数边着色。

Result: 证明了贪心策略至多使用5种颜色，且在 δ=O(log n/log log n) 时为在线算法中的最优策略；相关结果可推广至 1/k-多数边着色。

Conclusion: 在线多数边着色中，贪心策略在特定最小度条件下达到最优，且方法可扩展至更一般的 1/k-多数边着色设定。

Abstract: A majority edge-colouring of a graph $G$ is a colouring of the edges of $G$ such that, for every vertex $v$ of $G$, at most half of the edges incident with $v$ receive the same colour. This notion was introduced by Bock et al. in 2023, who proved that every graph of minimum degree at least $2$ has a majority $4$-edge-colouring.
  We investigate an on-line variant of majority edge-colouring in which the graph is revealed by the Presenter edge-by-edge and the Algorithm must colour each edge immediately and irrevocably. In particular. we prove that the greedy strategy, which uses at most five colours, is optimal among on-line algorithms if $δ= O(\frac{\log n}{\log\log n})$. We further extend our results to $1/k$-majority edge-colourings.

</details>


### [115] [There is no $8$-regular $K_3$-irregular graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37978)
*Artem Hak,Sergiy Kozerenko,Andrii Serdiuk*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明不存在8-正则的$K_3$-不规则图，结合已有结果，最终确定$r$-正则$K_3$-不规则图存在当且仅当$r\geq 9$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $K_3$-不规则图要求各顶点所属三角形数目互不相同。此前已知$r\in\{10,11,12\}$存在此类图，且$r\leq 7$不存在、$r=9$存在，而$r=8$成为唯一未解决的临界情形，因此需要判定其是否存在。

Method: 将三角形度数的精细分析与整数线性规划（ILP）技术相结合，针对此前已证明的$8$-正则候选图必须具有17至22个顶点这一结论，逐一排除这些可能的阶数。

Result: 成功排除了$8$-正则$K_3$-不规则图在17至22个顶点范围内的所有可能阶数，从而证明不存在$8$-正则$K_3$-不规则图。

Conclusion: 结合近期构造结果（$r\geq 9$时均存在此类图），完整刻画了正则$K_3$-不规则图的存在性：$r$-正则$K_3$-不规则图存在当且仅当$r\geq 9$。

Abstract: A graph is $K_3$-irregular if its vertices belong to pairwise distinct numbers of triangles. We prove that no $8$-regular $K_3$-irregular graph exists, settling the last unresolved case. Following the initial discovery of such graphs for regularities $r \in \{10,11,12\}$ (Stevanovi'c et al., 2024), our previous work (Hak et al., 2025) showed that no such graphs exist for $r \le 7$, provided the first example for $r=9$, and proved that any $8$-regular candidate must have between $17$ and $22$ vertices. We exclude these possible orders for $r=8$ by combining careful analysis of triangle degrees with integer linear programming techniques. Meanwhile, a recent construction (Zhang, 2026) established that regular $K_3$-irregular graphs do exist for all $r \ge 9$. Together with our results, this establishes that an $r$-regular $K_3$-irregular graph exists if and only if $r\geq 9$.

</details>


### [116] [Quadratic inequalities between the largest eigenvalues of a graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37980)
*Roland Paulin*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明图中最大两个特征值之间的精确二次不等式及第二、第三大特征值之间的二次不等式，并确定相应归一化特征值对的闭包，方法是将问题转化为低维极值几何问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图的特征值之间的定量关系，尤其是最大若干特征值满足的尖锐不等式与可达区域，从而刻画图的谱约束。

Method: 将谱不等式问题转化为 $\mathbb{R}^3$ 与 $\mathbb{R}^2$ 中的极值几何问题；利用拉格朗日乘子法把问题约化为至多五个点的特殊情形，并直接处理这些特殊情形。

Result: 证明 $\lambda_1,\lambda_2$ 与 $\lambda_2,\lambda_3$ 之间的尖锐二次不等式，得到 $\lambda_1+\lambda_2\le \frac{8}{7}n-2$、$\lambda_3\le \frac{n}{3}-1$、$\lambda_2+\lambda_3\le \frac{2}{3}n-2$；并确定 $\big((\lambda_1+1)/n,(\lambda_2+1)/n\big)$ 与 $\big((\lambda_2+1)/n,(\lambda_3+1)/n\big)$ 可达集合的闭包。更一般地，对 $[0,1]^{n\times n}$ 中对称矩阵给出二次界，并对 $[-1,1]^{n\times n}$ 中对称矩阵的两个特征值给出二次界。

Conclusion: 通过几何极值化与拉格朗日乘子约化，给出了图及对称矩阵若干最大特征值之间的尖锐二次界，并完整刻画了相关归一化特征值对的可行区域闭包。

Abstract: We prove a sharp quadratic inequality between the largest two eigenvalues $λ_1 \ge λ_2$ of a graph with $n$ vertices. We also prove a quadratic inequality between the second and third largest eigenvalues $λ_2 \ge λ_3$. These results in particular imply the bounds $λ_1 + λ_2 \le \frac{8}{7} n - 2$, $λ_3 \le \frac{n}{3} - 1$ and $λ_2 + λ_3 \le \frac{2}{3} n - 2$. In fact we determine the closure of the set of possible $(\frac{λ_1+1}{n}, \frac{λ_2+1}{n}) \in \mathbb{R}^2$ and $(\frac{λ_2+1}{n}, \frac{λ_3+1}{n}) \in \mathbb{R}^2$. More generally, we prove quadratic bounds in the case of symmetric matrices in $[0,1]^{n \times n}$, and we also give a quadratic bound for two eigenvalues of a symmetric matrix in $[-1,1]^{n \times n}$. These bounds are proved by transforming the problem into extremal geometric questions in $\mathbb{R}^3$ and $\mathbb{R}^2$. We use the method of Lagrange multipliers to reduce to special cases with at most five points, and we deal with these special cases directly.

</details>


### [117] [Independence number, essential connectivity and the distance spectral radius of graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37981)
*Shuang Ding,Dan Li,Yuanyuan Chen*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文刻画了在给定顶点数、独立数与本质连通度条件下，达到最小距离谱半径的连通图，并完整刻画了相应的极图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图的距离谱半径与图的结构参数（独立数、本质连通度）之间的极值关系，确定在给定约束下距离谱半径最小的图类。

Method: 在图论框架下，以独立数 alpha(G) 和本质连通度 kappa'(G) 为给定参数，通过距离谱半径的极值分析，对 n 个顶点的连通图进行刻画。

Result: 确定了给定独立数和本质连通度的 n 顶点连通图中达到最小距离谱半径的图，并完整刻画了对应的极图。

Conclusion: 建立了独立数、本质连通度与距离谱半径之间的极值对应关系，给出了达到最小距离谱半径的连通图的完整结构刻画。

Abstract: An independent set of a graph G is a subset of VG, no two of which are adjacent. The cardinality of a maximum independent set in a graph G is called the independence number of G, denoted by alpha(G). The essential connectivity kappa'(G) of a graph G is denoted as the minimum number of vertices of G whose removal produces a disconnected graph with at least two non-trivial components. In this paper, we determine the n-vertex connected graphs with given independence number and essential connectivity that attain the minimum distance spectral radius, and fully characterize the corresponding extremal graphs.

</details>


### [118] [On the asymptotics of the Erdős-Rogers function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37987)
*Domagoj Bradač,Oliver Janzer,Rik Sarkar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文渐近地确定了 Erdős-Rogers 函数 $f_{\ell,s}(n)$ 的量级，证明 $f_{\ell,s}(n)=n^{\frac{\ell}{2s}+O_\ell(s^{-2})}$，从而封闭了 Sudakov 下界与 Bradač 上界之间长期存在的指数差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $f_{\ell,s}(n)$ 定义为每个 $n$ 顶点 $K_s$-自由图中必然存在的 $K_\ell$-自由诱导子图的最大阶。当 $s=\ell+1$ 时该函数已被较好理解，但当 $s$ 远大于 $\ell$ 时长期未解决。此前 Sudakov 给出下界 $n^{\frac{\ell}{2s}+O_\ell(s^{-2})}$，Bradač 给出上界 $n^{\frac{\ell-1}{s-1}+o(1)}$，两者指数之间存在明显差距，本文旨在渐近地弥合这一差距。

Method: 证明建立在 Bradač 最近关于非对角 Ramsey 数的紧构造之上（该构造可视为本文结果 $\ell=2$ 的情形），并将其推广以得到一般 $\ell$ 的上界。

Result: 对所有 $2\leq \ell<s$，证明 $f_{\ell,s}(n)\leq n^{\frac{\ell}{2s-\ell}+o(1)}$，结合 Sudakov 的已有下界得到 $f_{\ell,s}(n)=n^{\frac{\ell}{2s}+O_\ell(s^{-2})}$。

Conclusion: 本文渐近地确定了 $s\gg\ell$ 情形下 Erdős-Rogers 函数的阶，封闭了此前上界与下界之间的指数差距，并表明 Bradač 的构造推广后在该问题中给出渐近最优的结果。

Abstract: The Erdős-Rogers function $f_{\ell,s}(n)$ is the largest order of a $K_\ell$-free induced subgraph guaranteed to exist in every $K_s$-free graph on $n$ vertices. While this function is well understood for $s=\ell+1$, the case where $s$ is much larger than $\ell$ has remained wide open. A long-standing lower bound of Sudakov states that $f_{\ell,s}(n)\geq n^{\frac{\ell}{2s}+O_\ell(s^{-2})}$, while a recent result of Bradač shows that $f_{\ell,s}(n)\leq n^{\frac{\ell-1}{s-1}+o(1)}$.
  In this paper, we close this gap asymptotically by proving that $f_{\ell,s}(n)= n^{\frac{\ell}{2s}+O_\ell(s^{-2})}$. More precisely, we prove that for all $2\leq \ell<s$, we have $f_{\ell,s}(n)\leq n^{\frac{\ell}{2s-\ell}+o(1)}$. Our proof builds on Bradač's recent tight construction for off-diagonal Ramsey numbers, which can be viewed as the $\ell=2$ case of our result.

</details>


### [119] [Merino-Welsh inequalities for matroids with controlled lattices of cyclic flats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38047)
*Kolja Knauer,Leonardo Martínez-Sandoval,Criel Merino*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究拟阵的乘性Merino-Welsh不等式商Φ(M)的界，证明在循环平坦格躲避固定有限偏序集的拟阵类上Φ一致有界，并在循环宽度≤7、循环高度≤6以及无loop无coloop的4-paving/4-copaving拟阵上证得Φ(M)≤1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beke等已证明Merino-Welsh商Φ(M)=T_M(1,1)^2/(T_M(2,0)T_M(0,2))可以任意大，因而乘性Merino-Welsh不等式对一般拟阵不成立。本文旨在刻画Φ有界（尤其Φ≤1）的拟阵类，以确定该不等式在哪些受限结构下仍成立。

Method: 以拟阵的循环平坦格（cyclic-flat lattice）为结构工具，考察其诱导子偏序集结构。首先对任意固定有限偏序集P，研究循环平坦格不含P作为诱导子偏序集的拟阵类，证明Φ在该类上一致有界；随后针对具体的宽度、高度限制以及4-paving/4-copaving条件，分别给出Φ≤1的证明。

Result: Φ在循环平坦格躲避任意固定有限偏序集P作为诱导子偏序集的拟阵类上一致有界；对循环宽度至多7、循环高度至多6的拟阵，以及无loop无coloop的4-paving或4-copaving拟阵，均有Φ(M)≤1。

Conclusion: 乘性Merino-Welsh不等式虽对一般拟阵失效，但在循环平坦格受结构限制的拟阵类上Φ一致有界，并在循环宽度≤7、循环高度≤6及无loop无coloop的4-paving/4-copaving拟阵上成立Φ≤1，说明循环平坦格的偏序结构是控制该商的关键。

Abstract: Beke, Csáji, Csikvári, and Pituk showed that the Merino--Welsh quotient $Φ(M)=T_M(1,1)^2/(T_M(2,0)T_M(0,2))$ can be arbitrarily large, so the multiplicative Merino-Welsh inequality fails for matroids in general. We show that $Φ$ is uniformly bounded on the class of matroids whose cyclic-flat lattice avoids any fixed finite poset $P$ as an induced subposet. We further prove $Φ(M)\leq1$ for matroids of cyclic width at most $7$, cyclic height at most $6$, and for loop- and coloop-free $4$-paving or $4$-copaving matroids.

</details>


### [120] [Peaks and peak-nestings on unit interval graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38084)
*Per Alexandersson,Leonardo Saud Maia Leite*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 引入单位区间图的新统计量"峰嵌套"，建立Dyck路径与双色Motzkin路径的双射，细化Touchard恒等式，并研究相关多项式的闭式、递推、gamma展开及零点/对数凹性，最后联系格路径拟阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单位区间图已有通过面积序列（等价于Dyck路径）的经典Catalan编码，作者希望在该编码框架下引入新的图统计量并挖掘其组合、代数与拟阵层面的意义。

Method: 在单位区间图上定义峰嵌套（包含同一顶点的峰团最大数目），通过面积序列对应将其转化为Dyck路径统计量；构造Dyck路径与双色Motzkin路径的双射，同时记录峰嵌套与峰数；研究峰多项式与峰嵌套多项式在全体及连通、Abelian、2-嵌套、约化等子类上的性质。

Result: 得到Touchard恒等式的细化、给定高度Dyck路径的系数公式、若干OEIS序列的新组合解释；给出闭式、递推及对称情形的gamma基非负展开与组合解释；部分多项式族构成广义Sturm序列，另一些非实根但系数似为对数凹。

Conclusion: 峰嵌套提供了单位区间图与 Dyck 路径的新统计视角，统一并细化已有计数结果，并将峰团表示与格路径拟阵相联系。

Abstract: Unit interval graphs admit a classical Catalan encoding by area sequences, or equivalently by Dyck paths. We introduce a new statistic on these graphs, called peak-nesting, defined as the largest number of peak-cliques containing a common vertex. Through the correspondence with area sequences, peak-nesting also defines a new statistic on Dyck paths. We construct a bijection between Dyck paths and bicolored Motzkin paths which simultaneously records peak-nesting and the number of peaks. This yields a refinement of Touchard's identity, coefficient formulas involving Dyck paths of given height, and new combinatorial interpretations for several sequences recorded in the OEIS. We study the peak and peak-nesting polynomials over all unit interval graphs and over the subclasses of connected, Abelian, $2$-nested, and reduced unit interval graphs. For these families we obtain closed formulas, recurrences, and, for the symmetric cases, nonnegative expansions in the gamma-basis with explicit combinatorial interpretations. We also investigate questions regarding the location of zeros and the log-concavity of the corresponding coefficient sequences: some of the polynomial families form generalized Sturm sequences, whereas others fail to be real-rooted but appear nevertheless to have log-concave coefficients. Finally, we relate the peak-clique presentation of a unit interval graph to lattice path matroids.

</details>


### [121] [Asymptotics of the Brown--Erdős--Sós problem at integer exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38115)
*Ting-Wei Chao,Xinqi Huang,Hong Liu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏超图Turán理论中的Brown–Erdős–Sós问题，确定了最大边数$f^{(r)}(n;(r-t)k+t,k)$的极限系数$\pi(r,t,k)$，除$(r,t)=(3,2)$且$k\ge4$为偶数的情形外均给出精确值，并证明该剩余情形中自然填充构造永不渐近最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Brown–Erdős–Sós问题自1971年证明$f^{(r)}(n;(r-t)k+t,k)=\Theta(n^t)$以来，其首项系数的存在性与具体值长期未决，是该领域的核心公开问题。本文旨在确定该极限系数$\pi(r,t,k)$并刻画其性质。

Method: 通过分析$n$顶点$r$-一致超图中不含$k$条不同边张成至多$s$个顶点的边数上界，取$n\to\infty$时$n^{-t}f^{(r)}(n;(r-t)k+t,k)$的极限，分别在$k$为奇数、$k$为偶数且$r\ge4$等情形下推导出精确系数表达式；对剩余情形$(r,t)=(3,2)$且$k$偶，构造并比较填充构造给出下界。

Result: 对除$(r,t)=(3,2)$且$k\ge4$为偶数外的所有$r,t,k$，证明了$\pi(r,t,k)$存在并确定其值：$k$为奇数时为$\frac{2}{t!(2\binom{r}{t}-1)}$，$k$为偶数且$r\ge4$时为$\frac{1}{t!\binom{r}{t}}$。特别地，$r\ge4$时$\pi(r,t,k)$仅依赖$k$的奇偶性而非其具体取值。对剩余情形，证明$\pi(3,2,k)>1/6$对所有偶数$k\ge4$成立，表明自然填充构造永不渐近最优。

Conclusion: 本文基本解决了Brown–Erdős–Sós问题的首项系数确定问题，揭示了系数对$k$仅通过奇偶性依赖的意外现象，并指出$(3,2)$偶数$k$情形下填充构造的非最优性。作为应用，推广了Bennett–Cushman–Dudek的关联，解决了Erdős–Gyárfás–Shelah广义Ramsey问题的若干情形。

Abstract: The Brown--Erdős--Sós problem is a fundamental problem in sparse hypergraph Turán theory. For integers $r,k\ge 2$ and $s\ge r$, the problem asks for the maximum number $f^{(r)}(n;s,k)$ of edges in an $n$-vertex $r$-uniform hypergraph containing no $k$ distinct edges spanning at most $s$ vertices. In 1971, Brown, Erdős, and Sós proved that $f^{(r)}\bigl(n;(r-t)k+t,k\bigr)=Θ(n^t)$ for all $r>t\ge 2$ and $k\ge 2$. However, the existence and the value of the leading coefficient have remained largely open.
  We determine the coefficient $π(r,t,k):=\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-t}f^{(r)}\bigl(n;(r-t)k+t,k\bigr)$ for every such $r,t,k$, except when $(r,t)=(3,2)$ and $k\ge 4$ is even. In particular, \[ π(r,t,k) = \begin{cases} \frac{2}{t!\bigl(2\binom{r}{t}-1\bigr)}, & \text{if $k$ is odd},\\ \frac{1}{t!\binom{r}{t}}, & \text{if $k$ is even and $r\ge4$}. \end{cases} \] Surprisingly, for $r\ge4$, the limit $π(r,t,k)$ depends on $k$ only through its parity, not its value. For the remaining case, we prove that $π(3,2,k)>1/6$ for all even $k\ge 4$, showing that the natural packing construction is never asymptotically optimal. As an application, we extend a connection of Bennett, Cushman, and Dudek to arbitrary uniformity to resolve several cases of the Erdős--Gyárfás--Shelah generalized Ramsey problem.

</details>


### [122] [A nearly linear bound for the Lovász conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38135)
*Bowen Li,Abhishek Methuku*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文将连通顶点传递图中最长圈的长度的下界从 n^{2/3-o(1)} 提升至 n^{1-o(1)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lovász 于 1969 年提出猜想：每个连通顶点传递图是否都含哈密顿路。近期 Bucić、Christoph、Pokrovskiy 和 Steiner 证明了 n 个顶点的此类图必含长度为 n^{2/3-o(1)} 的圈。本文旨在进一步改进这一长度下界，向哈密顿路的猜想逼近。

Method: 首先利用 Tessera 与 Tointon 的结构定理，把顶点集划分为原图中直径较小的部集。当部集较大时，在商图中反复遍历一棵最大度不超过 3 的生成树，并运用 Lovász 局部引理将随机短路径沿该遍历拼接，从而在原图中提取出一条长路。当部集较小时，应用 Babai 的收缩引理，把问题约化为在有限生成、幂零类有界的幂零群所对应的连通 Cayley 图中寻找长路，随后证明 m 个顶点的此类 Cayley 图含有 m^{1-o(1)} 个顶点的路径。

Result: 证明了每个 n 个顶点的连通顶点传递图都含有长度至少为 n^{1-o(1)} 的圈，改进了此前 n^{2/3-o(1)} 的界。

Conclusion: 通过结构定理划分、商图上生成树遍历结合 Lovász 局部引理，以及 Babai 收缩引理归约到幂零群 Cayley 图三个阶段，作者将连通顶点传递图中最长圈的渐近下界提高到 n^{1-o(1)}，显著推进了 Lovász 猜想的相关研究。

Abstract: The celebrated conjecture of Lovász from 1969 asks whether every connected vertex-transitive graph has a Hamiltonian path. Bucić, Christoph, Pokrovskiy and Steiner recently proved that every such graph on $n$ vertices contains a cycle of length $n^{2/3-o(1)}$.
  In this paper, we improve this bound to $n^{1-o(1)}$. Our proof uses a structure theorem of Tessera and Tointon to first obtain a partition of the vertex set into sets of small diameter in the original graph. When the parts are large, we repeatedly traverse a spanning tree of maximum degree at most three in the quotient graph, and use the Lovász local lemma to join random short paths along this traversal and extract a long path in the original graph. When the parts are small, we apply Babai's contraction lemma to reduce the problem to finding a long path in a connected Cayley graph of a nilpotent group with boundedly many generators and bounded nilpotency class, and then show that such a Cayley graph on $m$ vertices contains a path on $m^{1-o(1)}$ vertices.

</details>


### [123] [Maker Breaker Games on a Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38138)
*Sebastian Lüderssen,Fabien Nießen,Silas Rathke*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入带预算机制的Maker-Breaker游戏，精确确定为⌈√(2n-2)-3/2⌉，首次给出三角形博弈的精确阈值偏差，并研究K4博弈的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始Maker-Breaker三角形博弈的阈值偏差长期仅有下界⌈√(2n-2)-3/2⌉与上界(√(8/3)+o(1))√n，精确值悬而未决，动机在于通过新机制给出精确解。

Method: 引入预算版博弈：Breaker每轮可少取边、积累预算供后续轮次使用，额外获得该权力；并进一步研究禁用预算威胁的情形及K4博弈的原始与预算版本。

Result: 预算版三角形博弈阈值偏差精确为⌈√(2n-2)-3/2⌉（对所有n），匹配已知下界；禁用预算威胁时仍为(√2+o(1))√n；对K4博弈给出原始与预算版本首个显式上下界。

Conclusion: 该工作首次确定三角形博弈精确阈值偏差，展示预算机制可弥合上下界差距，并为K4博弈提供新界。

Abstract: The Maker Breaker Triangle Game involves two players, Maker and Breaker, who alternately claim 1 and $q$ edges of $K_n$, respectively. Maker's goal is to claim all three edges of any triangle, whereas Breaker's goal is to prevent this. The threshold bias, i.e. the minimum $q$ such that Breaker wins, is known to lie between $\big\lceil\sqrt{2n-2}-\frac{3}{2}\big\rceil$ and $\big(\sqrt{8/3}+o(1)\big)\sqrt{n}$. Determining its exact value is a longstanding open problem.
  In this paper, we introduce a novel version of the game in which Breaker may claim fewer than $q$ edges per round to build up a budget that he can spend in later rounds. With this additional power for Breaker, we determine the threshold bias to be precisely $\big\lceil\sqrt{2n-2}-\frac{3}{2}\big\rceil$ for all $n$, matching the known lower bound. This is the first version of the Maker Breaker Triangle Game for which the exact threshold bias is known. Even if Breaker is not allowed to use the budget for threats, we prove that the threshold bias is still $\big(\sqrt{2}+o(1)\big)\sqrt{n}$.
  Furthermore, we study the budget version of other Maker Breaker Games. Specifically, for the $K_4$-Game, we prove the first explicit lower and upper bounds on the threshold bias in both the original and the budget version of the game.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Higher-order convex orders under free additive convolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35901)
*Cheng-Xuan Soong*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明自由加性卷积保持$k$-凸序（$k\ge2$），在适当矩条件下成立，并借此肯定回答了Heinävaara关于四阶卷积比较的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典概率中加性卷积对凸序的保持性已有较完善的结果，而自由概率中自由加性卷积对$k$-凸序的保持性尚不清楚。Heinävaara曾就四阶卷积比较提出公开问题，本文旨在解决该问题并将结论推广到一般$k$阶。

Method: 在自由概率框架下研究自由加性卷积对$k$-凸序的保持性。假设所比较的两个测度具有有限的$(k-1)$阶绝对矩，参与卷积的测度具有有限的$\max\{2,k-1\}$阶绝对矩，在此矩条件下证明$k$-凸序在自由加性卷积下不变。

Result: 证明了对任意$k\ge2$，自由加性卷积均保持$k$-凸序，且在所述矩条件下成立。特别地，$k=4$的情形给出了Heinävaara四阶卷积比较问题的肯定回答。

Conclusion: 自由加性卷积在适当矩条件下保持$k$-凸序，将经典凸序理论的相关结果推广到自由概率情形，并解决了四阶卷积比较的公开问题。

Abstract: We prove that free additive convolution preserves the $k$-convex order for every $k\ge2$, assuming finite $(k-1)$st absolute moments for the compared measures and a finite $\max\{2,k-1\}$th absolute moment for the convolving measure. In particular, the case $k=4$ gives an affirmative answer to a question of Heinävaara concerning fourth-order convolution comparison.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [125] [Unique continuation for the periodic Benjamin-Bona-Mahony equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36204)
*Roberto de A. Capistrano Filho,Victor Hugo Gonzalez Martinez,Ailton Campos do Nascimento*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明了实周期Benjamin–Bona–Mahony方程在能量空间中的唯一延拓性：若解在非空时空条带上等于常数，则解处处等于该常数，从而无条件证实Rosier猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Rosier提出的唯一延拓猜想，去掉以往结果中的小性假设与均值条件，并据此使局部反馈与边界反馈的稳定性结论对任意初值无条件成立。

Method: 采用带指数权、变号的Hamiltonian量，结合具强制性的耗散恒等式进行证明；方法适用于任意非常数实多项式通量，并给出线性化方程单点唯一延拓的生成级数证明。

Result: 在能量空间中证明唯一延拓成立；仅需在单点x0及一时间区间上观测解与其通量势ψ即可推出u≡C，且当C=-1时第二条件不可去除；同时构造驻定阶梯型剖面说明定理在H^s（0≤s<1/2）中失效。

Conclusion: 该结果无条件成立Rosier唯一延拓猜想，并使两类局部反馈与边界反馈的镇定结论对任意数据成立；同时界定了H^s中的失效范围，并讨论了相关控制结果。

Abstract: We prove unique continuation for the real periodic Benjamin--Bona--Mahony equation in the energy space: a solution that equals a constant on a nonempty space--time strip is that constant everywhere. In particular, Rosier's unique continuation conjecture [23] holds without any smallness or mean assumption. It even suffices to observe, at one point $x_0$ and for $t$ in an interval, the solution and the potential $ψ=(1-\partial_x^2)^{-1}(u+u^2/2)$ of its flux: $u(x_0,t)=C$ and $ψ(x_0,t)=C+C^2/2$ imply $u\equiv C$, and the second condition cannot be dropped when $C=-1$. The proof uses an exponentially weighted Hamiltonian of indefinite sign with a coercive dissipation identity, and it applies to every nonconstant real polynomial flux. Consequently, Rosier's stabilization results for two localized feedback laws and for a boundary feedback hold unconditionally, for arbitrary data. Stationary step profiles show that the theorem fails in $H^s$ for $0\leq s<1/2$. We also give a generating-series proof of single-point unique continuation for the linearized equation and discuss related control results.

</details>


### [126] [A Fractional Logistic-Type Elliptic Problem with Global Interactions: Existence, Local Uniqueness, and the Fractional-to-Local Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36360)
*Alireza Khatib,Somayeh Mousavinasr,Bashir Zeimarani*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带齐次外部条件的分数阶logistic型椭圆问题，核可非对称、变号，故问题未必有变分结构。在对称竞争情形用全局极小化得到弱解；对任意固定交互参数，给出显式小性条件以应用Schauder不动点定理；附加符号假设得非负解；当 q≥2 时用压缩条件在不变球中得到唯一性与Picard迭代收敛；并证明分数阶趋于1时子列收敛到局部Dirichlet问题解，能量收敛到Dirichlet能量，唯一性给出全族收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典分数阶/logistic型椭圆问题通常依赖变分框架或对称、定号核。本文关注核非对称且变号的更一般情形，此时问题可能无变分表述，需要发展非变分方法；同时刻画分数阶向局部极限（阶数趋于1）的收敛行为。

Method: 在有界域上考虑带线性与非线性积分交互的分数阶logistic型椭圆问题，外部条件齐次。对称竞争情形下利用强制性（coercivity）通过全局极小化构造弱解；一般情形下对任意固定的交互参数给出显式小性条件，用Schauder不动点定理不作对称或符号限制地获得解；在附加符号假设下得到非负解；q≥2时给出压缩条件，在不变球中证唯一性并得到Picard迭代收敛；最后令分数阶趋于1，证明子列收敛到局部Dirichlet问题之解且分数阶能量收敛到Dirichlet能量，并借极限球中局部解的唯一性将子列收敛提升为全族收敛。

Result: 在对称竞争情形下由全局极小化得到弱解；在显式小性条件下，对任意固定交互参数、无须核对称或定号，应用Schauder定理得到解；附加符号假设给出非负解；q≥2时压缩条件在不变球中给出唯一性与Picard迭代收敛；当阶数趋于1时，子列收敛到局部Dirichlet问题的解，分数阶能量收敛到Dirichlet能量，极限球中局部解的唯一性蕴涵整族收敛。

Conclusion: 该工作将分数阶logistic型椭圆问题的可解性推广到非对称、变号核的非变分框架，并给出从非局部到局部Dirichlet问题及能量的收敛性，同时给出q≥2时不变球中的唯一性。

Abstract: We study a fractional logistic-type elliptic problem on a bounded domain with homogeneous exterior conditions and linear and nonlinear integral interactions. The interaction kernels may be nonsymmetric and sign-changing, so the problem need not admit a variational formulation. In the symmetric competitive regime, coercivity yields a weak solution by global minimization. For arbitrary fixed values of the interaction parameter, explicit smallness conditions allow us to apply Schauder's fixed-point theorem without symmetry or sign restrictions on the kernels. Additional sign assumptions give nonnegative solutions. When the nonlinear exponent satisfies $q\geq2$, a contraction condition yields uniqueness in an invariant ball and convergence of the Picard iteration. Finally, as the fractional order approaches one, we prove subsequential convergence to a solution of the local Dirichlet problem, together with convergence of the fractional energies to the Dirichlet energy. Uniqueness of the local solution in the limiting ball gives convergence of the entire family.

</details>


### [127] [Almost sure global well-posedness for the Benjamin-Bona-Mahony equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36403)
*Justin Forlano*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明了B Benjamin-Bona-Mahony（BBM）方程在圆环 $\mathbb{T}$ 上、针对负Sobolev正则性的随机高斯初始数据是几乎必然整体适定的，适用范围为任意 $s>-\frac{1}{4}$，且该结果在Oh-Tzvetkov（2026）的轻微概率不适定性意义下是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前作者本人仅在对数负正则性范围内证明了相应结果，存在"对数正则性障碍"；同时Oh-Tzvetkov（2026）给出的轻微概率不适定性结果表明 $-1/4$ 是天然阈值，因此需要突破对数障碍、达到该阈值附近的最优正则性。

Method: 将 $I$-方法与Bona-Tzvetkov（2007）的低-高（low-high）论证相结合，并由此动机化地对解进行精细化的一阶展开。

Result: 在 $H^{s}(\mathbb{T})$（$s>-\frac{1}{4}$）中，对随机高斯初始数据建立了几乎必然整体适定性；该结果与Oh-Tzvetkov（2026）的轻微概率不适定性相匹配，因而在概率意义下是尖锐的，同时改进了作者先前仅达对数负正则性的结论。

Conclusion: BBM方程在圆环上的随机高斯初值问题可在 $s>-\frac{1}{4}$ 的负正则性范围内实现几乎必然整体适定，达到现有概率不适定性所允许的最优正则性阈值，其关键手段是 $I$-方法与低-高分解的结合及精细化的一阶展开。

Abstract: We prove that the Benjamin-Bona-Mahony equation is almost surely globally well-posed with respect to random Gaussian initial data of negative Sobolev regularity in $H^{s}(\mathbb{T})$ for any $s>-\frac 14$. This result is sharp in view of the mild probabilistic ill-posedness due to Oh-Tzvetkov (2026). This also improves on a previous result of the author which established this only in a logarithmically negative regularity. To break through this logarithmic regularity barrier, we combine the $I$-method with the low-high argument from Bona-Tzvetkov (2007), which motivates a refined first-order expansion for solutions.

</details>


### [128] [Global existence of weak solutions to a nematic electrolyte model in 2D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36483)
*Hengrong Du,Fizay-Noah Lee,Gieri Simonett*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维环面上向列型电解质动力学偏微分方程组，证明其弱解的整体存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向列型电解质耦合了离子输运与液晶取向流动，其数学理论尚不完善，尤其是该耦合系统弱解的整体存在性缺乏结果，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 将Poisson--Nernst--Planck方程（离子浓度与静电势演化）与Ericksen--Leslie方程（向列液晶的流动与取向）耦合建模；采用Ginzburg--Landau近似格式，推导一致能量估计，并利用Pohozaev型恒等式克服Ericksen应力张量导致的紧性缺失，建立指向场的强收敛。

Result: 在二维环面$\mathbb{T}^2$上证明了该耦合系统弱解的整体存在性。

Conclusion: Pohozaev型恒等式是处理Ericksen应力张量紧性缺失的有效工具，该框架可为更一般的液晶电解质模型分析提供参考。

Abstract: In this paper, we study a system of partial differential equations modeling the dynamics of nematic electrolytes on a two-dimensional torus $\mathbb{T}^2$. The model couples the Poisson--Nernst--Planck equations for the evolution of ion concentrations and electrostatic potential with the Ericksen-Leslie equations for the flow and orientation of nematic liquid crystals. We prove the global existence of weak solutions to this system. The proof relies on a Ginzburg-Landau approximation scheme. Key steps include deriving uniform energy estimates and establishing the strong convergence of the director field by utilizing a Pohozaev-type identity to handle the lack of compactness in the Ericksen stress tensor.

</details>


### [129] [Spectral geometry of the magnetic $p$-Laplacian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37313)
*David Krejčiřík,Rossano Sannipoli*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究平面区域几何与非线性p-Laplacian谱（含磁场、Dirichlet边界）的相互作用：给出全平面上谱底的下界，证明有界凸平面区域第一特征值的Pólya型上界并说明其渐近最优性，最后提出若干公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典磁场下Laplacian（Landau能级）的谱估计推广到非线性p-Laplacian情形，并刻画区域几何量（面积、周长、最小宽度）与磁场强度对第一特征值的联合影响。

Method: 对带齐次磁场与Dirichlet条件的非线性p-Laplacian特征值问题进行分析：一方面在全平面上推导谱底的下界，推广Landau能级估计；另一方面对给定的有界凸平面区域，用面积、周长、最小宽度与磁场强度建立第一特征值的Pólya型界，并通过构造“变薄（thinning）”区域序列验证渐近尖锐性。

Result: 获得全平面上谱底的一个下界；获得有界凸平面区域第一特征值关于面积、周长、最小宽度及磁场强度的Pólya型估计，并证明该估计在一类合适的变薄区域序列上渐近最优。

Conclusion: 结果将磁场Landau型谱估计与Pólya型几何界统一到非线性p-Laplacian框架下，并给出若干待解的公开问题。

Abstract: We investigate the interplay between the geometry of a planar domain and the spectrum of the nonlinear $p$-Laplacian, subject to a homogeneous magnetic field and Dirichlet boundary conditions. First, we establish a lower bound for the bottom of the spectrum on the whole plane, extending the classical Landau-level estimate to the nonlinear regime. Second, we prove Pólya-type bounds for the first eigenvalue on bounded convex planar domains in terms of the area, perimeter, minimal width and magnetic field strength, and show that they are asymptotically sharp along sequences of suitable thinning domains. Finally, we post some remarks with open problems.

</details>


### [130] [Shock profiles for the Navier--Stokes--Fourier--Poisson system under the Boltzmann relation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36567)
*Renjun Duan,Rui Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究了Boltzmann关系下的一维可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier-Poisson系统，证明了连接准中性Euler系统（有效压力P_eff=ρ(θ+1)）Rankine-Hugoniot态的粘性激波剖面存在性、唯一性、Lax型及轨道稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立带Poisson耦合与Boltzmann关系的可压缩NSF-Poisson系统中粘性激波剖面的严格数学理论，特别是构造连接准中性Euler系统Rankine-Hugoniot态的激波剖面，并克服传统稳定性分析中需零质量假设的限制。

Method: 方法上采用行波ODE的中心流形约化来构造剖面，结合带位移的a-压缩方法证明轨道稳定性；同时引入由相对熵泛函并附加电能项构成的分析工具，并给出与有效压力P_eff相关的粘性通量的精细估计。

Result: 证明了该粘性激波剖面在平移意义下唯一、属于Lax型，并在时间上轨道稳定，且无需对扰动施加零质量假设。

Conclusion: 该工作为耗散性等离子体流体模型中的激波稳定性提供了无零质量假设的严格证明，拓展了a-压缩与相对熵方法在带电场耦合系统中的应用。

Abstract: The one-dimensional compressible Navier--Stokes--Fourier--Poisson system under the Boltzmann relation is studied for the existence of small-amplitude viscous shock profiles connecting the Rankine--Hugoniot states of the quasineutral Euler system with effective pressure $P_{\mathrm{eff}}=ρ(θ+1)$. This profile is unique up to translation, of Lax type, and orbitally stable in time without a zero-mass assumption on the perturbation. The proof is based on a center-manifold reduction of the traveling-wave ODE and on the method of $a$-contraction with shifts, together with a relative-entropy functional augmented by electric energy and a sharp estimate of the viscous flux associated with $P_{\mathrm{eff}}$.

</details>


### [131] [Interface-Shifted Minnaert Resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37423)
*Huaian Diao,Long Li,Mourad Sini*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文建立了小气泡在材料界面附近产生的Minnaert共振的统一渐近理论，按气泡与界面间距与气泡尺度的比值分为远分离、临界和近接触三个区域，揭示了共振频率从经典孤立气泡极限到接触极限的连续变化规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Minnaert共振描述的是均匀背景中孤立小气泡的共振行为，但当气泡位于材料不连续界面附近时，界面会显著改变共振特性。现有理论缺乏对气泡逐步靠近界面这一过程中共振频率如何从孤立气泡极限过渡到接触极限的统一刻画，尤其缺乏对分离尺度比值θ_ε取不同极限情形的系统性分析。

Method: 作者引入缩放分离参数θ_ε = dist(D_ε, Γ)/ε，将问题划分为θ_ε→∞、θ_ε→θ_*∈(0,∞)、θ_ε→0三个区域。核心手段是：将共振问题统一归约为一个标量方程；给出依赖于界面的电容的变分刻画；并利用相关透射能量的Mosco收敛性，得到电容势与最小能量的收敛，直至奇异的接触构型。

Result: 1) 远分离区：界面在首阶不可见，恢复经典全空间Minnaert定律。2) 临界区：气泡与界面在有限尺度距离下共存，牛顿电容被由极限切向透射问题确定的平界面电容取代。3) 近接触区：该族收敛到接触极限电容，对所有满足dist(D_ε, Γ) = o(ε)的间隙缩放给出相同的首阶Minnaert共振。

Conclusion: 三个区域统一描述了气泡接近界面时共振频率的演化，从经典孤立气泡极限直至接触极限。该理论框架依托于共振问题的标量归约与界面相关电容的变分刻画，并通过Mosco收敛将结论推广到奇异接触构型。

Abstract: We develop a unified asymptotic theory for Minnaert resonances generated by a small bubble $D_\varepsilon$ placed near an interface $Γ$ of material discontinuity in an acoustic background. The bubble has characteristic size $\varepsilon>0$, with its density and bulk modulus depending on the same small parameter through the Minnaert high-contrast scaling. The analysis is organized by the scaled separation \[ Θ_\varepsilon := \frac{\operatorname{dist}(D_\varepsilon,Γ)}{\varepsilon}, \] and covers the three natural regimes \[ Θ_\varepsilon\to+\infty, \qquad Θ_\varepsilon\toΘ_*\in(0,\infty), \qquad Θ_\varepsilon\to0: \]
  1) In the far-separation regime, the interface is invisible at leading order and the classical full-space Minnaert law is recovered.
  2) In the critical regime, the rescaled geometry retains both the bubble and the interface at finite separation, and the Newtonian capacitance is replaced by a flat-interface capacitance determined by the limiting tangent transmission problem.
  3) In the near-contact regime, this family converges to a contact-limiting capacitance, yielding the same leading-order Minnaert resonance for all gap scalings satisfying $\operatorname{dist}(D_\varepsilon,Γ) = o(\varepsilon)$.
  These three regimes provide a unified description of how the resonance frequency changes as the bubble approaches the interface, from the classical isolated-bubble limit to the contact limit. The analysis relies on a uniform reduction of the resonance problem to a scalar equation and on a variational characterization of the interface-dependent capacitances; in particular, Mosco convergence of the associated transmission energies yields convergence of the capacitary potentials and minimum energies up to the singular contact configuration.

</details>


### [132] [Existence of infinitely many singular self-similar solutions for the semilinear heat equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36821)
*Yūki Naito*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了半线性热方程在特定指数范围内存在无穷多个奇异正向前自相似解，并研究了其 profile 的渐近行为与界，最终利用奇异自相似解证明了临界指数下 Cauchy 问题正解的非唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半线性热方程的解结构与唯一性是重要问题，尤其涉及奇异解的存在性及临界指数情形下 Cauchy 问题正解的唯一性。

Method: 构造无穷多个奇异正向前自相似解，分析解的 profile 在 |x|→0 与 |x|→∞ 时的渐近行为及其界。

Result: 在 N≥3 且 N/(N-2)≤p≤(N+2)/(N-2) 范围内得到无穷多个奇异正向前自相似解，并刻画了 profile 的渐近行为与界。

Conclusion: 当 p=N/(N-2) 时，借助奇异自相似解证明了该方程 Cauchy 问题正解的非唯一性。

Abstract: We show the existence of infinitely many singular positive forward self-similar solutions for the semilinear heat equation $\partial_t u = Δu + u^p$ for $x \in \R^N$, $t > 0$, where $N \geq 3$ and $N/(N-2) \leq p \leq (N+2)/(N-2)$. We also investigate the asymptotic behavior and the bound of the profiles of the solutions as $|x| \to 0$ and $|x| \to \infty$, respectively. Furthermore, we show nonuniqueness of positive soutions of the Cauchy problem for the equation with $p = N/(N-2)$ by using singular self-similar solutions.

</details>


### [133] [On the minimality of the radial singularity in nematic liquid crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36972)
*Pierre Bousquet,Xavier Lamy*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明了在参数区域 $k_1>k_2$（超出已知 $k_1\leq k_2$ 范围）时，$x\mapsto x/|x|$ 仍在固定边界下极小化各向异性能量，核心工具是 $u_*$ 作为球面值调和映射的新稳定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究球面值映射在一般各向异性能量泛函下的极小性。已知结论仅在参数 $k_1\leq k_2$ 时成立，本文旨在突破该限制，处理更具挑战性的 $k_1>k_2$ 参数区域。

Method: 关键在于为径向投影映射 $u_*(x)=x/|x|$ 建立一个新的稳定性估计（作为极小化球面值调和映射），并借此处理各向异性泛函中散度项、螺旋项与叉积项的竞争。

Result: 证明了对于任意 $k_1,k_2,k_3>0$ 且 $k_1>k_2$（即超出已知 $k_1\leq k_2$ 范围），$u_*$ 在所有与它在边界上一致的 $\mathbb S^2$-值映射中极小化该能量。

Conclusion: 将 $u_*$ 的极小性从经典参数范围 $k_1\leq k_2$ 推广至更广的 $k_1>k_2$ 区域，表明其极小性并非仅由能量结构中的已知退化情形决定，稳定性估计为这类各向异性变分问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We show that the map $u_*\colon B_1\subset\mathbb R^3\to\mathbb S^2$, $x\mapsto x/|x|$, minimizes the anisotropic energy \begin{align*} \int_{B_1} \Big( k_1(\mathrm{div}\, u)^2+k_2 (u\cdot \mathrm{curl}\, u)^2 +k_3 |u\times\mathrm{curl}\, u|^2 \Big) \, dx, \end{align*} among $\mathbb S^2$-valued maps agreeing with it on the boundary, for values of $k_1,k_2,k_3>0$ beyond the known regime $k_1\leq k_2$. Our main tool is a new stability estimate for $u_*$ as a minimizing sphere-valued harmonic map.

</details>


### [134] [Optimal Hardy Inequalities for Fractional $p$-Laplacians on the Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37336)
*Florian Fischer,Marius Nietschmann*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在整数格 Z 上为分数阶 p-拉普拉斯算子 Δ_p^σ（1<p<∞，0<σ<1/p）建立了最优 Hardy 不等式，最优权函数在无穷远处渐近等价于 |x|^{-pσ}，并对 Riesz 分数阶 p-拉普拉斯给出类似结果；附录证明了局部可和图上一般 p-拉普拉斯算子中 p-零临界性蕴含无穷远处 p-最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hardy 型不等式刻画了拉普拉斯算子关于权函数的"临界"行为，最优 Hardy 不等式是最佳常数版本，能给出算子的最优改进。经典（局部、整数阶）情形已有成熟理论，但分数阶 p-拉普拉斯算子（尤其非局部、无界图情形）的最优 Hardy 权与渐近行为尚不清楚，且"零临界性"与"无穷远处最优性"之间的等价关系在图上的推广有待确立。

Method: 在 Z 上研究分数阶 p-拉普拉斯 Δ_p^σ，参数范围 p∈(1,∞)、σ∈(0,1/p)；通过构造显式（超）调和型测试函数与变分/临界性分析推导带最优常数的最优 Hardy 不等式，并分析对应最优权的渐近阶为 |x|^{-pσ}；对 Riesz 分数阶 p-拉普拉斯采用平行的论证。附录中在局部可和图上对一般 p-拉普拉斯算子，借助 p-零临界性与 p-最优性（无穷远处）的定义与函数空间论证建立蕴含关系。

Result: 得到 Z 上 Δ_p^σ 的最优 Hardy 不等式，最优权渐近行为为 |x|^{-pσ}（|x|→∞）；对 Riesz 分数阶 p-拉普拉斯获得类似结果；附录证明在局部可和图上一般 p-拉普拉斯算子满足 p-零临界性蕴含无穷远处 p-最优性。

Conclusion: 分数阶 p-拉普拉斯算子在离散格上存在最优 Hardy 不等式且最优权具有幂律渐近 |x|^{-pσ}，该结论亦适用于 Riesz 分数阶情形；同时将"零临界性⇒无穷远处最优性"的图论框架推广到一般 p-拉普拉斯算子。

Abstract: We obtain optimal Hardy inequalities for fractional powers of the $p$-Laplacian $Δ_p^σ$ on $\mathbb{Z}$, $p\in (1,\infty)$, $σ\in (0, 1/p)$. Asymptotically, the optimal weights behave like $|x|^{-pσ}$ as $|x|\to \infty$. We show a similar result for the Riesz fractional $p$-Laplacian.
  In an appendix, we show that for general $p$-Laplace operators on locally summable graphs, $p$-null-criticality implies $p$-optimality at infinity.

</details>


### [135] [Nonlinear stability of 3-D plane Couette flow for Boussinesq system in a finite channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36989)
*Jinkai Li,Zhilin Lin,Dong Wang*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文证明在有限通道、y方向无滑移边界条件下，三维Boussinesq系统平面库埃特流与常温度态具有非线性稳定性：当初值速度与温度分别以 O(ν) 和 O(ν²) 逼近平衡态时，解不会离开平衡态，表明强边界层效应不引起强不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面库埃特流在三维Boussinesq系统中的非线性稳定性此前尚无无滑移边界条件下的结果；而边界层效应是否会导致强不稳定性是核心关注点，本文旨在填补这一空白并与已有结果结合给出稳定性判断。

Method: 研究域为 T×[-1,1]×T，y方向施加无滑移边界条件，平衡态为 vs=(y,0,0) 与常温度 ρs；对初值施加 H² 范数扰动小性假设（速度 O(ν)、温度 O(ν²)），证明三维Boussinesq系统解的稳定性。

Result: 在速度扰动 ‖vin-(y,0,0)‖_{H²} ≤ c0ν、温度扰动 ‖ρin-ρs‖_{H²} ≤ c0ν²（c0 与黏性及热扩散 ν 无关）条件下，解不发生转捩，保持接近库埃特流与常温度态；这是无滑移边界条件下三维Boussinesq系统平面库埃特流非线性稳定性的首个结果。

Conclusion: 该结果与文献[18]共同表明，强边界层效应并不会导致强不稳定性，即无滑移条件下库埃特流对三维Boussinesq系统仍具有非线性稳定性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the hydrodynamics stability of the 3-D plane Couette flow $v_s=(y,0,0)$ with constant temperature state $ρ_s$ for the Boussinesq system in a finite channel $\mathbb{T}\times [-1,1]\times \mathbb{T}$ with non-slip boundary condition in $y$ direction. We prove that if the initial velocity field $v_{in}$ and initial temperature $ρ_{in}$ satisfy \begin{align*} \|v_{in}-(y,0,0)\|_{H^2} \leq c_0 ν, \ \ \|ρ_{in}-ρ_s\|_{H^2} \leq c_0 ν^2, \end{align*} for some $c_0>0$ independent of the viscosity and thermal diffusion $ν$, then the solutions to 3D Boussinesq system do not transit from the Couette flow and constant temperature state. To our best knowledge, this is the first result about the nonlinear stability of plane Couette flow for 3-D Boussinesq system in the domain with non-slip boundary condition. This result, together with [18], implies that the strong boundary layer effect does not lead to strong instability.

</details>


### [136] [Renormalized Invariant Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37005)
*Christian Kuehn,Jan-Eric Sulzbach*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出"谱重整化"方法，通过在高频处人为创造谱间隙而保持低模态不变，从而在半线性抛物型方程中构造有限维不变流形，并给出对原半流的近似半共轭与全局吸引子的Hausdorff误差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE的不变流形理论因通常缺乏谱间隙而在技术上极具挑战，许多方程不存在精确的惯性流形。作者受重整化方法近期进展的启发，希望在无自然谱间隙的情形下仍能获得有限维动力近似。

Method: 对半线性抛物型方程引入谱重整化，在高频段制造人工谱间隙而低模态不变；对重整化后的方程族应用经典惯性流形理论，得到维数随重整化参数趋于零而发散的有限维不变流形，并通过近似半共轭刻画其约化动力学；进一步在全局增量耗散假设下证明半共轭关于时间一致，并建立双参数快慢PDE框架。

Result: 约化动力学在有限时间区间上以O(ε²)误差逼近原半流；在全局增量耗散下，半共轭关于时间一致，并对（若存在的）全局吸引子给出O(ε²)的Hausdorff距离估计；同时证明了双曲平衡点的持久性及其Morse指数的保持；在有无自然谱间隙两种情形以及任意维有界区域上的快慢系统中均验证了该构造。

Conclusion: 谱重整化在精确惯性流形不可用时仍提供有限维动力近似，在完全不变流形与直接估计之间实现了实用平衡。

Abstract: Invariant manifold theory for partial differential equations (PDEs) is technically challenging as we often lack spectral gaps. Motivated by recent progress using renormalization in various areas of mathematics, we introduce a spectral renormalization method for semilinear parabolic equations that creates an artificial spectral gap at high frequencies while leaving the low modes unchanged. For the resulting family of renormalized equations, classical inertial-manifold theory yields finite-dimensional invariant manifolds whose dimension diverges as the renormalization parameter tends to zero. We show that the reduced dynamics on these manifolds approximate the original semiflow through an approximate semi-conjugacy, with an $O(ε^2)$ error on bounded finite-time intervals. Under global incremental dissipativity, the semi-conjugacy is uniform in time and yields an $O(ε^2)$ Hausdorff estimate for the global attractors, if they exist. We also establish further dynamical properties of the spectral renormalization such as persistence of hyperbolic equilibria and their Morse indices. We illustrate the construction in settings with and without a natural spectral gap and embed it into a two-parameter fast-slow PDE framework, where the artificial gap enables us to construct slow manifolds for systems on bounded domains with arbitrary dimension. Thus our new approach provides a finite-dimensional dynamical approximation even in regimes where an exact inertial manifold for the original equation is not available. In particular, this provides a practical balance between fully invariant manifolds and direct estimates.

</details>


### [137] [Morse index and spectral asymptotics of least-energy solutions to the Choquard equation in planar domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37026)
*Jiaoping Chen,Wenjing Chen,Shengbing Deng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究平面Choquard方程在$p\to+\infty$时最小能量解的Morse指数与谱渐近，精确给出线性化问题前四个特征对的渐近展开，并联系解谱指标与Robin函数临界点指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画非局部Choquard方程最小能量解在强非线性极限下的稳定性与谱结构，并揭示其与Robin函数临界点性质的对应关系。

Method: 结合变分估计、爆破分析（blow-up analysis）以及非局部Pohožaev恒等式。

Result: 在几何假设下获得线性化问题前四特征对的尖锐渐近：第一特征值恰为$(2p+1)^{-1}$；第二、三特征函数对应极限bubble的平移模式，其特征值展开由集中点处Robin函数的Hessian决定；第四特征函数对应伸缩模式，特征值为$1+3(4-\alpha)/(2p)+o(p^{-1})$。由此得到解谱指标与Robin函数临界点指标的比较。

Conclusion: 非局部Choquard方程最小能量解的谱指标可由Robin函数的临界点指标刻画，平移模式与伸缩模式的渐近分别由Hessian与显式主项控制。

Abstract: We investigate the Morse index and spectral asymptotics of least-energy solutions to the planar Choquard equation \begin{equation*} \begin{cases}
  -Δu = \displaystyle\left(\int_Ω \frac{u^{p+1}(y)}{|x - y|^α}dy\right) u^p & \text{in } Ω, \\ u > 0 & \text{in } Ω, \\ u = 0 & \text{on } \partial Ω, \end{cases} \end{equation*} where $Ω\subset\mathbb R^2$ is a smooth bounded domain and $0<α<1$. Under some geometric assumptions, we obtain sharp asymptotics for the first four eigenpairs of the linearized problem as $p\to+\infty$. The first eigenvalue is exactly $(2p+1)^{-1}$. The second and third eigenfunctions give the translation modes of the limiting bubble, and their eigenvalue expansions are determined by the Hessian of the Robin function at the concentration point. The fourth eigenfunction gives the dilation mode with eigenvalue $1+3(4-α)/(2p)+o(p^{-1})$. As a consequence, we derive a comparison between the spectral indices of the solution and the critical-point indices of the Robin function.The proofs combine variational estimates, blow-up analysis and nonlocal Pohožaev identities.

</details>


### [138] [Sequences of generalized moments and their applications to some moment partial differential equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37138)
*Sławomir Michalik,Hiroshi Yamazawa*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将Balser矩求和理论中的保可和性序列与矩序列推广为广义矩序列，构造了相应的Laplace与Borel积分算子扩展，给出广义矩的积分表示、矩-PDE可和解的刻画及奇异方向与跳跃表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Balser矩求和理论中的保可和性序列与矩序列，以扩展Laplace与Borel积分算子理论，并研究广义矩下矩-PDE可和解的奇异性。

Method: 引入广义矩序列；构造其Laplace与Borel积分算子的扩展；推导广义矩的矩导数积分表示；刻画广义矩下矩-PDE的可和解；描述奇异方向，并用超函数及与保可和性序列相关的核函数表达跳跃。

Result: 建立了广义矩序列框架下的Laplace与Borel积分算子扩展；获得矩导数的积分表示；实现了广义矩下矩-PDE可和解的刻画与奇异方向描述，并给出跳跃的超函数与核函数表达式。

Conclusion: 广义矩序列统一并推广了保可和性序列与矩序列的求和理论，为矩-PDE可和解的奇异性分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: We introduce sequences of generalized moments that are a generalization of sequences preserving summability and sequences of moments from Balser's theory of moment summability. We construct extensions of Laplace and Borel integral operators for these new sequences. We find integral representations of moment derivatives for generalized moments. We show the characterization of summable solutions of moment-PDEs with generalized moments. We describe singular directions for summable solutions of moment-PDEs with generalized moments and we express jumps across these singular directions in terms of hyperfunctions and in terms of kernel functions associated with sequences preserving summability, which are connected with given sequences of generalized moments.

</details>


### [139] [Dynamical stability for the periodic modified Mullins-Sekerka flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37201)
*Daniele De Gennaro,Anna Kubin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意维数下修正Mullins-Sekerka流（平环面上sharp-interface Ohta-Kawasaki能量的梯度流）的动力学稳定性：初值与严格稳定临界集体积相同且C^{3,α}接近时，流全局存在并以所有C^k范数指数收敛到该临界集的平移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究修正Mullins-Sekerka流的长期渐近行为，即其相对于严格稳定临界集的动力学稳定性，并将结果推广到任意维数。

Method: 利用针对该能量严格稳定临界集的定量Alexandrov型估计，证明流的全局存在性与指数收敛性。

Result: 若初始集与严格稳定临界集体积相同且在C^{3,α}范数下充分接近，则流对所有时间存在，并在每个C^k范数下指数快速收敛到该临界集的一个平移。

Conclusion: 修正Mullins-Sekerka流在任意维数下对严格稳定临界集具有动力学稳定性，收敛速率指数级且在所有C^k范数意义下成立。

Abstract: We prove dynamical stability in arbitrary dimension for the modified Mullins--Sekerka flow, the gradient flow of the sharp-interface Ohta--Kawasaki energy on the flat torus. Specifically, we show that if an initial set has the same volume as a strictly stable critical set for the energy and is sufficiently close to it in $C^{3,α}$, then the flow exists for all times and converges exponentially fast, in every $C^k$ norm, to a translate of that critical set. The proof relies on a quantitative Alexandrov-type estimate for strictly stable critical sets of the energy.

</details>


### [140] [Hölder estimates up to the singular time for the Córdoba-Córdoba-Fontelos equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37232)
*Ángel Castro,Alberto Enciso,Antonio J. Fernández*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明Córdoba-Córdoba-Fontelos方程在解析初值θ₀(x)=(1+x²)⁻¹下发生有限时间奇点，同时解在C^{2/5}中一致有界，并证明θ与u的C^{2/3}半范数在奇点附近发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究CCF方程解的正则性与奇点形成机制，明确解在何种Hölder空间中保持有界、何种半范数发散。

Method: 利用被非线性演化保持的概率测度积分表示；通过正核比较导出u方程关键非线性项的逐点二次估计，并结合测度矩的演化恒等式。

Result: 解在有限时间产生奇点；解在C^{2/5}中一致有界；当时间趋近奇点时，θ与u的C^{2/3}半范数均发散。

Conclusion: 概率测度表示与矩演化恒等式共同给出Hölder一致界与更高半范数发散，刻画了CCF方程奇点形成的正则性结构。

Abstract: We prove that the solution to the Córdoba-Córdoba-Fontelos equation $θ_t-uθ_x=0$, $u=Hθ$, on the real line with analytic initial datum $θ_0(x)=(1+x^2)^{-1}$ develops a finite-time singularity while remaining uniformly bounded in $C^{2/5}$. We also prove that, as the solution approaches the singular time, the $C^{2/3}$ seminorms of both $θ$ and $u$ diverge. The main ingredient is an integral representation in terms of a probability measure that is preserved by the nonlinear evolution. From this representation we derive a pointwise quadratic estimate for the key nonlinear term in the equation for $u$, using a comparison of positive kernels. Together with evolution identities for the moments of the measure, this estimate yields both the uniform Hölder bound and the divergence of the higher seminorms.

</details>


### [141] [Uniform $W^{2,m}$ approximation of almost isometric maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37249)
*Sergio Conti,Georg Dolzmann,Stefan Müller*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 证明从定向流形的Lipschitz子集到另一同维定向流形的近似等距Sobolev映射，可由$C^{1,\alpha}$映射逼近，并在$W^{2,m}$范数（任意$m\in(1,\infty)$）上具有一致界，进而得到$C^{1,\alpha}$半范数的一致界；逼近与原映射的$W^{1,p}$距离由偏离等距的$L^p$量以最优尺度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究近似等距映射能否被光滑（$C^{1,\alpha}$）映射逼近，并在高阶Sobolev范数上获得与映射无关的一致估计，从而把"偏离等距的程度"与逼近误差定量联系起来。

Method: 对定义在定向流形$M$的Lipschitz子集上、取值于同维定向流形$N$的近似等距Sobolev映射构造$C^{1,\alpha}$逼近，并对$W^{2,m}$范数（$m\in(1,\infty)$）建立一致界，由此导出$C^{1,\alpha}$半范数的一致界；逼近误差用$L^p$偏离等距的量以最优尺度估计。假设$M,N$紧致、定向且维数相同，$M$可带边界。

Result: 近似等距Sobolev映射可被$C^{1,\alpha}$映射逼近；存在不依赖于具体映射的$W^{2,m}$范数一致界（任意$m\in(1,\infty)$），并由此得到$C^{1,\alpha}$半范数的一致界；$W^{1,p}$逼近距离由$L^p$偏离等距量以最优尺度控制。

Conclusion: 在紧致、定向、等维（$M$可带边界）的流形间，近似等距Sobolev映射具有$C^{1,\alpha}$正则性逼近，且高阶Sobolev范数与$C^{1,\alpha}$半范数均有一致界，逼近误差与偏离等距的$L^p$量之间的依赖关系达到最优尺度。

Abstract: This paper shows that an almost-isometric Sobolev map from a Lipschitz subset of an oriented manifold $M$ into another oriented manifold $N$ can be approximated by a map of class $C^{1,α}$, with a universal bound on the $W^{2,m}$ norm, for any $m\in(1,\infty)$. This, in turn, implies a universal bound on the $C^{1,α}$ seminorm. The $W^{1,p}$ distance of the approximation from the original map is controlled in terms of the $L^p$-deviation from being an isometry, with the optimal scaling. The manifolds $M$ and $N$ are assumed to be compact, oriented and have equal dimension; $M$ may have a boundary.

</details>


### [142] [Existence of three solutions for parametric $p$-Laplacian equations in $\mathbb{R}^N$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37274)
*E. Amoroso,G. D'Aguì*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究全空间 $\mathbb{R}^N$ 上含 $p$-Laplacian 算子的非线性椭圆问题。在 $p>N$ 及适当条件下，借助两个三临界点定理证明至少存在三个不同的弱解，并给出参数 $\lambda>0$ 的显式范围；在附加符号条件下解非负，还讨论非平凡性并给出可分离变量非线性问题的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将近期针对 $p$-Laplacian 方程的多重性定理从有界区域推广到全空间 $\mathbb{R}^N$，并给出保证多解存在的参数 $\lambda$ 的显式取值范围。

Method: 变分方法，基于两个不同的三临界点定理（three critical points theorems）；对势项与非线性项施加适当假设。

Result: 在 $p>N$ 时，对适当假设下的势项与非线性项，建立至少三个不同弱解的存在性；给出参数 $\lambda>0$ 的显式范围；在附加符号条件下解为非负，并讨论解的非平凡性；最后给出非线性项具可分离变量形式的应用。

Conclusion: 结果把若干近期 $p$-Laplacian 方程的多重性定理推广至全空间情形，并提供多解存在性的参数范围与解的符号、非平凡性信息。

Abstract: In this paper we deal with a nonlinear elliptic problem in the whole space $\RN$ involving the $p$-Laplacian operator with $p>N$. Under suitable assumptions on the potential term and on the nonlinearity, we establish the existence of at least three distinct weak solutions. Our approach is variational and relies on two different three critical points theorems. The results extend to the whole space some recent multiplicity theorems for $p$-Laplacian equations, and provide explicit ranges for the parameter $λ> 0$ ensuring the existence of multiple solutions, which are nonnegative under additional sign conditions. The nontriviality of the solutions is also discussed. Applications to problems with nonlinearities with separated variables are finally presented.

</details>


### [143] [Second order estimates on transition layers for the Allen-Cahn equation with homogeneous Neumann boundary conditions: Part I - Boundary-orthogonal transition layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32829)
*Akashdeep Dey,Wenkui Du,Davide Parise,Lorenzo Sarnataro*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究带边Riemann流形上具有齐次Neumann边界条件的Allen-Cahn方程的稳定解，在研究过渡层与边界正交相交的情形下，建立边界直至边界的二阶Hölder估计与节点超曲面的定量平均曲率衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Wang-Wei）主要处理内部或光滑情形，缺乏对边界与过渡层正交相交时节点集的精细正则性刻画；而边界几何会引入内部问题中不存在的额外修正项，其效应需要单独分析。

Method: 在过渡区域对增强第二基本形式施加先验界，推广Wang-Wei的technique，并额外引入一个由环境边界几何决定的修正项；该修正项在节点集上首阶贡献相互抵消，但在非零水平集上残留。

Result: 在环境维数不超过10时，得到过渡区域直至边界的一致二阶Hölder估计与节点超曲面的定量平均曲率衰减；同时发现非零水平集的平均曲率未必一致衰减，此现象在内部问题中不存在，并由Kowalczyk构造的稳定解所体现。

Conclusion: 边界几何导致过渡区水平集平均曲率不衰减的新现象，揭示带边Allen-Cahn方程稳定解与纯内部问题的本质差异，为边界层正则性理论提供定量工具。

Abstract: We study stable solutions of the Allen-Cahn equation with homogeneous Neumann boundary condition on Riemannian manifolds with boundary in the regime where the transition layers meet the boundary orthogonally. Under an a priori bound for the enhanced second fundamental form in the transition region, we establish uniform second-order Hölder estimates and quantitative mean curvature decay for the nodal hypersurfaces up to the boundary in ambient dimensions at most 10. The proof adapts the technique pioneered by Wang-Wei, adding a further correction determined by the geometry of the ambient boundary. Its leading contribution to mean curvature cancels on the nodal set but survives on non-zero level sets in the transition region, whose mean curvature need not decay uniformly. This phenomenon has no interior analogue and is exhibited by stable solutions constructed by Kowalczyk.

</details>


### [144] [Boundary Value Problems for the $SU(\infty)$-Toda Equation via the Onofri Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37282)
*Sun-Yung Alice Chang,Hongyi Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文在 $[0,\frac12)\times\mathbb{S}^2$ 上建立并求解 $SU(\infty)$-Toda 方程的一类退化椭圆边值问题，从而构造出 4-球及 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上复线丛中具有圆对称性的反自对偶 Poincaré–Einstein 度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反自对偶 Poincaré–Einstein 度量是共形几何与四维引力中的核心对象，具有圆对称性的情形可归约为处理 $SU(\infty)$-Toda 方程，但相应边值问题通常退化，需要专门的分析工具加以处理。

Method: 在 $[0,\frac12)\times\mathbb{S}^2$ 上提出 $SU(\infty)$-Toda 方程的退化椭圆边值问题，通过求解该方程获得所需度量的显式或存在性构造。

Result: 成功构建了 4-球上以及 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上复线丛中具有圆对称性的反自对偶 Poincaré–Einstein 度量。

Conclusion: 该退化边值问题的可解性为构造相应几何提供了有效途径，所得度量给出相关几何结构的新例子。
